775: install_steam_runtime_arm64() {
776:   log_info "Downloading and installing Steam runtime (ARM64)..."
777:   # Keep the existing runtime until the complete staged archive and both library
778:   # links validate. The journal also recovers a process killed between renames.
779:   # This transaction does not cover the separate FEX/client/Proton operations.
780:   if /usr/bin/python3 -I -S -B - "${STEAM}" "${RUNTIME_TAR_VERSION_URL}" "${RUNTIME_TAR_BASE}" <<'PY_STEAM_RUNTIME'
781: import ctypes
782: import fcntl
783: import hashlib
784: import json
785: import lzma
786: import os
787: from pathlib import Path, PurePosixPath
788: import re
789: import resource
790: import shutil
791: import signal
792: import stat
793: import subprocess
794: import sys
795: import tarfile
796: 
797: RUNTIME = "steam-runtime-steamrt-arm64"
798: LINKS = ("libibus-1.0.so.5", "libva.so.2")
799: RESERVE = 256 * 1024 * 1024
800: MAX_ARCHIVE = 2 * 1024 * 1024 * 1024
801: MAX_EXPANDED = 16 * 1024 * 1024 * 1024
802: MAX_MEMBERS = 250000
803: MAX_LINK_BACKUP = 64 * 1024 * 1024
804: 
805: 
806: def identity(path):
807:     try:
808:         info = path.lstat()
809:     except FileNotFoundError:
810:         return None
811:     if not stat.S_ISDIR(info.st_mode):
812:         raise RuntimeError("Runtime transaction directory is not a real directory: " + str(path))
813:     return [info.st_dev, info.st_ino]
814: 
815: 
816: def describe(path):
817:     if path.is_symlink():
818:         return {"kind": "link", "target": os.readlink(path)}
819:     if not path.exists():
820:         return {"kind": "absent"}
821:     if not path.is_file():
822:         raise RuntimeError("Refusing to replace a non-file library entry: " + str(path))
823:     info = path.stat()
824:     if info.st_size > MAX_LINK_BACKUP:
825:         raise RuntimeError("Existing library entry exceeds the bounded backup size.")
826:     return {"kind": "file", "sha256": digest(path), "mode": stat.S_IMODE(info.st_mode),
827:             "mtime_ns": info.st_mtime_ns}
828: 
829: 
830: def digest(path):
831:     with path.open("rb") as stream:
832:         return hashlib.file_digest(stream, "sha256").hexdigest()
833: 
834: 
835: def capacity(root, needed):
836:     if shutil.disk_usage(root).free < needed + RESERVE:
837:         raise RuntimeError("Not enough free space to keep the previous Steam runtime while replacing it.")
838: 
839: 
840: def directory(path, device):
841:     if path.is_symlink() or not path.is_dir() or path.stat().st_dev != device:
842:         raise RuntimeError("Runtime update needs real directories on the Steam filesystem: " + str(path))
843: 
844: 
845: def no_mounts(path):
846:     # st_dev alone misses a bind mount on the same filesystem. Never walk a
847:     # mounted subtree while retiring or rolling back an owned runtime tree.
848:     boundary = path.resolve()
849:     for line in Path("/proc/self/mountinfo").read_text().splitlines():
850:         field = line.split()[4]
851:         mount = Path(re.sub(r"\\([0-7]{3})", lambda m: chr(int(m[1], 8)), field))
852:         if mount == boundary or boundary in mount.parents:
853:             raise RuntimeError("Mounted runtime paths must be released before replacement: " + str(path))
854: 
855: 
856: def validate(runtime):
857:     directory(runtime, runtime.parent.stat().st_dev)
858:     platforms = list(runtime.glob("steamrt3c_platform_*"))
859:     if len(platforms) != 1 or platforms[0].is_symlink() or not platforms[0].is_dir():
860:         raise RuntimeError("Expected one complete Steam ARM64 platform directory.")
861:     targets = {}
862:     for name in LINKS:
863:         matches = list((platforms[0] / "files/lib/aarch64-linux-gnu").glob(name + ".*"))
864:         if len(matches) != 1:
865:             raise RuntimeError("Missing or ambiguous runtime library: " + name)
866:         target = matches[0]
867:         if not target.resolve(strict=True).is_relative_to(runtime.resolve(strict=True)):
868:             raise RuntimeError("Runtime library escapes the staged runtime.")
869:         with target.open("rb") as stream:
870:             header = stream.read(20)
871:         if (len(header) != 20 or header[:6] != b"\x7fELF\x02\x01"
872:                 or header[16:20] != b"\x03\x00\xb7\x00"):
873:             raise RuntimeError("Runtime library is not an ARM64 shared object: " + name)
874:         targets[name] = {"path": str(target.relative_to(runtime)), "sha256": digest(target)}
875:     return targets
876: 
877: 
878: class RuntimeUpdate:
879:     def __init__(self, root):
880:         self.link_root = Path(os.path.abspath(root))
881:         self.root = root.resolve(strict=True)
882:         if self.root == Path("/"):
883:             raise RuntimeError("Refusing the filesystem root as the Steam directory.")
884:         self.device = self.root.stat().st_dev
885:         self.work = self.root / ".steam-runtime-update"
886:         self.live = self.root / RUNTIME
887:         self.lib = self.root / "lib/aarch64-linux-gnu"
888:         self.state = None
889: 
890:     def write_state(self):
891:         pending = self.work / "state.tmp"
892:         with pending.open("w") as stream:
893:             json.dump(self.state, stream)
894:             stream.flush()
895:             os.fsync(stream.fileno())
896:         os.replace(pending, self.work / "state.json")
897:         fd = os.open(self.work, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY)
898:         try:
899:             os.fsync(fd)
900:         finally:
901:             os.close(fd)
902: 
903:     def clean(self):
904:         # Keep the journal until all other owned remnants are gone. A killed
905:         # cleanup is retried without accumulating another staging tree.
906:         for child in self.work.iterdir():
907:             if child.name == "state.json":
908:                 continue
909:             if child.is_dir() and not child.is_symlink():
910:                 no_mounts(child)
911:                 shutil.rmtree(child)
912:             else:
913:                 child.unlink()
914:         (self.work / "state.json").unlink(missing_ok=True)
915:         self.work.rmdir()
916:         self.state = None
917: 
918:     def check_parents(self):
919:         for path in (self.root / "lib", self.lib):
920:             if path.exists() or path.is_symlink():
921:                 directory(path, self.device)
922:                 no_mounts(path)
923: 
924:     def recover(self):
925:         if not self.work.exists() and not self.work.is_symlink():
926:             return
927:         directory(self.work, self.device)
928:         journal = self.work / "state.json"
929:         if not journal.exists():
930:             # mkdir can be interrupted before the first journal is written.
931:             # An empty directory is safe to remove; unknown contents are not.
932:             self.work.rmdir()
933:             return
934:         if journal.is_symlink() or not journal.is_file() or journal.stat().st_size > 65536:
935:             raise RuntimeError("Refusing an invalid runtime recovery journal.")
936:         self.state = json.loads(journal.read_text())
937:         if (self.state.get("format") != 1 or self.state.get("root") != str(self.root)
938:                 or self.state.get("link_root") != str(self.link_root)):
939:             raise RuntimeError("Unrecognized runtime recovery journal; retaining it.")
940:         no_mounts(self.work)
941:         phase = self.state["phase"]
942:         if phase in ("prepared", "committed"):
943:             no_mounts(self.live)
944:             if (set(self.state["targets"]) != set(LINKS)
945:                     or set(self.state["links"]) != set(LINKS)
946:                     or any(p not in ("lib/aarch64-linux-gnu", "lib")
947:                            for p in self.state["created_dirs"])):
948:                 raise RuntimeError("Invalid runtime recovery paths; retaining them.")
949:             for target in self.state["targets"].values():
950:                 relative = PurePosixPath(target["path"])
951:                 if relative.is_absolute() or ".." in relative.parts or not relative.parts:
952:                     raise RuntimeError("Invalid runtime recovery target.")
953:         self.check_parents()
954:         if phase == "committed":
955:             if identity(self.live) != self.state["candidate_id"] or validate(self.live) != self.state["targets"]:
956:                 raise RuntimeError("Committed runtime changed; keeping its recovery copy.")
957:             for name in LINKS:
958:                 if describe(self.lib / name) != self.new_link(name):
959:                     raise RuntimeError("Committed library link changed; keeping its recovery copy.")
960:         elif phase == "prepared":
961:             old_id = self.state["old_id"]
962:             live_id = identity(self.live)
963:             backup = self.work / "previous"
964:             if old_id is not None and live_id == old_id:
965:                 pass  # Not activated yet, or rollback was itself interrupted.
966:             else:
967:                 if old_id is not None and identity(backup) != old_id:
968:                     raise RuntimeError("Previous runtime identity changed; no automatic cleanup.")
969:                 if live_id == self.state["candidate_id"]:
970:                     os.replace(self.live, self.work / "discard")
971:                 elif live_id is not None:
972:                     raise RuntimeError("Active runtime identity changed; no automatic cleanup.")
973:                 if old_id is not None:
974:                     os.replace(backup, self.live)
975:             for name in LINKS:
976:                 saved = self.state["links"][name]
977:                 live = self.lib / name
978:                 current = describe(live)
979:                 if current == saved:
980:                     continue
981:                 if current != self.new_link(name):
982:                     raise RuntimeError("Library link changed outside the update; retaining recovery data.")
983:                 if saved["kind"] == "absent":
984:                     live.unlink()
985:                 else:
986:                     restore = self.work / ("restore-" + name)
987:                     restore.unlink(missing_ok=True)
988:                     shutil.copy2(self.work / ("saved-" + name), restore, follow_symlinks=False)
989:                     os.replace(restore, live)
990:             for relative in self.state["created_dirs"]:
991:                 path = self.root / relative
992:                 if path.exists():
993:                     path.rmdir()  # Never remove another writer's contents.
994:         elif phase != "staging":
995:             raise RuntimeError("Unknown runtime recovery phase; retaining it.")
996:         self.clean()
997: 
998:     def new_link(self, name):
999:         return {"kind": "link", "target": str(self.link_root / RUNTIME / self.state["targets"][name]["path"])}
1000: 
1001:     def begin(self):
1002:         self.work.mkdir(mode=0o700)
1003:         self.state = {"format": 1, "root": str(self.root), "link_root": str(self.link_root), "phase": "staging"}
1004:         self.write_state()
1005:         (self.work / "unpack").mkdir()
1006: 
1007:     def prepare(self):
1008:         candidate = self.work / "unpack" / RUNTIME
1009:         targets = validate(candidate)
1010:         self.check_parents()
1011:         links = {name: describe(self.lib / name) for name in LINKS}
1012:         capacity(self.root, sum((self.lib / name).stat().st_size for name in LINKS
1013:                                if links[name]["kind"] == "file"))
1014:         for name in LINKS:
1015:             if links[name]["kind"] != "absent":
1016:                 shutil.copy2(self.lib / name, self.work / ("saved-" + name), follow_symlinks=False)
1017:         old_id = identity(self.live)
1018:         no_mounts(self.live)
1019:         if old_id is not None and old_id[0] != self.device:
1020:             raise RuntimeError("Previous runtime is on a different filesystem.")
1021:         self.state.update(phase="prepared", targets=targets, links=links, old_id=old_id,
1022:                           candidate_id=identity(candidate),
1023:                           created_dirs=[p for p in ("lib/aarch64-linux-gnu", "lib")
1024:                                         if not (self.root / p).exists()])
1025:         self.write_state()  # The complete rollback description precedes every rename.
1026: 
1027:     def activate(self):
1028:         self.lib.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
1029:         if self.state["old_id"] is not None:
1030:             os.replace(self.live, self.work / "previous")
1031:         os.replace(self.work / "unpack" / RUNTIME, self.live)
1032:         for name in LINKS:
1033:             pending = self.work / ("new-" + name)
1034:             pending.symlink_to(self.new_link(name)["target"])
1035:             os.replace(pending, self.lib / name)
1036:         if validate(self.live) != self.state["targets"]:
1037:             raise RuntimeError("Activated runtime failed validation.")
1038:         for name in LINKS:
1039:             if describe(self.lib / name) != self.new_link(name) or not (self.lib / name).is_file():
1040:                 raise RuntimeError("Activated runtime library link failed validation.")
1041:         self.state["phase"] = "committed"
1042:         self.write_state()  # Only this point permits retiring the superseded tree.
1043:         self.clean()
1044: 
1045: 
1046: def fetch_archive(update, version_url, base_url):
1047:     owner_pid = os.getpid()
1048: 
1049:     def child_setup(limit=None):
1050:         # SIGKILL cannot run a Python cleanup handler. Linux must kill the
1051:         # transport child with its owner, before another updater can retry.
1052:         libc = ctypes.CDLL(None, use_errno=True)
1053:         if libc.prctl(1, signal.SIGKILL, 0, 0, 0) != 0:  # PR_SET_PDEATHSIG
1054:             raise OSError(ctypes.get_errno(), "Cannot bind downloader lifetime to installer")
1055:         if os.getppid() != owner_pid:  # Parent may have died before prctl.
1056:             os.kill(os.getpid(), signal.SIGKILL)
1057:         if limit is not None:
1058:             resource.setrlimit(resource.RLIMIT_FSIZE, (limit, limit))
1059: 
1060:     version = subprocess.run(["curl", "-fsSL", "--connect-timeout", "10", "--max-time", "30",
1061:                               "--max-filesize", "128", version_url],
1062:                              check=True, capture_output=True, timeout=35, preexec_fn=child_setup).stdout.decode().strip()
1063:     if not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]{0,79}", version):
1064:         raise RuntimeError("Invalid Steam runtime version.")
1065:     capacity(update.root, 1)
1066:     budget = min(MAX_ARCHIVE, shutil.disk_usage(update.root).free - RESERVE)
1067:     archive = update.work / "runtime.tar.xz"
1068: 
1069:     subprocess.run(["wget", "-t", "3", "-T", "30", "-O", str(archive),
1070:                     base_url + "/" + version + "/" + RUNTIME + ".tar.xz"],
1071:                    check=True, timeout=900, preexec_fn=lambda: child_setup(budget))
1072:     return archive
1073: 
1074: 
1075: def unpack_archive(update, archive):
1076:     # Inspect the complete compressed stream (including its integrity footer)
1077:     # before extraction; never use the live Steam directory as tar's destination.
1078:     needed = 0
1079:     seen = set()
1080:     with lzma.open(archive, "rb") as stream:
1081:         with tarfile.open(fileobj=stream, mode="r|") as archive_stream:
1082:             for member in archive_stream:
1083:                 path = PurePosixPath(member.name)
1084:                 if (path.is_absolute() or ".." in path.parts or not path.parts
1085:                         or path.parts[0] != RUNTIME or path.as_posix() in seen or member.size < 0):
1086:                     raise RuntimeError("Unsafe or duplicate runtime archive path.")
1087:                 if not (member.isfile() or member.isdir() or member.issym() or member.islnk()):
1088:                     raise RuntimeError("Unsupported runtime archive entry.")
1089:                 seen.add(path.as_posix())
1090:                 needed += ((member.size + 4095) // 4096) * 4096 + 8192
1091:                 if len(seen) > MAX_MEMBERS or needed > MAX_EXPANDED:
1092:                     raise RuntimeError("Runtime archive exceeds the bounded extraction budget.")
1093:         while True:
1094:             chunk = stream.read(1024 * 1024)
1095:             if stream.tell() > MAX_EXPANDED:
1096:                 raise RuntimeError("Runtime archive exceeds the decoded-stream budget.")
1097:             if not chunk:
1098:                 break
1099:     capacity(update.root, needed)
1100:     with tarfile.open(archive, "r:xz") as payload:
1101:         payload.extractall(update.work / "unpack", filter="data")
1102:     capacity(update.root, 0)
1103:     archive.unlink()
1104: 
1105: 
1106: def interrupted(signum, frame):
1107:     raise InterruptedError("Steam runtime update interrupted by signal " + str(signum))
1108: 
1109: 
1110: def main():
1111:     update = RuntimeUpdate(Path(sys.argv[1]))
1112:     # A persistent lock inode serializes this component; never unlink the lock
1113:     # file while another invocation may have opened it. No service is started.
1114:     lock_path = update.root / ".steam-runtime-update.lock"
1115:     fd = os.open(lock_path, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_NONBLOCK, 0o600)
1116:     try:
1117:         if not stat.S_ISREG(os.fstat(fd).st_mode):
1118:             raise RuntimeError("Runtime update lock is not a regular file.")
1119:         fcntl.flock(fd, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
1120:         for sig in (signal.SIGINT, signal.SIGTERM, signal.SIGHUP, signal.SIGALRM):
1121:             signal.signal(sig, interrupted)
1122:         signal.alarm(1800)
1123:         try:
1124:             update.recover()
1125:             update.check_parents()
1126:             capacity(update.root, 1)
1127:             update.begin()
1128:             archive = fetch_archive(update, sys.argv[2], sys.argv[3])
1129:             unpack_archive(update, archive)
1130:             update.prepare()
1131:             update.activate()
1132:             return 0
1133:         except Exception as error:
1134:             print("Steam runtime replacement failed: " + str(error), file=sys.stderr)
1135:             signal.alarm(0)
1136:             try:
1137:                 update.recover()
1138:             except Exception as recovery_error:
1139:                 print("Runtime recovery needs attention; previous data is retained at "
1140:                       + str(update.work) + ": " + str(recovery_error), file=sys.stderr)
1141:             return 1
1142:         finally:
1143:             signal.alarm(0)
1144:     finally:
1145:         os.close(fd)
1146: 
1147: 
1148: if __name__ == "__main__":
1149:     sys.exit(main())
1150: PY_STEAM_RUNTIME
1151:   then
1152:     return 0
1153:   fi
1154:   die "Steam runtime replacement failed. The installer has stopped."
1155: }

1157: install_steam_client_arm64() {
1158:   if steam_client_state install; then
1159:     return 0
1160:   fi
1161:   die "Steam client setup could not finish. Existing client files were kept."
1162: }
1163: 
1164: # The first call inspects only the client tree, before profile side effects.
1165: # Support metadata uses STEAM_DOT after profiles, preserving that override.
1166: # A legacy bootstrap check is not native readiness; no old receipt hash is used.
1167: steam_client_state() {
1168:   /usr/bin/python3 -I -S -B - "$1" "${STEAM_ROOT_PLAN}" "${STEAM_DOT:-}" "${STEAM_CLIENT_PLAN:-}" "${STEAM_CLIENT_METADATA_PLAN:-}" <<'PY_STEAM_CLIENT'
1169: # SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
1170: # Copyright (C) 2026 ROCKNIX (https://github.com/ROCKNIX)
1171: # #582: structure is a bootstrap check, never native readiness or account proof.
1172: import ctypes
1173: import errno
1174: import fcntl
1175: import hashlib
1176: import json
1177: import os
1178: from pathlib import Path
1179: import re
1180: import resource
1181: import selectors

1270: def need(ok, text):
1271:     if not ok:
1272:         raise RuntimeError(text)
