1274: 
1275: def identity(path):
1276:     try:
1277:         s = path.lstat()
1278:     except FileNotFoundError:
1279:         return None
1280:     return [s.st_dev, s.st_ino, stat.S_IFMT(s.st_mode)]
1281: 
1282: 
1283: def directory(path):
1284:     fd = os.open("/", os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY)
1285:     try:
1286:         for part in path.parts[1:]:
1287:             child = os.open(part, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY | os.O_NOFOLLOW, dir_fd=fd)
1288:             os.close(fd)
1289:             fd = child
1290:         return fd
1291:     except BaseException:
1292:         os.close(fd)
1293:         raise
1294: 
1295: 
1296: def open_file(path, flags=os.O_RDONLY, mode=0o600):
1297:     parent = directory(path.parent)
1298:     try:
1299:         return os.open(path.name, flags | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_NONBLOCK, mode, dir_fd=parent)
1300:     finally:
1301:         os.close(parent)
1302: 
1303: 
1304: def read_file(path, maximum):
1305:     fd = open_file(path)
1306:     try:
1307:         before = os.fstat(fd)
1308:         need(stat.S_ISREG(before.st_mode) and before.st_size <= maximum, "Unexpected or oversized file: " + str(path))
1309:         with os.fdopen(fd, "rb", closefd=False) as stream:
1310:             data = stream.read(maximum + 1)
1311:         after = os.fstat(fd)
1312:         need(len(data) <= maximum and stamp(before) == stamp(after), "File changed during inspection.")
1313:         return data
1314:     finally:
1315:         os.close(fd)
1316: 
1317: 
1318: def stamp(s):
1319:     return [s.st_dev, s.st_ino, s.st_mode, s.st_size, s.st_mtime_ns, s.st_ctime_ns]
1320: 
1321: 
1322: def fingerprint(path):
1323:     fd = open_file(path)
1324:     try:
1325:         s = os.fstat(fd)
1326:         need(stat.S_ISREG(s.st_mode), "Expected a regular file: " + str(path))
1327:         return stamp(s)
1328:     finally:
1329:         os.close(fd)
1330: 
1331: 
1332: def elf(path, executable=False):
1333:     # Bounded header/program-table reads. No loader, execution, ldd, or full-file
1334:     # receipt comparison: legitimate self-updates may change every file digest.
1335:     fd = open_file(path)
1336:     try:
1337:         before = os.fstat(fd)
1338:         need(stat.S_ISREG(before.st_mode) and before.st_size >= 64, "A core Steam file is missing or incomplete.")
1339:         header = os.pread(fd, 64, 0)
1340:         need(len(header) == 64 and header[:7] == b"\x7fELF\x02\x01\x01", "A core Steam file is not an ARM64 ELF.")
1341:         _, kind, machine, version, entry, phoff, _, _, ehsize, phsize, count, _, _, _ = struct.unpack("<16sHHIQQQIHHHHHH", header)
1342:         need(machine == 183 and version == 1 and kind in (2, 3) and (executable or kind == 3), "A core Steam file has an unsupported architecture or type.")
1343:         need(ehsize == 64 and phsize == 56 and 0 < count <= 128 and phoff >= 64
1344:              and phoff + count * 56 <= before.st_size, "A core Steam program table is incomplete.")
1345:         raw = os.pread(fd, count * 56, phoff)
1346:         need(len(raw) == count * 56, "A core Steam program table could not be read.")
1347:         loads = []
1348:         for i in range(count):
1349:             typ, flags, offset, address, _, filesz, memsz, align = struct.unpack_from("<IIQQQQQQ", raw, i * 56)
1350:             need(offset <= before.st_size and filesz <= before.st_size - offset, "A core Steam segment exceeds its file.")
1351:             if typ == 1:
1352:                 need(filesz <= memsz and (align in (0, 1) or align & (align - 1) == 0)
1353:                      and (align in (0, 1) or offset % align == address % align), "A core Steam load segment is invalid.")
1354:                 loads.append((flags, address, memsz))
1355:         need(loads, "A core Steam file has no load segment.")
1356:         if executable:
1357:             need(before.st_mode & 0o111 and entry > 0
1358:                  and any(flags & 1 and address <= entry < address + size for flags, address, size in loads),
1359:                  "The Steam bootstrap entrypoint is not executable.")
1360:         need(stamp(before) == stamp(os.fstat(fd)), "A core Steam file changed during inspection.")
1361:         return stamp(before)
1362:     finally:
1363:         os.close(fd)
1364: 
1365: 
1366: def bootstrap(root):
1367:     path = root / CLIENT
1368:     ident = identity(path)
1369:     need(ident is not None and ident[2] == stat.S_IFDIR, "The existing Steam client folder is ambiguous.")
1370:     fd = directory(path)
1371:     os.close(fd)
1372:     result = {"directory": ident, "core": {}}
1373:     for name in CORE:
1374:         result["core"][name] = elf(path / name, name == "steam")
1375:     return result
1376: 
1377: 
1378: def mounts_below(path):
1379:     raw = read_file(Path("/proc") / str(os.getpid()) / "mountinfo", 1024**2)
1380:     # proc regular files have size 0; read_file bounds bytes rather than trusting it.
1381:     for line in raw.decode().splitlines():
1382:         point = Path(re.sub(r"\\([0-7]{3})", lambda m: chr(int(m[1], 8)), line.split()[4]))
1383:         need(point != path and path not in point.parents, "A client transaction path has a mounted subtree.")
1384: 
1385: 
1386: def mount_rows():
1387:     raw = read_file(Path("/proc") / str(os.getpid()) / "mountinfo", 1024**2)
1388:     result = {}
1389:     for line in raw.decode().splitlines():
1390:         fields = line.split()
1391:         separator = fields.index("-")
1392:         need(separator >= 6 and len(fields) == separator + 4, "Storage mounts could not be inspected.")
1393:         point = re.sub(r"\\([0-7]{3})", lambda m: chr(int(m[1], 8)), fields[4])
1394:         result.setdefault(point, []).append(fields)
1395:     return result
1396: 
1397: 
1398: def available(path):
1399:     rows = mount_rows()
1400:     required = [Path("/storage")]
1401:     for p in (Path("/storage/games-external"), Path("/storage/roms")):
1402:         if path == p or p in path.parents:
1403:             required.append(p)
1404:     media = Path("/var/media")
1405:     if media in path.parents:
1406:         required.append(media / path.relative_to(media).parts[0])
1407:     covers = [Path(p) for p in rows if path == Path(p) or Path(p) in path.parents]
1408:     need(covers, "The Steam support storage is unavailable.")
1409:     required.append(max(covers, key=lambda p: len(p.parts)))
1410:     result = {}
1411:     for p in set(required):
1412:         found = rows.get(str(p), [])
1413:         need(p != Path("/") and len(found) == 1 and "rw" in found[0][5].split(",")
1414:              and "rw" in found[0][-1].split(","), "The Steam support storage is unavailable or read-only.")
1415:         result[str(p)] = found[0]
1416:     return result
1417: 
1418: 
1419: def bindings(plan, root, require_root=False):
1420:     current_mounts = mount_rows()
1421:     need(all(current_mounts.get(point) == [fields] for point, fields in plan["mounts"].items()),
1422:          "The Steam storage mount changed.")
1423:     for key in ("root", "target"):
1424:         for row in plan.get(key, {}).get("entries", []):
1425:             path = Path(row[0])
1426:             s = path.lstat()
1427:             now = [str(path), s.st_dev, s.st_ino, s.st_mode]
1428:             if stat.S_ISLNK(s.st_mode):
1429:                 now.append(os.readlink(path))
1430:             need(now == row, "The Steam storage path changed.")
1431:     if require_root:
1432:         need(Path(plan["steam"]).resolve(strict=True) == root, "The Steam storage destination changed.")
1433:         fd = directory(root)
1434:         os.close(fd)
1435: 
1436: 
1437: def consumers(root):
1438:     # A visible installer lock is not a lock on Steam's independent updater.
1439:     # Refuse an observed client; do not kill it or claim this scan eliminates races.
1440:     for p in Path("/proc").iterdir():
1441:         if not p.name.isdecimal() or int(p.name) == os.getpid():
1442:             continue
1443:         try:
1444:             exe = os.readlink(p / "exe").removesuffix(" (deleted)")
1445:             fd = os.open(p / "cmdline", os.O_RDONLY | os.O_NOFOLLOW)
1446:             try:
1447:                 cmd = os.read(fd, 4096).split(b"\0", 1)[0].decode(errors="replace")
1448:             finally:
1449:                 os.close(fd)
1450:         except (FileNotFoundError, ProcessLookupError):
1451:             continue
1452:         except PermissionError as error:
1453:             raise RuntimeError("Steam processes could not be checked safely.") from error
1454:         paths = (root / CLIENT, Path(plan_global["steam"]) / CLIENT)
1455:         need(not any(exe == str(x) or exe.startswith(str(x) + "/") or cmd.startswith(str(x) + "/") for x in paths),
1456:              "Close Steam before installing it again.")
1457: 
1458: 
1459: def journal_schema(rows, root):
1460:     header = rows[0]
1461:     need(header.get("op") == "header" and header.get("policy") == "arm64-bootstrap-55-v2",
1462:          "Unsupported client ownership policy was kept.")
1463:     work = root / WORK
1464:     need(stat.S_IMODE(work.lstat().st_mode) == 0o700
1465:          and work.lstat().st_uid == os.geteuid()
1466:          and (work / "owner.jsonl").lstat().st_nlink == 1, "Client staging ownership is ambiguous.")
1467:     meta = header["metadata"]
1468:     committed = any(row["op"] == "committed" for row in rows)
1469:     physical = Path(meta["dot"]["path"])
1470:     if not committed:
1471:         dot = probe_directory(header["dot"])
1472:         need(dot["path"] == str(physical), "The previous support destination changed.")
1473:         check_probe(meta["dot"])
1474:         need(available(physical) == meta["mounts"], "The previous support storage changed.")
1475:     need(physical != root and physical not in root.parents and root not in physical.parents
1476:          and physical not in (Path("/storage"), Path("/var/media"), Path("/"))
1477:          and (physical.is_relative_to("/storage") or physical.is_relative_to("/var/media")),
1478:          "Unsupported client metadata ownership.")
1479:     specifications = [
1480:         (str(root / "package"), "directory", None),
1481:         (str(root / "package/beta"), "file", "7075626c6963626574610a"),
1482:         (str(physical), "directory", None),
1483:         (str(physical / "steam"), "link", plan_global["steam"]),
1484:         (str(physical / "sdkarm64"), "link", str(Path(plan_global["steam"]) / "linuxarm64")),
1485:     ]
1486:     need([(n["path"], n["kind"], n["value"]) for n in meta["nodes"]] == specifications,
1487:          "Unexpected client metadata plan was kept.")
1488:     stage_allowed = {"archive": "file", "manifest": "file", "payload": "directory"}
1489:     for name, kind in EXPECTED.items():
1490:         stage_allowed["payload/" + name.rstrip("/")] = kind
1491:         for p in Path(name.rstrip("/")).parents:
1492:             if str(p) != ".":
1493:                 stage_allowed["payload/" + str(p)] = "directory"
1494:     external_allowed = {name: (kind, value) for name, kind, value in specifications}
1495:     for node in meta["nodes"]:
1496:         path = Path(node["path"]).parent
1497:         # Extra parents may be created only along the saved absent-dot path,
1498:         # below a pre-existing, identity-bound ancestor.
1499:         known_dirs = {row[0] for row in meta["dot"]["entries"]}
1500:         while path != Path("/") and str(path) not in known_dirs and path != root:
1501:             if physical == path or path in physical.parents:
1502:                 external_allowed[str(path)] = ("directory", None)
1503:             path = path.parent
1504:     seen_stage, seen_external = set(), set()
1505:     active_transfer = None
1506:     transfer_count = 0
1507:     for row in rows[1:]:
1508:         op = row["op"]
1509:         if op == "transfer-start":
1510:             transfer_count += 1
1511:             need(active_transfer is None and transfer_count <= 2
1512:                  and row.get("serial") == transfer_count, "Ambiguous transfer ownership record.")
1513:             active_transfer = transfer_count
1514:         elif op == "transfer-stopped":
1515:             need(active_transfer is not None and row.get("serial") == active_transfer,
1516:                  "Unmatched transfer quiescence record.")
1517:             active_transfer = None
1518:         elif op == "stage":
1519:             name = row["path"]
1520:             need(name in stage_allowed and name not in seen_stage and row["kind"] == stage_allowed[name],
1521:                  "Unrecognized stage ownership record.")
1522:             seen_stage.add(name)
1523:         elif op == "external":
1524:             name = row["path"]
1525:             need(name in external_allowed and name not in seen_external
1526:                  and (row["kind"], row["value"]) == external_allowed[name], "Unrecognized metadata ownership record.")
1527:             seen_external.add(name)
1528:         elif op == "intent":
1529:             need(all(n["path"] in external_allowed and (n["kind"], n["value"]) == external_allowed[n["path"]]
1530:                      for n in row["nodes"]), "Unrecognized metadata creation intent.")
1531:         elif op == "prepared":
1532:             need(isinstance(row["tree"], dict) and len(row["tree"]) <= 256
1533:                  and all(not Path(p).is_absolute() and ".." not in Path(p).parts for p in row["tree"]),
1534:                  "Unrecognized prepared client inventory.")
1535:         else:
1536:             need(op in ("publishing", "committed", "failure"), "Unexpected client phase.")
1537:         if op in ("stage", "external"):
1538:             ident = row["id"]
1539:             kind = {"file": stat.S_IFREG, "directory": stat.S_IFDIR, "link": stat.S_IFLNK}[row["kind"]]
1540:             need(isinstance(ident, list) and len(ident) == 3
1541:                  and all(type(v) is int for v in ident) and ident[2] == kind,
1542:                  "Malformed client node identity.")
1543: 
1544: 
1545: def load_journal(work, root):
1546:     need(identity(work) is not None and identity(work)[2] == stat.S_IFDIR, "Unknown client staging state was kept.")
1547:     mounts_below(work)
1548:     raw = read_file(work / "owner.jsonl", MAX_JOURNAL)
1549:     need(raw.endswith(b"\n"), "An interrupted client journal was kept for review.")
1550:     lines = raw.splitlines(keepends=True)
1551:     need(lines and lines[0].endswith(b"\n"), "Incomplete client ownership record was kept.")
1552:     rows = [json.loads(line) for line in lines if line.endswith(b"\n")]
1553:     h = rows[0]
1554:     need(h.get("format") == 1 and h.get("root") == str(root)
1555:          and h.get("root_id") == identity(root) and h.get("work_id") == identity(work)
1556:          and h.get("journal_id") == identity(work / "owner.jsonl"), "Client ownership changed; existing state was kept.")
1557:     need(len(rows) <= 512 and all(isinstance(row, dict) for row in rows), "Unsupported client ownership record.")
1558:     allowed = {"header", "stage", "external", "intent", "prepared", "publishing", "committed", "failure", "transfer-start", "transfer-stopped"}
1559:     need(all(row.get("op") in allowed for row in rows), "Unknown client transaction phase was kept.")
1560:     journal_schema(rows, root)
1561:     tx = Transaction(root, h["dot"], h["metadata"], rows)
1562:     tx.check_owned_state()
1563:     if any(row["op"] == "committed" for row in rows):
1564:         bootstrap(root)  # A committed client's updater may legitimately change bytes.
1565:     elif tx.published():
1566:         need(tx.tree(root / CLIENT) == tx.prepared()["tree"], "An uncommitted published client changed; it was kept.")
1567:     return rows
1568: 
1569: 
1570: def inspect_tree(plan, root, check_lock=True):
1571:     bindings(plan, root)
1572:     consumers(root)
1573:     if check_lock and identity(root / LOCK) is not None:
1574:         fd = open_file(root / LOCK)
1575:         try:
1576:             need(stat.S_ISREG(os.fstat(fd).st_mode), "The client lock is ambiguous.")
1577:             fcntl.flock(fd, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
1578:             fcntl.flock(fd, fcntl.LOCK_UN)
1579:         finally:
1580:             os.close(fd)
1581:     work = root / WORK
1582:     if identity(work) is not None:
1583:         rows = load_journal(work, root)
1584:         # Recovery runs later under the lock. This read-only branch does not
1585:         # bless an existing directory as installed, or normalize old contents.
1586:         return {"kind": "recovery", "root": str(root), "work_id": identity(work),
1587:                 "journal_id": identity(work / "owner.jsonl"), "dot": rows[0]["dot"],
1588:                 "committed": any(row["op"] == "committed" for row in rows)}
1589:     if identity(root / CLIENT) is not None:
1590:         return {"kind": "existing", "root": str(root), "bootstrap": bootstrap(root)}
1591:     for name in ("linuxarm64.zip", "steamrtarm64\\libs", "steamrtarm64\\swiftshader",
1592:                  "steamrtarm64\\libs\\libcurl.so", "steamrtarm64\\libs\\libnghttp2.so", "steamrtarm64\\libs\\libnghttp2.so.14"):
1593:         need(identity(root / name) is None, "An earlier Steam download is incomplete. Its files were kept.")
1594:     return {"kind": "fresh", "root": str(root)}
1595: 
1596: 
1597: def probe_directory(value):
1598:     path = Path(value)
1599:     need(path.is_absolute() and str(path) == os.path.normpath(value) and not value.startswith("//")
1600:          and not any(ord(c) < 32 for c in value), "The Steam support folder path is ambiguous.")
1601:     entries = []
1602:     current = Path("/")
1603:     pending = list(path.parts[1:])
1604:     links = 0
1605:     while pending:
1606:         part = pending.pop(0)
1607:         if part in ("", "."):
1608:             continue
1609:         if part == "..":
1610:             current = current.parent
1611:             continue
1612:         candidate = current / part
1613:         ident = identity(candidate)
1614:         if ident is None:
1615:             need(".." not in pending, "A missing support folder precedes a parent traversal.")
1616:             return {"input": value, "path": str(candidate.joinpath(*pending)), "entries": entries, "exists": False}
1617:         s = candidate.lstat()
1618:         row = [str(candidate), *ident]
1619:         if stat.S_ISLNK(s.st_mode):
1620:             links += 1
1621:             need(links <= 40, "The Steam support folder has too many links.")
1622:             target = os.readlink(candidate)
1623:             need(target and not any(ord(c) < 32 for c in target), "The Steam support folder link is ambiguous.")
1624:             row.append(target)
1625:             entries.append(row)
1626:             # Resolve each target component before applying any following '..'.
1627:             # Lexical normpath would erase a preceding directory symlink.
1628:             pending = target.split("/") + pending
1629:             if target.startswith("/"):
1630:                 current = Path("/")
1631:         else:
1632:             need(stat.S_ISDIR(s.st_mode), "A file is blocking the Steam support folder.")
1633:             entries.append(row)
1634:             current = candidate
1635:     return {"input": value, "path": str(current), "entries": entries, "exists": True}
1636: 
1637: 
1638: def check_probe(probe):
1639:     for row in probe["entries"]:
1640:         path = Path(row[0])
1641:         now = [str(path), *(identity(path) or [])]
1642:         if path.is_symlink():
1643:             now.append(os.readlink(path))
1644:         need(now == row, "The Steam support folder changed.")
1645:     need(probe_directory(probe["input"])["path"] == probe["path"],
1646:          "The logical Steam support destination changed.")
1647: 
1648: 
1649: def describe(path):
1650:     ident = identity(path)
1651:     if ident is None:
1652:         return None
1653:     if ident[2] == stat.S_IFLNK:
1654:         return {"id": ident, "target": os.readlink(path)}
1655:     if ident[2] == stat.S_IFREG:
1656:         return {"id": ident, "bytes": read_file(path, 4096).hex(), "stamp": fingerprint(path)}
1657:     need(ident[2] == stat.S_IFDIR, "Unsupported Steam metadata entry.")
1658:     return {"id": ident}
1659: 
1660: 
1661: def metadata(root, dot_value, tree):
1662:     dot = probe_directory(dot_value)
1663:     physical = Path(dot["path"])
1664:     need(physical not in (Path("/"), Path("/storage"), Path("/var/media")) and physical != root and root not in physical.parents
1665:          and physical not in root.parents, "The Steam support folder overlaps client storage.")
1666:     # Preserve profile-selected paths, but require them on available /storage or
1667:     # /var/media storage. Root-path/mount admission remains the shared root helper.
1668:     need(physical.is_relative_to("/storage") or physical.is_relative_to("/var/media"), "The Steam support folder is outside supported storage.")
1669:     if tree["kind"] == "existing":
1670:         need(dot["exists"], "The existing client support folder is missing. Existing files were kept.")
1671:     nodes = [
1672:         (root / "package", "directory", None),
1673:         (root / "package/beta", "file", "7075626c6963626574610a"),
1674:         (physical, "directory", None),
1675:         (physical / "steam", "link", plan_global["steam"]),
1676:         (physical / "sdkarm64", "link", str(Path(plan_global["steam"]) / "linuxarm64")),
1677:     ]
1678:     result = {"dot": dot, "mounts": available(physical), "nodes": []}
1679:     for path, kind, value in nodes:
1680:         before = describe(path)
1681:         if before is not None:
1682:             if kind == "directory":
1683:                 need(before["id"][2] == stat.S_IFDIR, "A Steam metadata directory is blocked.")
1684:             elif tree["kind"] == "fresh":
1685:                 equal = before.get("bytes") == value if kind == "file" else (
1686:                     "target" in before and os.path.realpath(path) == os.path.realpath(value))
1687:                 need(equal, "Existing Steam support settings were kept. Fresh setup cannot replace them.")
1688:             elif kind == "file":
1689:                 need(before["id"][2] == stat.S_IFREG, "The existing Steam channel entry is ambiguous.")
1690:             else:
1691:                 need(before["id"][2] == stat.S_IFLNK, "An existing Steam support alias is ambiguous.")
1692:             # Existing client channels and aliases belong to Steam/the player:
1693:             # inspect their types but neither replace them nor assert readiness.
1694:         result["nodes"].append({"path": str(path), "kind": kind, "value": value, "before": before})
1695:     return result
1696: 
1697: 
1698: def same_metadata(saved, root, dot, tree):
1699:     current = metadata(root, dot, tree)
1700:     need(current == saved, "Steam support settings changed during installation.")
1701:     check_probe(saved["dot"])
1702: 
1703: 
1704: def digest_file(path, maximum):
1705:     fd = open_file(path)
1706:     try:
1707:         s = os.fstat(fd)
1708:         need(stat.S_ISREG(s.st_mode) and s.st_size <= maximum, "Unexpected client file size or type.")
1709:         h = hashlib.sha256()
1710:         with os.fdopen(fd, "rb", closefd=False) as stream:
1711:             while chunk := stream.read(65536):
1712:                 h.update(chunk)
1713:         need(stamp(s) == stamp(os.fstat(fd)), "A client file changed during validation.")
1714:         return h.hexdigest()
1715:     finally:
1716:         os.close(fd)
1717: 
1718: 
1719: def manifest(raw):
1720:     need(len(raw) <= 2 * 1024**2, "Steam manifest exceeded its limit.")
1721:     text = raw.decode("ascii")
1722:     tokens, end = [], 0
1723:     for match in re.finditer(r'"(?:[^"\\]|\\.)*"|[{}]', text):
1724:         need(not text[end:match.start()].strip(), "Unrecognized Steam manifest syntax.")
1725:         tokens.append(match.group())
1726:         end = match.end()
1727:     need(not text[end:].strip() and len(tokens) < 5000, "Steam manifest exceeded its syntax limits.")
1728:     pos = 0
1729:     def obj(depth=0):
1730:         nonlocal pos
1731:         need(depth < 8, "Steam manifest nesting exceeded its limit.")
1732:         result = {}
1733:         while pos < len(tokens) and tokens[pos] != "}":
1734:             key = json.loads(tokens[pos])
1735:             pos += 1
1736:             need(isinstance(key, str) and key not in result and pos < len(tokens), "Ambiguous Steam manifest key.")
1737:             token = tokens[pos]
1738:             pos += 1
1739:             if token == "{":
1740:                 result[key] = obj(depth + 1)
1741:                 need(pos < len(tokens) and tokens[pos] == "}", "Incomplete Steam manifest.")
1742:                 pos += 1
1743:             else:
1744:                 result[key] = json.loads(token)
1745:         return result
1746:     tree = obj()
1747:     need(pos == len(tokens), "Trailing Steam manifest data.")
1748:     selected = tree["linuxarm64"]["bins_linuxarm64_linuxarm64"]
1749:     name, checksum, size = selected["file"], selected["sha2"], selected["size"]
1750:     need(isinstance(name, str) and re.fullmatch(r"bins_linuxarm64_linuxarm64\.zip\.[0-9a-f]{40}", name)
1751:          and isinstance(checksum, str) and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", checksum)
1752:          and isinstance(size, str) and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9]{1,9}", size), "Unsupported Steam package selection.")
1753:     size = int(size)
1754:     need(22 <= size <= MAX_ZIP, "Steam client archive exceeds the supported size.")
1755:     return name, checksum, size
1756: 
1757: 
1758: def process_identity(pid):
1759:     path = Path("/proc") / str(pid)
1760:     try:
1761:         raw = read_file(path / "stat", 16384).decode()
1762:     except (FileNotFoundError, ProcessLookupError):
1763:         # A disappeared PID is a normal race. A present PID with unreadable stat
1764:         # is a visibility failure, never evidence of an empty process census.
1765:         need(identity(path) is None, "A live process identity could not be read.")
1766:         return None
1767:     prefix, separator, suffix = raw.rpartition(")")
1768:     fields = suffix.split()
1769:     need(separator and prefix.startswith(str(pid) + " (") and len(fields) >= 20,
1770:          "Malformed process identity; client state was kept.")
1771:     ppid, start = int(fields[1]), int(fields[19])
1772:     need(ppid >= 0 and start >= 0, "Invalid process owner or start time.")
1773:     return {"pid": pid, "ppid": ppid, "start_ticks": start}
1774: 
1775: 
1776: def direct_children(deadline):
1777:     # /proc/PID/task/PID/children is optional on the target kernel. The numeric
1778:     # stat census is the already-used PPID/start-time contract instead.
1779:     owner = os.getpid()
1780:     result, seen_owner, count = [], False, 0
1781:     for path in Path("/proc").iterdir():
1782:         if not path.name.isdecimal():
1783:             continue
1784:         count += 1
1785:         need(count <= 65536 and time.monotonic() < deadline, "Process census exceeded its bound.")
1786:         row = process_identity(int(path.name))
1787:         if row is not None:
1788:             seen_owner |= row["pid"] == owner
1789:             if row["ppid"] == owner:
1790:                 result.append(row)
1791:     need(seen_owner, "Process census does not show this supervisor.")
1792:     return result
1793: 
1794: 
1795: def terminate_group(proc):
1796:     # Always retire the group, including successful drivers' leftover children.
1797:     # The helper is a subreaper: orphaned setsid descendants become its children.
1798:     try:
1799:         os.killpg(proc.pid, signal.SIGKILL)
1800:     except ProcessLookupError:
1801:         pass
1802:     proc.wait(timeout=5)
1803:     deadline = time.monotonic() + 5
1804:     while True:
1805:         try:
1806:             pid, _ = os.waitpid(-1, os.WNOHANG)
1807:         except ChildProcessError:
1808:             return  # Kernel ECHILD after the leader was reaped is quiescence.
1809:         need(time.monotonic() < deadline, "A client transfer child did not retire.")
1810:         if pid:
1811:             continue
1812:         for row in direct_children(deadline):
1813:             need(time.monotonic() < deadline, "Transfer child cleanup exceeded its bound.")
1814:             try:
1815:                 pfd = os.pidfd_open(row["pid"], 0)
1816:             except ProcessLookupError:
1817:                 continue
1818:             try:
1819:                 current = process_identity(row["pid"])
1820:                 if current is None:
1821:                     continue
1822:                 need(current == row and current["ppid"] == os.getpid(),
1823:                      "A transfer child identity changed; client state was kept.")
1824:                 try:
1825:                     signal.pidfd_send_signal(pfd, signal.SIGKILL)
1826:                 except ProcessLookupError:
1827:                     pass
1828:             finally:
1829:                 os.close(pfd)
1830:         time.sleep(0.01)
1831: 
1832: 
1833: def transfer(url, fd, limit, seconds, quiescent):
1834:     argv = ["curl", "--disable", "--fail", "--silent", "--show-error", "--location",
1835:             "--proto", "=https", "--proto-redir", "=https", "--max-redirs", "5",
1836:             "--connect-timeout", "10", "--max-time", str(seconds),
1837:             "--max-filesize", str(limit), "--output", "-", url]
1838:     parent = os.getpid()
1839:     def child_limits():
1840:         resource.setrlimit(resource.RLIMIT_FSIZE, (limit, limit))
1841:         if LIBC.prctl(1, signal.SIGKILL, 0, 0, 0) or os.getppid() != parent:
1842:             os._exit(125)
1843:     proc = subprocess.Popen(argv, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=fd, stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
1844:                             start_new_session=True, preexec_fn=child_limits)
1845:     errors = bytearray()
1846:     deadline = time.monotonic() + seconds + 2
1847:     selector = None
1848:     try:
1849:         selector = selectors.DefaultSelector()
1850:         selector.register(proc.stderr, selectors.EVENT_READ)
1851:         while selector.get_map():
1852:             need(time.monotonic() < deadline, "Steam client transfer timed out.")
1853:             for key, _ in selector.select(0.1):
1854:                 block = os.read(key.fd, 8192)
1855:                 if not block:
1856:                     selector.unregister(key.fileobj)
1857:                 else:
1858:                     errors.extend(block)
1859:                     need(len(errors) <= 65536, "Steam client transfer log exceeded its limit.")
1860:         code = proc.wait(timeout=max(0.01, deadline - time.monotonic()))
1861:         need(code == 0, "Steam client transfer failed (exit " + str(code) + ").")
1862:         os.fsync(fd)
1863:     finally:
1864:         try:
1865:             if selector is not None:
1866:                 selector.close()
1867:             proc.stderr.close()
1868:         finally:
1869:             terminate_group(proc)
1870:             # An exception, visibility gap or deadline before ECHILD must leave
1871:             # a durable unmatched transfer-start. Never authorize stage cleanup.
1872:             quiescent()
1873: 
1874: 
1875: def rename_new(source, target):
1876:     a, b = directory(source.parent), directory(target.parent)
1877:     try:
1878:         need(LIBC.renameat2(a, os.fsencode(source.name), b, os.fsencode(target.name), 1) == 0,
1879:              "The new Steam client could not be published without replacing an existing path.")
1880:         os.fsync(a)
1881:         os.fsync(b)
1882:     finally:
1883:         os.close(a)
1884:         os.close(b)
1885: 
1886: 
1887: class Transaction:
1888:     def __init__(self, root, dot, meta, rows=None):
1889:         self.root, self.work, self.dot, self.meta = root, root / WORK, dot, meta
1890:         self.rows = rows or []
1891:         self.journal = None
1892: 
1893:     def append(self, row):
1894:         self.rows.append(row)  # Active cleanup knows identity even if journaling fails.
1895:         raw = (json.dumps(row, sort_keys=True) + "\n").encode()
1896:         need(os.fstat(self.journal).st_size + len(raw) <= MAX_JOURNAL, "Client journal exceeded its limit.")
1897:         need(os.write(self.journal, raw) == len(raw), "Short client journal write.")
1898:         os.fsync(self.journal)
1899: 
1900:     def begin(self):
1901:         self.work.mkdir(mode=0o700)
1902:         self.journal = open_file(self.work / "owner.jsonl", os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL | os.O_APPEND)
1903:         self.append({"op": "header", "format": 1, "policy": "arm64-bootstrap-55-v2", "root": str(self.root), "root_id": identity(self.root),
1904:                      "work_id": identity(self.work), "journal_id": identity(self.work / "owner.jsonl"),
1905:                      "dot": self.dot, "metadata": self.meta})
1906:         fd = directory(self.root)
1907:         os.fsync(fd)
1908:         os.close(fd)
1909: 
1910:     def add(self, relative, kind, data=None):
1911:         path = self.work / relative
1912:         need(not Path(relative).is_absolute() and ".." not in Path(relative).parts, "Invalid owned stage name.")
1913:         if kind == "directory":
1914:             parent = directory(path.parent)
1915:             try:
1916:                 os.mkdir(path.name, 0o700, dir_fd=parent)
1917:             finally:
1918:                 os.close(parent)
1919:             self.append({"op": "stage", "path": relative, "id": identity(path), "kind": kind})
1920:             return None
1921:         if kind == "link":
1922:             parent = directory(path.parent)
1923:             try:
1924:                 os.symlink(data, path.name, dir_fd=parent)
1925:             finally:
1926:                 os.close(parent)
1927:             self.append({"op": "stage", "path": relative, "id": identity(path), "kind": kind, "target": data})
1928:             return None
1929:         fd = open_file(path, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL)
1930:         try:
1931:             self.append({"op": "stage", "path": relative, "id": identity(path), "kind": "file"})
1932:         except BaseException:
1933:             os.close(fd)
1934:             raise
1935:         return fd
1936: 
1937:     def put(self, relative, data):
1938:         fd = self.add(relative, "file")
1939:         try:
1940:             need(os.write(fd, data) == len(data), "Short staging write.")
1941:             os.fsync(fd)
1942:         finally:
1943:             os.close(fd)
1944: 
1945:     def download(self, url, fd, limit, seconds):
1946:         serial = 1 + sum(row["op"] == "transfer-start" for row in self.rows)
1947:         self.append({"op": "transfer-start", "serial": serial})
1948:         transfer(url, fd, limit, seconds,
1949:                  lambda: self.append({"op": "transfer-stopped", "serial": serial}))
1950: 
1951:     def fetch(self):
1952:         fd = self.add("manifest", "file")
1953:         try:
1954:             self.download("https://client-update.fastly.steamstatic.com/steam_client_publicbeta_linuxarm64", fd, 2 * 1024**2, 30)
1955:         finally:
1956:             os.close(fd)
1957:         name, checksum, size = manifest(read_file(self.work / "manifest", 2 * 1024**2))
1958:         need(os.statvfs(self.root).f_bavail * os.statvfs(self.root).f_frsize >= size + MAX_OUTPUT + RESERVE,
1959:              "There is not enough free space to prepare the Steam client.")
1960:         fd = self.add("archive", "file")
1961:         try:
1962:             self.download("https://client-update.steamstatic.com/" + name, fd, size, 180)
1963:         finally:
1964:             os.close(fd)
1965:         need((self.work / "archive").stat().st_size == size and digest_file(self.work / "archive", MAX_ZIP) == checksum,
1966:              "The Steam client download did not match its manifest.")
1967:         return size, checksum
1968: 
1969:     def extract(self, size):
1970:         archive = self.work / "archive"
1971:         fd = open_file(archive)
1972:         rows = []
1973:         try:
1974:             def read(offset, length):
1975:                 need(0 <= offset <= size and 0 <= length <= size - offset, "ZIP record exceeds the archive.")
1976:                 raw = os.pread(fd, length, offset)
1977:                 need(len(raw) == length, "Incomplete ZIP record.")
1978:                 return raw
1979:             sig, disk, cd_disk, count_disk, count, cd_size, cd_start, comment = struct.unpack("<4s4H2IH", read(size - 22, 22))
1980:             need(sig == b"PK\x05\x06" and disk == cd_disk == comment == 0 and count_disk == count
1981:                  and count == len(EXPECTED) <= 128 and cd_start + cd_size == size - 22 and cd_size <= 65536,
1982:                  "Unsupported Steam ZIP directory.")
1983:             central = read(cd_start, cd_size)
1984:             pos, next_local, expanded, seen = 0, None, 0, set()
1985:             for _ in range(count):
1986:                 need(pos + 46 <= len(central), "Incomplete ZIP directory.")
1987:                 fields = struct.unpack_from("<4s6H3I5H2I", central, pos)
1988:                 sig, made, required, flags, method, mtime, mdate, crc, packed, unpacked, nlen, extra, note, disk, _, attr, local = fields
1989:                 need(sig == b"PK\x01\x02" and flags == extra == note == disk == 0 and required == 20
1990:                      and method in (0, 8) and pos + 46 + nlen <= len(central), "Unsupported Steam ZIP member.")
1991:                 name_bytes = central[pos + 46:pos + 46 + nlen]
1992:                 name = name_bytes.decode("ascii")
1993:                 canonical = NAME_MAP.get(name, name)
1994:                 kind = EXPECTED.get(canonical)
1995:                 need(kind is not None and canonical not in seen and "\\" not in canonical
1996:                      and all(part not in ("", ".", "..") for part in canonical.rstrip("/").split("/")),
1997:                      "Unexpected or duplicate Steam ZIP path.")
1998:                 seen.add(canonical)
1999:                 if kind == "directory":
2000:                     need(method == 0 and unpacked == packed == crc == 0 and made == 20 and attr == 0x40000000,
2001:                          "Unexpected Steam directory attributes.")
2002:                 elif kind == "link":
2003:                     need(made == 788 and stat.S_IFMT(attr >> 16) == stat.S_IFLNK and method == 0
2004:                          and 0 < unpacked == packed <= 4096, "Unexpected stored Steam link.")
2005:                 else:
2006:                     need(made == 20 and attr == 2176188416 and method == 8, "Unsupported Steam file attributes.")
2007:                 need(unpacked <= MAX_MEMBER, "Steam ZIP member exceeds its output limit.")
2008:                 expanded += unpacked
2009:                 need(expanded <= MAX_OUTPUT, "Steam ZIP exceeds its total output limit.")
2010:                 if next_local is None:
2011:                     need(local in (0, 20), "Unsupported ZIP prefix.")
2012:                 else:
2013:                     need(local == next_local, "Overlapping or unlisted ZIP local data.")
2014:                 header = struct.unpack("<4s5H3I2H", read(local, 30))
2015:                 need(header == (b"PK\x03\x04", required, flags, method, mtime, mdate, crc, packed, unpacked, nlen, 0)
2016:                      and read(local + 30, nlen) == name_bytes, "ZIP local and central records disagree.")
2017:                 offset = local + 30 + nlen
2018:                 next_local = offset + packed
2019:                 need(next_local <= cd_start, "ZIP payload overlaps its directory.")
2020:                 rows.append({"name": canonical, "kind": kind, "offset": offset, "packed": packed,
2021:                              "bytes": unpacked, "crc": crc, "method": method})
2022:                 pos += 46 + nlen
2023:             need(pos == len(central) and next_local == cd_start and seen == set(EXPECTED), "Incomplete Steam ZIP inventory.")
2024:             self.add("payload", "directory")
2025:             dirs = {str(Path(row["name"]).parent) for row in rows}
2026:             dirs |= {row["name"].rstrip("/") for row in rows if row["kind"] == "directory"}
2027:             dirs.discard(".")
2028:             for name in sorted(dirs, key=lambda x: (len(Path(x).parts), x)):
2029:                 self.add("payload/" + name, "directory")
2030:             deadline, total, links, receipt = time.monotonic() + 90, 0, [], []
2031:             for row in rows:
2032:                 if row["kind"] == "directory":
2033:                     continue
2034:                 target = "payload/" + row["name"]
2035:                 out = self.add(target, "file") if row["kind"] == "file" else None
2036:                 unpacker = zlib.decompressobj(-15) if row["method"] == 8 else None
2037:                 length, crc, sha, link = 0, 0, hashlib.sha256(), bytearray()
2038:                 try:
2039:                     remaining, offset = row["packed"], row["offset"]
2040:                     while remaining:
2041:                         chunk = read(offset, min(65536, remaining))
2042:                         remaining -= len(chunk)
2043:                         offset += len(chunk)
2044:                         while chunk:
2045:                             need(time.monotonic() < deadline, "Steam ZIP decoding timed out.")
2046:                             data = unpacker.decompress(chunk, 65536) if unpacker else chunk
2047:                             chunk = unpacker.unconsumed_tail if unpacker else b""
2048:                             length += len(data)
2049:                             total += len(data)
2050:                             need(length <= row["bytes"] and total <= MAX_OUTPUT, "Steam ZIP produced too much output.")
2051:                             crc = zlib.crc32(data, crc)
2052:                             sha.update(data)
2053:                             if out is None:
2054:                                 link.extend(data)
2055:                             else:
2056:                                 need(os.write(out, data) == len(data), "Short Steam client write.")
2057:                             if unpacker:
2058:                                 need(not unpacker.unused_data, "Trailing compressed Steam data.")
2059:                     need(length == row["bytes"] and crc == row["crc"] and (not unpacker or unpacker.eof),
2060:                          "Steam client CRC, length, or compressed stream verification failed.")
2061:                     if out is not None:
2062:                         os.fchmod(out, 0o755 if Path(row["name"]).name in EXECUTABLES else 0o644)
2063:                         os.fsync(out)
2064:                     else:
2065:                         value = link.decode("ascii")
2066:                         need(value not in ("", ".", "..") and "/" not in value and "\\" not in value
2067:                              and not any(ord(c) < 32 for c in value)
2068:                              and EXPECTED.get(str(Path(row["name"]).parent / value)) == "file",
2069:                              "Steam link target is outside the validated client.")
2070:                         links.append((target, value))
2071:                     receipt.append({"path": row["name"], "kind": row["kind"], "bytes": length, "sha256": sha.hexdigest()})
2072:                 finally:
2073:                     if out is not None:
2074:                         os.close(out)
2075:             for target, value in links:
2076:                 self.add(target, "link", value)
2077:             for name in dirs:
2078:                 os.chmod(self.work / "payload" / name, 0o755, follow_symlinks=False)
2079:             bootstrap(self.work / "payload")
2080:             tree = self.tree(self.work / "payload" / CLIENT)
2081:             self.append({"op": "prepared", "client_id": identity(self.work / "payload" / CLIENT),
2082:                          "tree": tree, "members": receipt})
2083:         finally:
2084:             os.close(fd)
2085: 
2086:     def tree(self, path):
2087:         mounts_below(path)
2088:         result = {}
2089:         for base, dirs, files in os.walk(path, followlinks=False):
2090:             need(len(result) <= 256, "The staged client has too many paths.")
2091:             for name in dirs + files:
2092:                 p = Path(base) / name
2093:                 s = p.lstat()
2094:                 key = str(p.relative_to(path))
2095:                 entry = {"id": identity(p), "mode": stat.S_IMODE(s.st_mode)}
2096:                 if stat.S_ISLNK(s.st_mode):
2097:                     entry["target"] = os.readlink(p)
2098:                 elif stat.S_ISREG(s.st_mode):
2099:                     need(s.st_nlink == 1, "The staged client has a hard link.")
2100:                     entry.update(bytes=s.st_size, sha256=digest_file(p, MAX_MEMBER))
2101:                 else:
2102:                     need(stat.S_ISDIR(s.st_mode), "Unexpected staged client node.")
2103:                 result[key] = entry
2104:         return result
2105: 
2106:     def prepare_metadata(self):
2107:         check_probe(self.meta["dot"])
2108:         need(available(Path(self.meta["dot"]["path"])) == self.meta["mounts"],
2109:              "The support storage changed before metadata writes.")
2110:         self.append({"op": "intent", "nodes": self.meta["nodes"]})
2111:         for node in self.meta["nodes"]:
2112:             path = Path(node["path"])
2113:             if node["before"] is not None:
2114:                 need(describe(path) == node["before"], "Steam support settings changed.")
2115:                 continue
2116:             # Only absent parents/nodes are created, never substituted for
2117:             # existing aliases, channels, SDK trees or custom files.
2118:             missing = []
2119:             parent = path.parent
2120:             while identity(parent) is None:
2121:                 missing.append(parent)
2122:                 parent = parent.parent
2123:             for p in reversed(missing):
2124:                 self.external(p, "directory", None)
2125:             self.external(path, node["kind"], node["value"])
2126: 
2127:     def external(self, path, kind, value):
2128:         check_probe(self.meta["dot"])
2129:         self.append({"op": "intent", "nodes": [{"path": str(path), "kind": kind, "value": value, "before": None}]})
2130:         need(identity(path) is None, "A Steam metadata destination appeared.")
2131:         parent = directory(path.parent)
2132:         parent_id = identity(path.parent)
2133:         try:
2134:             if kind == "directory":
2135:                 os.mkdir(path.name, 0o755, dir_fd=parent)
2136:             elif kind == "link":
2137:                 os.symlink(value, path.name, dir_fd=parent)
2138:             else:
2139:                 fd = os.open(path.name, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL | os.O_NOFOLLOW, 0o644, dir_fd=parent)
2140:                 try:
2141:                     data = bytes.fromhex(value)
2142:                     need(os.write(fd, data) == len(data), "Short Steam metadata write.")
2143:                     os.fsync(fd)
2144:                 finally:
2145:                     os.close(fd)
2146:             self.append({"op": "external", "path": str(path), "id": identity(path),
2147:                          "parent_id": parent_id, "kind": kind, "value": value})
2148:             os.fsync(parent)
2149:         finally:
2150:             os.close(parent)
2151: 
2152:     def prepared(self):
2153:         rows = [row for row in self.rows if row["op"] == "prepared"]
2154:         need(len(rows) <= 1, "Ambiguous prepared client record.")
2155:         return rows[0] if rows else None
2156: 
2157:     def published(self):
2158:         prepared = self.prepared()
2159:         return prepared is not None and identity(self.root / CLIENT) == prepared["client_id"]
2160: 
2161:     def check_external(self, row):
2162:         path = Path(row["path"])
2163:         need(identity(path.parent) == row["parent_id"], "A client metadata parent changed; state was kept.")
2164:         current = describe(path)
2165:         need(current is not None and current["id"] == row["id"], "Client metadata ownership changed; state was kept.")
2166:         if row["kind"] == "file":
2167:             need(current.get("bytes") == row["value"], "Client metadata contents changed; state was kept.")
2168:         elif row["kind"] == "link":
2169:             need(current.get("target") == row["value"], "Client metadata link changed; state was kept.")
2170: 
2171:     def commit(self):
2172:         need(self.published(), "Published client identity changed.")
2173:         prepared = self.prepared()
2174:         need(self.tree(self.root / CLIENT) == prepared["tree"], "The published client changed before commit.")
2175:         for row in self.rows:
2176:             if row["op"] == "external":
2177:                 self.check_external(row)
2178:         for node in self.meta["nodes"]:
2179:             if node["before"] is not None:
2180:                 need(describe(Path(node["path"])) == node["before"], "Existing Steam metadata changed before commit.")
2181:         self.append({"op": "committed"})
2182:         self.cleanup(keep_client=True)
2183: 
2184:     def check_owned_state(self):
2185:         starts = [row["serial"] for row in self.rows if row["op"] == "transfer-start"]
2186:         stops = [row["serial"] for row in self.rows if row["op"] == "transfer-stopped"]
2187:         need(starts == stops, "Transfer quiescence is unestablished; client staging was kept for review.")
2188:         bindings(plan_global, self.root, True)
2189:         need(identity(self.work) == self.rows[0]["work_id"] and identity(self.work / "owner.jsonl") == self.rows[0]["journal_id"],
2190:              "Client stage ownership changed; state was kept.")
2191:         mounts_below(self.work)
2192:         stage = [row for row in self.rows if row["op"] == "stage"]
2193:         known = {row["path"]: row for row in stage}
2194:         actual = set()
2195:         for base, dirs, files in os.walk(self.work, followlinks=False):
2196:             for name in dirs + files:
2197:                 p = Path(base) / name
2198:                 relative = str(p.relative_to(self.work))
2199:                 if relative == "owner.jsonl":
2200:                     continue
2201:                 need(relative in known and identity(p) == known[relative]["id"], "Unknown or changed stage contents were kept.")
2202:                 need(p.lstat().st_dev == self.work.stat().st_dev and
2203:                      (not p.is_file() or p.lstat().st_nlink == 1), "A client stage crosses an ownership boundary.")
2204:                 actual.add(relative)
2205:         # Unrecorded creation after interruption is deliberately held. No
2206:         # ownership is inferred from a familiar filename or partial journal.
2207:         external = [row for row in self.rows if row["op"] == "external"]
2208:         owned = {row["path"] for row in external}
2209:         for row in self.rows:
2210:             if row["op"] == "intent":
2211:                 for node in row["nodes"]:
2212:                     if node["before"] is None and identity(Path(node["path"])) is not None:
2213:                         need(node["path"] in owned, "Unrecorded client metadata was kept for review.")
2214:         committed = any(row["op"] == "committed" for row in self.rows)
2215:         if not committed:
2216:             for row in external:
2217:                 if identity(Path(row["path"])) is not None or self.published():
2218:                     self.check_external(row)
2219:         return known, actual, external
2220: 
2221:     def cleanup(self, keep_client=False):
2222:         known, actual, external = self.check_owned_state()
2223:         if not keep_client:
2224:             need(not self.published(), "A published client must be verified before recovery.")
2225:             for row in external:
2226:                 if identity(Path(row["path"])) is not None:
2227:                     self.check_external(row)
2228:             for row in reversed(external):
2229:                 self.remove(Path(row["path"]), row["id"], row["kind"])
2230:         for relative in sorted(actual, key=lambda x: (len(Path(x).parts), x), reverse=True):
2231:             row = known[relative]
2232:             self.remove(self.work / relative, row["id"], row["kind"])
2233:         if self.journal is not None:
2234:             os.close(self.journal)
2235:             self.journal = None
2236:         self.remove(self.work / "owner.jsonl", self.rows[0]["journal_id"], "file")
2237:         self.remove(self.work, self.rows[0]["work_id"], "directory")
2238: 
2239:     def remove(self, path, expected, kind):
2240:         if identity(path) is None:
2241:             return  # An earlier interrupted cleanup already retired this owned node.
2242:         parent = directory(path.parent)
2243:         try:
2244:             s = os.stat(path.name, dir_fd=parent, follow_symlinks=False)
2245:             need([s.st_dev, s.st_ino, stat.S_IFMT(s.st_mode)] == expected, "Client cleanup identity changed.")
2246:             if kind == "directory":
2247:                 os.rmdir(path.name, dir_fd=parent)
2248:             else:
2249:                 os.unlink(path.name, dir_fd=parent)
2250:             os.fsync(parent)
2251:         finally:
2252:             os.close(parent)
2253: 
2254: 
2255: def install(root, tree, meta, dot):
2256:     bindings(plan_global, root, True)
2257:     consumers(root)
2258:     lock = open_file(root / LOCK, os.O_RDWR | os.O_CREAT, 0o600)
2259:     try:
2260:         s = os.fstat(lock)
2261:         need(stat.S_ISREG(s.st_mode) and s.st_nlink == 1, "The client lock is ambiguous.")
2262:         fcntl.flock(lock, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
2263:         work = root / WORK
2264:         if identity(work) is not None:
2265:             rows = load_journal(work, root)
2266:             committed = any(row["op"] == "committed" for row in rows)
2267:             need(committed or rows[0]["dot"] == dot, "Recovery needs the same profile-selected Steam support folder.")
2268:             tx = Transaction(root, dot, rows[0]["metadata"], rows)
2269:             if not committed:
2270:                 check_probe(tx.meta["dot"])
2271:             tx.journal = open_file(work / "owner.jsonl", os.O_WRONLY | os.O_APPEND)
2272:             try:
2273:                 if committed:
2274:                     tx.cleanup(keep_client=True)
2275:                     print("An existing Steam client was kept; completed setup scratch was retired.")
2276:                     return
2277:                 if tx.published():
2278:                     tx.commit()
2279:                     print("Steam client file setup recovered.")
2280:                     return
2281:                 tx.cleanup()
2282:             finally:
2283:                 if tx.journal is not None:
2284:                     os.close(tx.journal)
2285:             # Do not turn a recovered failure into a successful installed state.
2286:             # This same explicit invocation can start a fresh bounded download.
2287:             tree = inspect_tree(plan_global, root, False)
2288:             meta = metadata(root, dot, tree)
2289:         else:
2290:             need(inspect_tree(plan_global, root, False) == tree, "The Steam client changed during installation.")
2291:             same_metadata(meta, root, dot, tree)
2292:         if tree["kind"] == "existing":
2293:             print("Existing Steam client files were kept.")
2294:             return
2295:         need(tree["kind"] == "fresh" and identity(root / CLIENT) is None, "Steam client state is ambiguous.")
2296:         need(os.statvfs(root).f_bavail * os.statvfs(root).f_frsize >= MAX_ZIP + MAX_OUTPUT + RESERVE,
2297:              "There is not enough free space to prepare the Steam client.")
2298:         tx = Transaction(root, dot, meta)
2299:         try:
2300:             tx.begin()
2301:             size, _ = tx.fetch()
2302:             tx.extract(size)
2303:             bindings(plan_global, root, True)
2304:             consumers(root)
2305:             same_metadata(meta, root, dot, tree)
2306:             tx.prepare_metadata()
2307:             tx.append({"op": "publishing"})
2308:             rename_new(work / "payload" / CLIENT, root / CLIENT)
2309:             tx.commit()
2310:         except BaseException:
2311:             if tx.rows and tx.journal is not None:
2312:                 try:
2313:                     tx.append({"op": "failure"})
2314:                     if not tx.published():
2315:                         tx.cleanup()
2316:                 except BaseException as cleanup_error:
2317:                     print("Steam client recovery state was kept: " + str(cleanup_error), file=sys.stderr)
2318:             raise
2319:         finally:
2320:             if tx.journal is not None:
2321:                 os.close(tx.journal)
2322:         print("Steam client files are in place.")
2323:     finally:
2324:         os.close(lock)
2325: 
2326: 
2327: def interrupted(signum, frame):
2328:     raise RuntimeError("Steam client setup was interrupted (signal " + str(signum) + ").")
2329: 
2330: 
2331: def main():
2332:     global plan_global
2333:     mode, plan_text, dot, tree_text, meta_text = sys.argv[1:]
2334:     plan_global = json.loads(plan_text)
2335:     root = Path(plan_global.get("target", plan_global["root"])["path"])
2336:     for sig in (signal.SIGALRM, signal.SIGTERM, signal.SIGINT):
2337:         signal.signal(sig, interrupted)
2338:     signal.alarm(360 if mode == "install" else 30)
2339:     resource.setrlimit(resource.RLIMIT_AS, (512 * 1024**2, 512 * 1024**2))
2340:     if mode == "tree":
2341:         print(json.dumps(inspect_tree(plan_global, root), sort_keys=True))
2342:         return
2343:     tree = json.loads(tree_text)
2344:     if mode == "metadata":
2345:         need(inspect_tree(plan_global, root) == tree, "The Steam client changed while profiles loaded.")
2346:         if tree["kind"] == "recovery" and not tree["committed"]:
2347:             need(tree["dot"] == dot, "Recovery needs the same profile-selected Steam support folder.")
2348:             rows = load_journal(root / WORK, root)
2349:             print(json.dumps(rows[0]["metadata"], sort_keys=True))
2350:         else:
2351:             print(json.dumps(metadata(root, dot, {"kind": "existing"} if tree["kind"] == "recovery" else tree), sort_keys=True))
2352:         return
2353:     need(mode == "install", "Unsupported Steam client operation.")
2354:     need(LIBC.prctl(36, 1, 0, 0, 0) == 0, "Client transfer child supervision is unavailable.")
2355:     install(root, tree, json.loads(meta_text), dot)
2356: 
2357: 
2358: if __name__ == "__main__":
2359:     try:
2360:         main()
2361:     except (OSError, RuntimeError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError, IndexError, struct.error, zlib.error, subprocess.SubprocessError) as error:
2362:         print("Steam client setup stopped: " + str(error), file=sys.stderr)
2363:         sys.exit(1)
2364: PY_STEAM_CLIENT
2365: }
2366: 
2367: install_bundled_proton_files() {
2368:   log_info "Installing bundled Proton files..."
2369:   cp -f "/usr/share/steam/registry.vdf" "${STEAM_DOT}" || die "Failed to copy registry.vdf."
2370: }
2371: 
2372: install_proton_variant() {
2373:   local display_name="$1"
2374:   # The fifth argument is retained for caller compatibility, never for deletion:
2375:   # games can select an older named version even after a new version installs.
2376:   log_info "Checking and installing ${display_name}; keeping existing versions..."
2377:   if /usr/bin/python3 -I -S -B - "${STEAM}" "$2" "$3" "$4" "$5" <<'PY_PROTON_VARIANT'
2378: import ctypes
2379: import fcntl
2380: import gzip
2381: import json
2382: import lzma
2383: import os
2384: from pathlib import Path, PurePosixPath
2385: import posixpath
2386: import re
2387: import resource
2388: import shutil
2389: import signal
2390: import stat
2391: import subprocess
2392: import sys
2393: import tarfile
2394: from urllib.parse import urlsplit
2395: 
2396: RESERVE = 256 * 1024**2
2397: MAX_ARCHIVE = 2 * 1024**3
2398: MAX_EXPANDED = 16 * 1024**3
2399: MAX_MEMBERS = 250000
2400: WORK = ".proton-variant-stage"
2401: LOCK = ".proton-variant.lock"
2402: FAMILIES = {"proton-cachyos-*-arm64": (r"proton-cachyos-[A-Za-z0-9._+-]+-arm64", ".tar.xz"),
2403:             "GE-Proton*-aarch64": (r"GE-Proton[A-Za-z0-9._+-]+-aarch64", ".tar.gz")}
2404: 
2405: 
2406: def directory(path, device):
2407:     info = path.lstat()
2408:     if not stat.S_ISDIR(info.st_mode) or info.st_dev != device:
2409:         raise RuntimeError("Proton installation requires a real directory on the Steam filesystem.")
2410: 
2411: 
2412: def no_mounts(path):
2413:     for line in Path("/proc/self/mountinfo").read_text().splitlines():
2414:         field = line.split()[4]
2415:         mount = Path(re.sub(r"\\([0-7]{3})", lambda m: chr(int(m[1], 8)), field))
2416:         if mount == path or path in mount.parents:
2417:             raise RuntimeError("A mounted Proton staging or target path must be released first.")
2418: 
2419: 
2420: def capacity(root, needed):
2421:     if shutil.disk_usage(root).free < needed + RESERVE:
2422:         raise RuntimeError("Not enough space to install Proton while keeping existing versions.")
2423: 
2424: 
2425: def manifest(path):
2426:     if path.is_symlink() or not path.is_file() or not 0 < path.stat().st_size <= 65536:
2427:         raise RuntimeError("Missing or unsupported Proton manifest.")
2428:     raw = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
2429:     tokens, offset = [], 0
2430:     pattern = re.compile(r'\s+|//[^\n]*|"(?:\\.|[^"\\])*"|[{}]')
2431:     for match in pattern.finditer(raw):
2432:         if match.start() != offset:
2433:             raise RuntimeError("Malformed Proton manifest.")
2434:         offset = match.end()
2435:         token = match[0]
2436:         if not token.isspace() and not token.startswith("//"):
2437:             tokens.append(token)
2438:     if offset != len(raw):
2439:         raise RuntimeError("Malformed Proton manifest.")
2440:     index = 0
2441: 
2442:     def object_value(depth=0):
2443:         nonlocal index
2444:         if depth > 16:
2445:             raise RuntimeError("Proton manifest nesting exceeds the supported format.")
2446:         result = {}
2447:         while index < len(tokens) and tokens[index] != "}":
2448:             key = json.loads(tokens[index])
2449:             index += 1
2450:             if not isinstance(key, str) or key in result or index == len(tokens):
2451:                 raise RuntimeError("Malformed or duplicate Proton manifest field.")
2452:             if tokens[index] == "{":
2453:                 index += 1
2454:                 value = object_value(depth + 1)
2455:                 if index == len(tokens) or tokens[index] != "}":
2456:                     raise RuntimeError("Unclosed Proton manifest object.")
2457:                 index += 1
2458:             else:
2459:                 value = json.loads(tokens[index])
2460:                 index += 1
2461:             result[key] = value
2462:         return result
2463: 
2464:     result = object_value()
2465:     if index != len(tokens):
2466:         raise RuntimeError("Unexpected Proton manifest closing brace.")
2467:     return result
2468: 
2469: 
2470: def validate(target, device):
2471:     directory(target, device)
2472:     no_mounts(target)
2473:     tool = manifest(target / "toolmanifest.vdf")["manifest"]
2474:     if tool.get("version") != "2" or tool.get("commandline") != "/proton %verb%":
2475:         raise RuntimeError("Unsupported Proton command manifest.")
2476:     entries = manifest(target / "compatibilitytool.vdf")["compatibilitytools"]["compat_tools"]
2477:     if not isinstance(entries, dict) or len(entries) != 1:
2478:         raise RuntimeError("Expected one Proton compatibility tool.")
2479:     name, entry = next(iter(entries.items()))
2480:     if (not name or "##" in name or not isinstance(entry, dict) or entry.get("install_path") != "."
2481:             or entry.get("from_oslist") != "windows" or entry.get("to_oslist") != "linux"):
2482:         raise RuntimeError("Unsupported Proton compatibility manifest.")
2483:     for relative in ("proton", "filelock.py", "version", "files/bin-arm64/wine", "files/bin-arm64/wineserver"):
2484:         path = target / relative
2485:         resolved = path.resolve(strict=True)
2486:         if not resolved.is_relative_to(target) or not resolved.is_file() or resolved.stat().st_size == 0:
2487:             raise RuntimeError("Missing or escaping required Proton payload: " + relative)
2488:         if relative == "proton" or relative.startswith("files/"):
2489:             if not resolved.stat().st_mode & 0o111:
2490:                 raise RuntimeError("Required Proton launcher is not executable: " + relative)
2491:         if relative.startswith("files/"):
2492:             with resolved.open("rb") as stream:
2493:                 header = stream.read(20)
2494:             if (len(header) != 20 or header[:6] != b"\x7fELF\x02\x01" or header[18:20] != b"\xb7\x00"
2495:                     or header[16:18] not in (b"\x02\x00", b"\x03\x00")):
2496:                 raise RuntimeError("Required Proton payload is not ARM64 ELF: " + relative)
2497: 
2498: 
2499: def unpack(archive, stage, name, root, suffix):
2500:     # Validate every member and the compressed footer before extraction. Python's
2501:     # data filter then checks resolved link paths again during extraction.
2502:     members, seen, expanded = [], {}, 0
2503:     opener = lzma.open if suffix == ".tar.xz" else gzip.open
2504:     with opener(archive, "rb") as compressed:
2505:         class BoundedReader:
2506:             count = 0
2507: 
2508:             def read(self, size):
2509:                 data = compressed.read(size)
2510:                 self.count += len(data)
2511:                 if self.count > MAX_EXPANDED + MAX_MEMBERS * 8192:
2512:                     raise RuntimeError("Proton tar stream exceeds the size bound.")
2513:                 return data
2514: 
2515:         reader = BoundedReader()
2516:         with tarfile.open(fileobj=reader, mode="r|") as bundle:
2517:             for member in bundle:
2518:                 path = PurePosixPath(member.name)
2519:                 if (path.is_absolute() or ".." in path.parts or not path.parts or path.parts[0] != name
2520:                         or str(path) in seen or member.size < 0 or member.issparse()
2521:                         or not (member.isdir() or member.isfile() or member.issym() or member.islnk())):
2522:                     raise RuntimeError("Unsafe or unsupported Proton archive member.")
2523:                 if len(path.parts) == 1 and not member.isdir():
2524:                     raise RuntimeError("Proton archive root must be a directory.")
2525:                 if member.issym() or member.islnk():
2526:                     link = PurePosixPath(member.linkname)
2527:                     base = str(path.parent) if member.issym() else ""
2528:                     resolved = PurePosixPath(posixpath.normpath(posixpath.join(base, str(link))))
2529:                     if link.is_absolute() or not resolved.parts or resolved.parts[0] != name:
2530:                         raise RuntimeError("Proton archive link escapes its version directory.")
2531:                     if member.islnk() and seen.get(str(resolved)) != "file":
2532:                         raise RuntimeError("Proton hard link must name an earlier regular file.")
2533:                 seen[str(path)] = "file" if member.isfile() else "other"
2534:                 expanded += ((member.size + 4095) // 4096) * 4096 + 8192
2535:                 members.append(member)
2536:                 if expanded > MAX_EXPANDED or len(members) > MAX_MEMBERS:
2537:                     raise RuntimeError("Proton archive exceeds the extraction bound.")
2538:         while reader.read(1024 * 1024):
2539:             pass
2540:     capacity(root, expanded)
2541:     stage.mkdir()
2542:     with tarfile.open(archive, "r:*") as bundle:
2543:         bundle.extractall(stage, members=members, filter="data")
2544: 
2545: 
2546: def cleanup(work, lock_info, device):
2547:     if not os.path.lexists(work):
2548:         return
2549:     directory(work, device)
2550:     no_mounts(work)
2551:     children = {path.name for path in work.iterdir()}
2552:     if not children:  # Interrupted before the ownership link or after unlinking it.
2553:         work.rmdir()
2554:         return
2555:     owner = work / "owner"
2556:     info = owner.lstat()
2557:     if (not stat.S_ISREG(info.st_mode) or (info.st_dev, info.st_ino) != lock_info
2558:             or not children <= {"owner", "archive", "unpack"}):
2559:         raise RuntimeError("Unrecognized Proton staging contents; leaving them untouched.")
2560:     for name in ("archive", "unpack"):
2561:         path = work / name
2562:         if path.is_symlink() or path.is_file():
2563:             path.unlink()
2564:         elif path.exists():
2565:             directory(path, device)
2566:             shutil.rmtree(path)
2567:     owner.unlink()  # Ownership anchor is always removed last, for retry after a kill.
2568:     work.rmdir()
2569: 
2570: 
2571: def activate(candidate, target):
2572:     # Linux renameat2 refuses any existing target, including a concurrently created
2573:     # empty directory. Never replace a player-owned same-name installation.
2574:     libc = ctypes.CDLL(None, use_errno=True)
2575:     rename = libc.renameat2
2576:     rename.argtypes = [ctypes.c_int, ctypes.c_char_p, ctypes.c_int, ctypes.c_char_p, ctypes.c_uint]
2577:     rename.restype = ctypes.c_int
2578:     if rename(-100, os.fsencode(candidate), -100, os.fsencode(target), 1) != 0:
2579:         raise OSError(ctypes.get_errno(), "Could not activate staged Proton without replacing existing data")
2580: 
2581: 
2582: def install(root, url, tar_name, name, family):
2583:     pattern, suffix = FAMILIES[family]
2584:     if not re.fullmatch(pattern, name) or tar_name != name + suffix:
2585:         raise RuntimeError("Unsupported Proton family or archive name.")
2586:     parsed = urlsplit(url)
2587:     if parsed.scheme != "https" or not parsed.netloc or parsed.username or parsed.password:
2588:         raise RuntimeError("Proton download requires an HTTPS release URL.")
2589:     root = Path(root).resolve(strict=True)
2590:     if root == Path("/"):
2591:         raise RuntimeError("Refusing the filesystem root as a Steam directory.")
2592:     device = root.stat().st_dev
2593:     directory(root, device)
2594:     fd = os.open(root / LOCK, os.O_CREAT | os.O_RDWR | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_NONBLOCK, 0o600)
2595:     try:
2596:         info = os.fstat(fd)
2597:         if not stat.S_ISREG(info.st_mode) or info.st_size:
2598:             raise RuntimeError("Unsupported Proton installer lock.")
2599:         fcntl.flock(fd, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
2600:         work = root / WORK
2601:         lock_info = (info.st_dev, info.st_ino)
2602:         cleanup(work, lock_info, device)
2603:         dest = root / "compatibilitytools.d"
2604:         if not os.path.lexists(dest):
2605:             dest.mkdir()
2606:         directory(dest, device)
2607:         no_mounts(dest)
2608:         target = dest / name
2609:         if os.path.lexists(target):
2610:             try:
2611:                 validate(target, device)
2612:             except Exception as error:
2613:                 raise RuntimeError("Existing Proton target is incomplete or unsupported; kept untouched: "
2614:                                    + name + ". Resolve that version separately before retrying.") from error
2615:             print("Requested Proton version passes payload checks; keeping it unchanged.")
2616:             return
2617:         capacity(root, MAX_ARCHIVE)
2618:         work.mkdir(mode=0o700)
2619:         try:
2620:             os.link(root / LOCK, work / "owner", follow_symlinks=False)
2621:             archive = work / "archive"
2622:             parent = os.getpid()
2623: 
2624:             def child_setup():
2625:                 libc = ctypes.CDLL(None, use_errno=True)
2626:                 if libc.prctl(1, signal.SIGKILL, 0, 0, 0) != 0 or os.getppid() != parent:
2627:                     os._exit(125)
2628:                 resource.setrlimit(resource.RLIMIT_FSIZE, (MAX_ARCHIVE, MAX_ARCHIVE))
2629: 
2630:             subprocess.run(["wget", "-t", "3", "-T", "30", "-O", str(archive), url],
2631:                            check=True, timeout=900, preexec_fn=child_setup)
2632:             if not 0 < archive.stat().st_size <= MAX_ARCHIVE:
2633:                 raise RuntimeError("Proton download is empty or exceeds the size bound.")
2634:             unpack(archive, work / "unpack", name, root, suffix)
2635:             candidate = work / "unpack" / name
2636:             validate(candidate, device)
2637:             capacity(root, 0)
2638:             activate(candidate, target)
2639:             print("Proton installed; every existing version has been kept.")
2640:         finally:
2641:             cleanup(work, lock_info, device)
2642:     finally:
2643:         os.close(fd)  # Keep the lock inode: never unlink a lock another caller may hold.
2644: 
2645: 
2646: def interrupted(signum, frame):
2647:     raise InterruptedError("Proton installation interrupted; existing versions are kept.")
2648: 
2649: 
2650: def main():
2651:     for signum in (signal.SIGINT, signal.SIGTERM, signal.SIGHUP, signal.SIGALRM):
2652:         signal.signal(signum, interrupted)
2653:     signal.alarm(1800)
2654:     try:
2655:         install(*sys.argv[1:])
2656:     except Exception as error:
2657:         print("Proton installation stopped: " + str(error), file=sys.stderr)
2658:         return 1
2659:     finally:
2660:         signal.alarm(0)
2661:     return 0
2662: 
2663: 
2664: if __name__ == "__main__":
2665:     sys.exit(main())
2666: PY_PROTON_VARIANT
2667:   then
2668:     return 0

2670:     die "Could not install ${display_name}. Existing Proton versions have been kept."
2671:   fi
2672: }

2692: run_steam_first_launch() {
2693:   log_info "Running Steam first launch routine..."
2694:   echo 0 > /proc/sys/fs/binfmt_misc/x86 || true
2695:   echo 0 > /proc/sys/fs/binfmt_misc/box32 || true
2696:   echo 0 > /proc/sys/fs/binfmt_misc/box64 || true
2697: 
2698:   if [ "${DEVICE_HAS_DUAL_SCREEN}" = "true" ]; then
2699:     swaymsg 'seat seat1 fallback true' || log_info "Swaymsg dual screen setup failed, ignoring."
2700:   fi
2701: 
2702:   # Allow Steam launch commands to fail cleanly if needed, though they shouldn't
2703:   LD_LIBRARY_PATH="${STEAM}/lib/aarch64-linux-gnu/" "${CLIENT_DIR}/steam" -deckard -exitsteam || log_info "Native Steam execution exited with an error."
2704: 
2705:   if [ "${DEVICE_HAS_DUAL_SCREEN}" = "true" ]; then
2706:     swaymsg 'seat seat1 fallback false' || log_info "Swaymsg dual screen teardown failed, ignoring."
2707:   fi
2708: 
2709:   systemctl restart systemd-binfmt || die "Failed to restart systemd-binfmt."
2710: }
2711: 
2712: # --- Execution ---
2713: # Freeze the checked inputs before profile scripts can run or change the roots.
2714: STEAM_ROOT_PLAN="$(steam_root_layout inspect)" || die "Steam storage is not ready. Nothing was installed."
2715: readonly STEAM STEAM_GAMES_ROOT STEAM_ROOT_PLAN
2716: STEAM_CLIENT_PLAN="$(steam_client_state tree)" || die "Steam client files could not be checked. Nothing was changed."
2717: readonly STEAM_CLIENT_PLAN
2718: source /etc/profile
2719: STEAM_DOT="${STEAM_DOT:-/storage/.steam}"
2720: APPLICATIONS="${APPLICATIONS:-/storage/.local/share/applications}"
2721: FEX_DATA="/storage/.local/share/fex-emu"
2722: FEX_ARCH_ROOT="${FEX_DATA}/RootFS/ArchLinux"
2723: FEX_ARCH_USR_LIB="${FEX_ARCH_ROOT}/usr/lib"
2724: RUNTIME_DIR="${STEAM}/steam-runtime-steamrt-arm64"
2725: CLIENT_DIR="${STEAM}/steamrtarm64"
2726: RUNTIME_TAR_BASE="https://repo.steampowered.com/steamrt3c/images"
2727: RUNTIME_TAR_VERSION_URL="${RUNTIME_TAR_BASE}/latest-public-stable.txt"
2728: STEAM_MANIFEST_URL="https://client-update.fastly.steamstatic.com/steam_client_publicbeta_linuxarm64"
2729: STEAM_CDN="https://client-update.steamstatic.com"
2730: PROTON_CACHYOS_RELEASE_API="https://api.github.com/repos/CachyOS/proton-cachyos/releases/latest"
2731: PROTON_GE_RELEASE_API="https://api.github.com/repos/GloriousEggroll/proton-ge-custom/releases/latest"
2732: unset MESA_LOADER_DRIVER_OVERRIDE
2733: 
2734: steam_root_layout verify || die "Steam storage changed. Installation stopped."
2735: # Profiles may already have written. Metadata refusal preserves their selected
2736: # path and does not claim rollback of profile effects or other transactions.
2737: STEAM_CLIENT_METADATA_PLAN="$(steam_client_state metadata)" || die "Steam support folders could not be checked. Client files were kept."
2738: readonly STEAM_DOT STEAM_CLIENT_METADATA_PLAN
2739: set_kill set "-9 steam FEX"
2740: log_info "Starting Steam Installation Process..."
2741: 
2742: ensure_fex_rootfs
2743: link_steam_library
2744: ensure_steam_desktop_stub
2745: install_steam_runtime_arm64
2746: install_steam_client_arm64
2747: install_bundled_proton_files
2748: fetch_proton_latest_release "${PROTON_CACHYOS_RELEASE_API}" "arm64" "cachyos-" "PROTON_CACHYOS" "Proton-CachyOS"
2749: install_proton_cachyos
2750: fetch_proton_latest_release "${PROTON_GE_RELEASE_API}" "aarch64" "" "PROTON_GE" "Proton-GE"

2755: log_success "Steam setup finished. Start Steam to continue."

1: #!/usr/bin/python3
2: # SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
3: # Copyright (C) 2026 ROCKNIX (https://github.com/ROCKNIX)
4: """Prepare Dolphin's known save links without converting retained real data."""
5: import argparse
6: import ctypes
7: import fcntl
8: import os
9: from pathlib import Path
10: import re
11: import shutil
12: import signal
13: import stat
14: import sys
15: import tempfile
16: 
17: REGIONS = ("EUR", "JAP", "USA")
18: MAX_COPY_FILES = 4096
19: MAX_COPY_BYTES = 64 * 1024 * 1024
20: 
21: 
22: # The first line is a fixed ownership discriminator consumed by runemu, not UI.
23: ERROR_OWNER = "DOLPHIN DID NOT START"
24: MESSAGES_FR = {
25:     'Existing Dolphin data needs attention. Back up these locations, then use the file manager to choose and arrange the saved data before trying again. No saved data was removed.':
26:         'Les données Dolphin existantes nécessitent votre attention. Sauvegardez ces emplacements, puis utilisez le gestionnaire de fichiers pour choisir et organiser les données à conserver avant de réessayer. Aucune donnée sauvegardée n’a été supprimée.',
27:     'Dolphin storage path is outside its expected root.':
28:         'L’emplacement de stockage Dolphin se trouve hors du dossier prévu.',
29:     'Dolphin save storage is not writable. Check the game storage and folder permissions, then try again.':
30:         'Impossible d’écrire dans le stockage des sauvegardes Dolphin. Vérifiez le stockage des jeux et les permissions des dossiers, puis réessayez.',

32:         'Le stockage des jeux n’est pas prêt. Reconnectez-le et réessayez. Aucune donnée sauvegardée n’a été supprimée.',
