34:         'Un emplacement de sauvegarde Dolphin possède un montage distinct. Vérifiez l’organisation du stockage avant de réessayer.',

36:         'Les ressources GameCube conservées dépassent la limite de préparation. Organisez-les dans le gestionnaire de fichiers avant de réessayer.',
37:     'Dolphin configuration is missing. Check the installed defaults before trying again.':
38:         'La configuration Dolphin est absente. Vérifiez la configuration fournie avec le système avant de réessayer.',
39:     'Dolphin save preparation was interrupted. Saved data was kept. Try again.':
40:         'La préparation des sauvegardes Dolphin a été interrompue. Les données sauvegardées ont été conservées. Réessayez.',

42:         'La préparation des sauvegardes Dolphin est indisponible. Réessayez après la fin de l’autre lancement.',
43:     'Dolphin save preparation is already running. Wait for it to finish, then try again.':
44:         'La préparation des sauvegardes Dolphin est déjà en cours. Attendez sa fin, puis réessayez.',
45:     'Dolphin could not prepare save storage. Check the storage connection and permissions, then try again. Saved data was kept.':
46:         'Dolphin n’a pas pu préparer le stockage des sauvegardes. Vérifiez la connexion du stockage et les permissions, puis réessayez. Les données sauvegardées ont été conservées.',

49: 
50: def message_language():
51:     # ES exports LANGUAGE; use the same precedence as the cloud companion page.
52:     value = next((os.environ[key] for key in ("LANGUAGE", "LC_ALL", "LC_MESSAGES", "LANG")
53:                   if os.environ.get(key)), "en")
54:     primary = value.split(":", 1)[0].replace("-", "_").split("_", 1)[0].split(".", 1)[0].split("@", 1)[0].lower()
55:     return "fr" if primary == "fr" else "en"
56: 
57: 
58: def visible_path(path):
59:     # The native message wraps only at whitespace. Keep this known long card
60:     # path intact except for a display line break at its final slash.
61:     prefix = "/storage/.config/dolphin-emu/GC/"
62:     leaf = path.removeprefix(prefix)
63:     if path.startswith(prefix) and re.fullmatch(r"MemoryCard[AB]\.(EUR|JAP|USA)\.raw", leaf):
64:         return prefix + "\n" + leaf
65:     return path
66: 
67: 
68: def visible_error(message):
69:     language = message_language()
70:     title = "DOLPHIN N’A PAS DÉMARRÉ" if language == "fr" else "DOLPHIN DID NOT START"
71:     # Translate only the known sentence, never player data paths below it.
72:     sentence, separator, paths = message.partition("\n")
73:     if language == "fr":
74:         sentence = MESSAGES_FR.get(sentence, sentence)
75:     paths = "\n".join(visible_path(path) for path in paths.split("\n"))
76:     return title + "\n\n" + sentence + separator + paths + "\n"
77: 
78: 
79: class Refused(Exception):
80:     pass
81: 
82: 
83: def kind(path):
84:     try:
85:         mode = path.lstat().st_mode
86:     except FileNotFoundError:
87:         return "absent"
88:     if stat.S_ISLNK(mode):
89:         return "link"
90:     if stat.S_ISDIR(mode):
91:         return "dir"
92:     if stat.S_ISREG(mode):
93:         return "file"
94:     return "other"
95: 
96: 
97: def link_target(path):
98:     return Path(os.path.abspath(path.parent / os.readlink(path)))
99: 
100: 
101: def reject(path, destination=None):
102:     detail = "\n" + str(path)
103:     if destination is not None:
104:         detail += "\n" + str(destination)
105:     raise Refused("Existing Dolphin data needs attention. Back up these locations, then use the file manager "
106:                   "to choose and arrange the saved data before trying again. No saved data was removed." + detail)
107: 
108: 
109: def directories(path, boundary):
110:     # Missing descendants are fine; existing parents must not redirect traversal.
111:     if not path.is_relative_to(boundary):
112:         raise Refused("Dolphin storage path is outside its expected root.")
113:     for item in (boundary, *reversed(path.relative_to(boundary).parents)):
114:         if item == boundary:
115:             current = boundary
116:         else:
117:             current = boundary / item
118:         if kind(current) not in ("dir", "absent"):
119:             reject(current)
120:     if kind(path) not in ("dir", "absent"):
121:         reject(path)
122: 
123: 
124: def mounts():
125:     result = set()
126:     for line in Path("/proc/self/mountinfo").read_text().splitlines():
127:         field = line.split()[4]
128:         result.add(Path(re.sub(r"\\([0-7]{3})", lambda m: chr(int(m[1], 8)), field)))
129:     return result
130: 
131: 
132: def writable(path):
133:     current = path
134:     while kind(current) == "absent":
135:         current = current.parent
136:     # Mode check also catches a read-only player directory when the launcher is root.
137:     mode = current.stat().st_mode
138:     if not mode & 0o222 or not os.access(current, os.W_OK):
139:         raise Refused("Dolphin save storage is not writable. Check the game storage and folder permissions, then try again.\n" + str(path))
140: 
141: 
142: def plan(storage, system):
143:     conf = storage / ".config/dolphin-emu"
144:     roms = storage / "roms"
145:     shared = storage / ".local/share/dolphin-emu"
146:     mounted = mounts()
147:     if kind(storage) != "dir" or kind(roms) != "dir" or roms not in mounted:
148:         raise Refused("Game storage is not ready. Reconnect it and try again. No saved data was removed.")
149:     for path in (conf, roms / "savestates", roms / "bios/GC", shared.parent):
150:         directories(path, storage)
151:     # Do not rewrite a save path hidden behind a nested/bind mount.
152:     for mount in mounted:
153:         if any(mount == path or path in mount.parents for path in (conf, shared, roms / "savestates", roms / "bios")):
154:             raise Refused("A Dolphin save path is separately mounted. Check the storage layout before trying again.\n" + str(mount))
155:     directories(conf / "GC", storage)
156:     for temporary in (roms / "bios/.GC.dolphin-new", roms / "bios/GC/.Triforce.dolphin-new"):
157:         if kind(temporary) != "absent":
158:             reject(temporary)
159:     links = [(conf / "StateSaves", roms / "savestates" / system,
160:               (roms / "savestates/gamecube", roms / "savestates/wii")),
161:              (shared, conf, (conf,))]
162:     dirs = [roms / "savestates" / system, roms / "bios/GC", conf / "GC", shared.parent]
163:     for name in ("gamecube", "wii"):
164:         directories(roms / "savestates" / name, storage)
165:     for region in REGIONS:
166:         canonical = roms / "bios/GC" / region
167:         directories(canonical, storage)
168:         dirs.append(canonical)
169:         links.append((conf / "GC" / region, canonical, (canonical,)))
170:         for slot in ("A", "B"):
171:             card = "MemoryCard" + slot + "." + region + ".raw"
172:             canonical = roms / "bios/GC" / card
173:             if kind(canonical) not in ("absent", "file"):
174:                 reject(canonical)
175:             if kind(canonical) == "file":
176:                 writable(canonical)
177:             links.append((conf / "GC" / card, canonical, (canonical,)))
178:     directories(conf / "GC", storage)
179:     directories(roms / "bios/GC/Triforce", storage)
180:     for source, destination, allowed in links:
181:         value = kind(source)
182:         if value == "link":
183:             if link_target(source) not in allowed:
184:                 reject(source, destination)
185:         elif value != "absent":
186:             # A real StateSaves tree has no reliable GC/Wii ownership tag. Even
187:             # equal-looking region/card copies need an explicit retention choice.
188:             reject(source, destination)
189:     pending = conf / ".dolphin-state-link"
190:     if kind(pending) != "absent" and (kind(pending) != "link" or link_target(pending) not in links[0][2]):
191:         reject(pending, conf / "StateSaves")
192:     for path in (*dirs, conf, shared.parent):
193:         writable(path)
194:     return conf, roms, dirs, links, pending
195: 
196: 
197: def copy_new_tree(source, target, skip=()):
198:     # Historical GC/Triforce seeding is copy-only and only to an absent target.
199:     # A killed copy leaves one reserved stage and refuses retry until reviewed.
200:     # Canonical data is never replaced, and the original config tree stays intact.
201:     # Shipped resources are real files; refuse retained redirects before copying.
202:     if kind(source) == "absent":
203:         target.mkdir()
204:         return
205:     if kind(source) != "dir":
206:         reject(source, target)
207:     count = total = 0
208:     def unreadable(error):
209:         raise error
210: 
211:     for base, dirs, files in os.walk(source, followlinks=False, onerror=unreadable):
212:         if Path(base) == source:
213:             dirs[:] = [name for name in dirs if name not in skip]
214:             files = [name for name in files if name not in skip]
215:         for name in dirs + files:
216:             path = Path(base) / name
217:             if kind(path) not in ("dir", "file"):
218:                 reject(path, target)
219:             count += 1
220:             if kind(path) == "file":
221:                 total += path.lstat().st_size
222:             if count > MAX_COPY_FILES or total > MAX_COPY_BYTES:
223:                 raise Refused("Dolphin's retained GC resources exceed the preparation limit. Arrange them in the file manager before trying again.")
224:     temporary = target.parent / ("." + target.name + ".dolphin-new")
225:     temporary.mkdir(mode=0o700)
226:     owned = temporary.lstat()
227:     try:
228:         shutil.copytree(source, temporary, symlinks=True, dirs_exist_ok=True,
229:                         ignore=lambda base, names: set(skip) if Path(base) == source else set())
230:         rename(temporary, target, 1)  # RENAME_NOREPLACE: canonical data wins a race.
231:     finally:
232:         if kind(temporary) != "absent":
233:             current = temporary.lstat()
234:             if (current.st_dev, current.st_ino) != (owned.st_dev, owned.st_ino) or kind(temporary) != "dir":
235:                 reject(temporary)
236:             if any(m == temporary or temporary in m.parents for m in mounts()):
237:                 reject(temporary)
238:             shutil.rmtree(temporary)  # Only this invocation's private, unactivated copy.
239: 
240: 
241: def rename(first, second, flags):
242:     libc = ctypes.CDLL(None, use_errno=True)
243:     operation = libc.renameat2
244:     operation.argtypes = [ctypes.c_int, ctypes.c_char_p, ctypes.c_int, ctypes.c_char_p, ctypes.c_uint]
245:     operation.restype = ctypes.c_int
246:     if operation(-100, os.fsencode(first), -100, os.fsencode(second), flags):
247:         raise OSError(ctypes.get_errno(), "Could not switch Dolphin's save-state link")
248: 
249: 
250: def apply(storage, system):
251:     conf, roms, dirs, links, pending = plan(storage, system)
252:     if kind(conf) != "dir":
253:         raise Refused("Dolphin configuration is missing. Check the installed defaults before trying again.")
254:     # Seed only an absent GC root, retaining the existing launchers' resource copy.
255:     (roms / "bios").mkdir(exist_ok=True)
256:     if kind(roms / "bios/GC") == "absent":
257:         managed = (*REGIONS, *("MemoryCard" + slot + "." + region + ".raw"
258:                               for region in REGIONS for slot in ("A", "B")))
259:         copy_new_tree(conf / "GC", roms / "bios/GC", skip=managed)
260:     if system == "gamecube" and kind(roms / "bios/GC/Triforce") == "absent":
261:         copy_new_tree(conf / "GC/Triforce", roms / "bios/GC/Triforce")
262:     for directory in dirs:
263:         directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
264:     if kind(pending) == "link":
265:         if link_target(pending) not in links[0][2]:
266:             reject(pending, conf / "StateSaves")
267:         pending.unlink()  # Only a recognized leftover symlink; never its target.
268:     for source, destination, allowed in links:
269:         value = kind(source)
270:         if value == "absent":
271:             source.symlink_to(destination)  # Exclusive creation; never overwrites a racing entry.
272:         elif value == "link" and link_target(source) == destination:
273:             continue
274:         elif source == conf / "StateSaves" and value == "link" and link_target(source) in allowed:
275:             previous = source.lstat()
276:             pending.symlink_to(destination)
277:             rename(pending, source, 2)  # RENAME_EXCHANGE keeps the displaced object.
278:             moved = pending.lstat()
279:             if (moved.st_dev, moved.st_ino) != (previous.st_dev, previous.st_ino) or kind(pending) != "link":
280:                 # Keep the unexpected object; do not delete data moved by a race.
281:                 # Refusal identifies the reserved path for explicit recovery.
282:                 reject(pending, source)
283:             pending.unlink()
284:         else:
285:             reject(source, destination)
286: 
287: 
288: def write_error(path, message):
289:     # ES already consumes this native launch-error file on exit 250.
290:     fd, temporary = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix=".dolphin-launch-error-", dir=path.parent)
291:     try:
292:         with os.fdopen(fd, "w", encoding="utf-8", errors="backslashreplace") as stream:
293:             stream.write(ERROR_OWNER + "\n" + visible_error(message))
294:         os.replace(temporary, path)
295:     finally:
296:         if os.path.lexists(temporary):
297:             os.unlink(temporary)
298: 
299: 
300: def interrupted(signum, frame):
301:     raise Refused("Dolphin save preparation was interrupted. Saved data was kept. Try again.")
302: 
303: 
304: def main():
305:     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
306:     parser.add_argument("phase", choices=("check", "prepare"))
307:     parser.add_argument("system", choices=("gamecube", "wii"))
308:     parser.add_argument("--storage", type=Path, default=Path("/storage"))
309:     parser.add_argument("--lock", type=Path, default=Path("/run/dolphin-save-paths.lock"))
310:     parser.add_argument("--error-file", type=Path, default=Path("/tmp/launch_error.log"))
311:     args = parser.parse_args()
312:     try:
313:         for sig in (signal.SIGINT, signal.SIGTERM, signal.SIGHUP, signal.SIGALRM):
314:             signal.signal(sig, interrupted)
315:         signal.alarm(30)
316:         storage = Path(os.path.abspath(args.storage))
317:         fd = os.open(args.lock, os.O_CREAT | os.O_RDWR | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_NONBLOCK, 0o600)
318:         try:
319:             if not stat.S_ISREG(os.fstat(fd).st_mode):
320:                 raise Refused("Dolphin save preparation lock is unavailable. Try again after the other launch finishes.")
321:             try:
322:                 fcntl.flock(fd, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
323:             except BlockingIOError:
324:                 raise Refused("Dolphin save preparation is already running. Wait for it to finish, then try again.") from None
325:             if args.phase == "check":
326:                 plan(storage, args.system)
327:             else:
328:                 apply(storage, args.system)
329:         finally:
330:             os.close(fd)
331:         return 0
332:     except Exception as error:
333:         message = str(error) if isinstance(error, Refused) else "Dolphin could not prepare save storage. Check the storage connection and permissions, then try again. Saved data was kept."
334:         print(message, file=sys.stderr)
335:         print("Dolphin preparation detail: " + str(error), file=sys.stderr)
336:         try:
337:             write_error(args.error_file, message)
338:         except OSError:
339:             print("Could not write the native launch error message.", file=sys.stderr)
340:         return 250
341:     finally:
342:         signal.alarm(0)
343: 
344: 
345: if __name__ == "__main__":
346:     sys.exit(main())
~~~~

### projects/ROCKNIX/packages/emulators/standalone/dolphin-sa/scripts/cheevos_dolphin.sh

Kind: frozen source. Original SHA-256: `01473794711dafd9607e1b6894ec482e34820807b81e1c45c352d5da9768c28a`. Original bytes: 5106. Ranges: 1-148. Line prefixes below are original file lines. Host path substitutions are declared in the packet manifest.

~~~~text
1: #! /bin/bash
2: 
3: # SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
4: # Copyright (C) 2024-present ROCKNIX (https://github.com/ROCKNIX)
5: 
6: . /etc/profile
7: 
8: LOG_FILE="/var/log/cheevos.log"
9: 
10: # Extract username, password, token, if enabled, and hardcore mode from system.cfg
11: username=$(get_setting "global.retroachievements.username")
12: password=$(get_setting "global.retroachievements.password")
13: token=$(get_setting "global.retroachievements.token")
14: enabled=$(get_setting "global.retroachievements")
15: hardcore=$(get_setting "global.retroachievements.hardcore")
16: encore=$(get_setting "global.retroachievements.encore")
17: unofficial=$(get_setting "global.retroachievements.unofficial")
18: [ -z "${unofficial}" ] && unofficial=$(get_setting "global.retroachievements.testunofficial")  # the spelling before 2026-09; EmulationStation writes .unofficial
19: hosturl=$(get_setting "global.retroachievements.customhost")
20: 
21: # Publish the generated session as one file so disabling or signing out cannot
22: # reuse an earlier active session. Saved ES account settings are read-only here.
23: INI_FILE="/storage/.config/dolphin-emu/RetroAchievements.ini"
24: temporary=""
25: trap '[ -z "${temporary}" ] || rm -f -- "${temporary}"' EXIT
26: 
27: settings_failed() {
28:   echo "Dolphin achievements settings could not be written; refusing launch." >> "${LOG_FILE}"
29:   local language="${LANGUAGE:-${LC_ALL:-${LC_MESSAGES:-${LANG:-en}}}}"
30:   language="${language,,}"
31:   local title="DOLPHIN DID NOT START"
32:   local message="Achievements settings could not be updated. Check the game storage and folder permissions, then try again."
33:   case "${language}" in
34:     fr|fr_*|fr-*|fr.*|fr@*|fr:*)
35:       title="DOLPHIN N’A PAS DÉMARRÉ"
36:       message="Les paramètres des succès n’ont pas pu être mis à jour. Vérifiez le stockage des jeux et les permissions des dossiers, puis réessayez."
37:       ;;
38:   esac
39:   printf 'DOLPHIN DID NOT START\n%s\n\n%s\n' "${title}" "${message}" > /tmp/launch_error.log
40:   exit 250
41: }
42: 
43: publish_settings() {
44:   temporary=$(umask 077; mktemp "${INI_FILE}.XXXXXX") || return 1
45:   if ! cat > "${temporary}" <<EOF
46: [Achievements]
47: DiscordPresenceEnabled = False
48: Enabled = ${enabled}
49: EncoreEnabled = ${encore}
50: HardcoreEnabled = ${hardcore}
51: ProgressEnabled = False
52: SpectatorEnabled = False
53: UnofficialEnabled = ${unofficial}
54: Username = ${username}
55: ApiToken = ${token}
56: HostUrl = ${hosturl}
57: EOF
58:   then
59:     return 1
60:   fi
61:   # -T refuses a destination directory instead of moving a token file into it.
62:   mv -fT -- "${temporary}" "${INI_FILE}" || return 1
63:   temporary=""
64: }
65: 
66: publish_disabled() {
67:   enabled="False"
68:   encore="False"
69:   hardcore="False"
70:   unofficial="False"
71:   username=""
72:   token=""
73:   hosturl=""
74:   publish_settings || settings_failed
75: }
76: 
77: # Check if RetroAchievements are enabled in Emulation Station
78: if [ "${enabled}" = 1 ]; then
79:   enabled="True"
80: else
81:   echo "RetroAchievements are not enabled, please turn them on in Emulation Station." > ${LOG_FILE}
82:   publish_disabled
83:   exit 1
84: fi
85: 
86: # Check if api token is present in system.cfg
87: if [ -z "${token}" ]; then
88:     echo "RetroAchievements token is empty, please log in with your RetroAchievements credentials in Emulation Station." > ${LOG_FILE}
89:     publish_disabled
90:     exit 1
91: fi
92: 
93: # Set hardcore mode
94: hardcore_raw="${hardcore}"
95: if [ "${hardcore}" = 1 ]; then
96:   hardcore="True"
97: else
98:   hardcore="False"
99: fi
100: 
101: # Set encore mode
102: if [ "${encore}" = 1 ]; then
103:   encore="True"
104: else
105:   encore="False"
106: fi
107: 
108: # Set unofficial mode
109: if [ "${unofficial}" = 1 ]; then
110:   unofficial="True"
111: else
112:   unofficial="False"
113: fi
114: 
115: # The offline toggle routes only a known casual session to a listening proxy
116: # (#584, D-RA-002). Keep the configured custom host for every fallback; never
117: # change saved settings or credentials here. An empty/unknown hardcore read
118: # is not permission to use a service that cannot queue hardcore awards.
119: # Dolphin2606 passes HostUrl to rc_client_set_host; its bundled rcheevos adds
120: # http:// to a host without a scheme. The bare host below therefore uses HTTP.
121: # Match PPSSPP's read-only IPv4-capable listener check (0 listening, 1 absent,
122: # 2 unreadable). A listening socket is not proof of proxy identity or readiness.
123: proxy_listening() {
124:   local proc="${RAOFFLINEPROXY_PROC_NET:-/proc/net}" f readable=0 sl addr rem st rest
125:   for f in "${proc}/tcp" "${proc}/tcp6"; do
126:     [ -r "${f}" ] || continue
127:     readable=1
128:     while read -r sl addr rem st rest; do
129:       [ "${st}" = "0A" ] || continue
130:       case "${addr}" in
131:         0100007F:1F90|00000000:1F90|0000000000000000FFFF00000100007F:1F90) return 0 ;;
132:       esac
133:     done < "${f}"
134:   done
135:   [ "${readable}" -eq 1 ] && return 1
136:   return 2
137: }
138: 
139: if [ "$(get_setting "global.retroachievements.offlineproxy")" = 1 ] && [ "${hardcore_raw}" = "0" ]; then
140:   proxy_listening
141:   case $? in
142:     0) hosturl="127.0.0.1:8080" ;;
143:     1) echo "Offline RetroAchievements is on but nothing is listening on 127.0.0.1:8080; keeping the configured achievements host." >> "${LOG_FILE}" ;;
144:     *) echo "Offline RetroAchievements is on but the listener tables could not be read; keeping the configured achievements host." >> "${LOG_FILE}" ;;
145:   esac
146: fi
147: 
148: publish_settings || settings_failed
~~~~

1: #!/bin/bash
2: 
3: # SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
4: # Copyright (C) 2024-present ROCKNIX (https://github.com/ROCKNIX)
5: 
6: . /etc/profile
7: 
8: # Detect version of Dolphin
9: DOLPHIN_CORE=$(echo "${3}"| sed "s#^/.*/##")
10: if [ "${DOLPHIN_CORE}" = 'dolphin-qt-gc' ]; then
11:   DOLPHIN_CORE="dolphin-emu"
12: else
13:   DOLPHIN_CORE="dolphin-emu-nogui"
14: fi
15: 
16: set_kill set "-9 ${DOLPHIN_CORE}"
17: 
18: # control-gen supplies param_device and the SDL controller database
19: control-gen_init.sh
20: source /storage/.config/gptokeyb/control.ini
21: get_controls
22: 
23: # Conf files vars
24: CONF_DIR="/storage/.config/dolphin-emu"
25: DOLPHIN_INI="Dolphin.ini"
26: GFX_INI="GFX.ini"
27: CONTROLLER_INI="GCPadNew.ini"
28: 
29: # Refuse ambiguous retained save paths before any configuration preparation.
30: if ! /usr/bin/python3 -I -S -B /usr/bin/dolphin-save-paths check gamecube; then
31:   exit 250
32: fi
33: 
34: # Dolphin writes its shader and pipeline caches here but will not create the
35: # directory itself, so an orderly shutdown had nothing to flush into.
36: mkdir -p "/storage/.cache/dolphin-emu"
37: 
38: # Check if dolphin-emu exists in .config
39: if [ ! -d "${CONF_DIR}" ]; then
40:         cp -r "/usr/config/dolphin-emu" "/storage/.config/"
41: fi
42: 
43: # Check if Hotkeys.ini exists
44: if [ "${DOLPHIN_CORE}" = 'dolphin-emu' ]; then
45:   if [ ! -f "${CONF_DIR}/Hotkeys.ini" ]; then
46:         cp -r "/usr/config/dolphin-emu/Hotkeys.ini" "${CONF_DIR}/"
47:   fi
48: fi
49: 
50: # Check if GC controller dir exists in .config/dolphin-emu/GamecubeControllerProfiles
51: if [ ! -d "${CONF_DIR}/GamecubeControllerProfiles" ]; then
52:         cp -r "/usr/config/dolphin-emu/GamecubeControllerProfiles" "${CONF_DIR}/"
53: fi
54: 
55: # Check if each stock profile exists in .config/dolphin-emu/GamecubeControllerProfiles
56: for PROFILE in east.stacked east.inline south.stacked south.inline; do
57:         if [ ! -f "${CONF_DIR}/GamecubeControllerProfiles/GCPadNew.ini.${PROFILE}" ]; then
58:                 cp -r "/usr/config/dolphin-emu/GamecubeControllerProfiles/GCPadNew.ini.${PROFILE}" "${CONF_DIR}/GamecubeControllerProfiles/GCPadNew.ini.${PROFILE}"
59:         fi
60: done
61: 
62: # Check if GC custom controller profile exists in .config/dolphin-emu/GamecubeControllerProfiles
63: if [ ! -f "${CONF_DIR}/GamecubeControllerProfiles/GCPadNew.ini.custom" ]; then
64:         cp -r "/usr/config/dolphin-emu/GamecubeControllerProfiles/GCPadNew.ini.south.stacked" "${CONF_DIR}/GamecubeControllerProfiles/GCPadNew.ini.custom"
65: fi
66: 
67: # Prepare only known save links; retained real data requires an explicit choice.
68: if ! /usr/bin/python3 -I -S -B /usr/bin/dolphin-save-paths prepare gamecube; then
69:   exit 250
70: fi
71: 
72: # Make sure all Triforce Gecko codes are copied to GameSettings
73: for f in /usr/config/dolphin-emu/triforce_gecko_codes/*; do
74:   [ -e "/storage/.config/dolphin-emu/GameSettings/$(basename "$f")" ] || cp -fH "$f" "/storage/.config/dolphin-emu/GameSettings/"
75: done
76: rm -rf "/storage/.config/dolphin-emu/triforce_gecko_codes"
77: 
78: # Emulation Station Features
79: GAME=$(echo "${1}"| sed "s#^/.*/##")
80: PLATFORM=$(echo "${2}"| sed "s#^/.*/##")
81: AA=$(get_setting anti_aliasing "${PLATFORM}" "${GAME}")
82: ASPECT=$(get_setting aspect_ratio "${PLATFORM}" "${GAME}")
83: AUDIOBE=$(get_setting audio_backend "${PLATFORM}" "${GAME}")
84: ASWAPDISCS=$(get_setting auto_swap_discs "${PLATFORM}" "${GAME}")
85: CLOCK=$(get_setting clock_speed "${PLATFORM}" "${GAME}")
86: ENBCHEATS=$(get_setting enable_cheats "${PLATFORM}" "${GAME}")
87: GRENDERER=$(get_setting graphics_backend "${PLATFORM}" "${GAME}")
88: IRES=$(get_setting internal_resolution "${PLATFORM}" "${GAME}")
89: FPS=$(get_setting show_fps "${PLATFORM}" "${GAME}")
90: CON=$(get_setting gamecube_controller_profile "${PLATFORM}" "${GAME}")
91: SWAPSHOULDER=$(get_setting swap_shoulders "${PLATFORM}" "${GAME}")
92: HKEY=$(get_setting hotkey_enable_button "${PLATFORM}" "${GAME}")
93: SAVETYPE=$(get_setting save_type "${PLATFORM}" "${GAME}")
94: SAVEREG=$(get_setting save_gci_region "${PLATFORM}" "${GAME}")
95: SHADERM=$(get_setting shader_mode "${PLATFORM}" "${GAME}")
96: SHADERP=$(get_setting shader_precompile "${PLATFORM}" "${GAME}")
97: VSYNC=$(get_setting vsync "${PLATFORM}" "${GAME}")
98: SKIPBIOS=$(get_setting use_bios "${PLATFORM}" "${GAME}")
99: EFBACCESS=$(get_setting skip_efb_cpu_access "${PLATFORM}" "${GAME}")
100: EFBTEXTURE=$(get_setting store_efb_to_texture_only "${PLATFORM}" "${GAME}")
101: XFBTEXTURE=$(get_setting store_xfb_to_texture_only "${PLATFORM}" "${GAME}")
102: TEXTURE_CACHE_ACCURACY=$(get_setting texture_cache_accuracy "${PLATFORM}" "${GAME}")
103: SYNC_GPU_THREAD=$(get_setting sync_gpu_thread "${PLATFORM}" "${GAME}")
104: RUMBLE=$(get_setting rumble "${PLATFORM}" "${GAME}")
105: WHACK=$(get_setting widescreen_hack "${PLATFORM}" "${GAME}")
~~~~

1: #include "guis/GuiWifi.h"

25: 
26: namespace
27: {
28: void releasePickerButtons(GuiComponent* component)
29: {
30: 	// Called only after hitTest(-1, -1) has cleared every hover flag. A
31: 	// held mouse press is then released without invoking a button action.
32: 	if (auto button = dynamic_cast<ButtonComponent*>(component))
33: 		button->onMouseClick(1, false, -1, -1);
34: 	for (int i = 0; i < component->getChildCount(); ++i)
35: 		releasePickerButtons(component->getChild(i));
36: }
37: }
38: 
39: // Owned by the main thread, independently of any picker. Like the save-state
40: // bookkeeper, keep the owner alive until the explicit shutdown/atexit join;
41: // a static joinable thread could otherwise be destroyed before that hook.
42: bool GuiWifi::sScansStopping = false;
43: 
44: struct GuiWifi::ScanWorker
45: {
46: 	std::shared_ptr<Scan> scan;
47: 	std::thread thread;
48: 	bool stopping = false;
49: };
50: 
51: GuiWifi::ScanWorker* GuiWifi::scanWorker(bool create)
52: {
53: 	static ScanWorker* worker = nullptr;
54: 	if (create && worker == nullptr)
55: 		worker = new ScanWorker();
56: 	return worker;
57: }
58: 
59: void GuiWifi::shutdownScans()
60: {
61: 	sScansStopping = true;
62: 	auto worker = scanWorker(false);
63: 	if (worker == nullptr || worker->stopping)
64: 		return;
65: 	worker->stopping = true;
66: 	if (worker->scan)
67: 		worker->scan->cancelled.store(true, std::memory_order_release);
68: 	if (worker->thread.joinable())
69: 	{
70: 		LOG(LogInfo) << "wifi picker: stopping scan worker";
71: 		worker->thread.join();
72: 		LOG(LogInfo) << "wifi picker: scan worker joined";
73: 	}
74: 	worker->scan.reset();
75: }
76: 
77: GuiWifi::GuiWifi(Window* window, const std::string& title, const std::function<void()>& onJoined)
78: 	: GuiComponent(window), mMenu(window, title.c_str()), mTitle(title), mOnJoined(onJoined), mSavedKnown(false), mWaitingLoad(false)
79: {
80: 	setSize(Renderer::getScreenWidth(), Renderer::getScreenHeight());
81: 	addChild(&mMenu);
82: 	mMenu.setUpdateType(ComponentListFlags::UPDATE_WHEN_SELECTED);
83: 	mMenu.addButton(_("REFRESH"), _("REFRESH"), [this] { onRefresh(); });
84: 	mMenu.addButton(_("INPUT MANUALLY"), _("INPUT MANUALLY"), [this] { onManualInput(); });
85: 	mMenu.addButton(_("BACK"), _("BACK"), [this] { if (!mWaitingLoad) delete this; });
86: 
87: 	// Reserve the viewport once, including an in-list status band. Never
88: 	// call updateSize/setSubTitle or rebuild buttons as scan answers arrive.
89: 	const bool fullScreen = Renderer::ScreenSettings::fullScreenMenus();
90: 	mMenu.setSize(fullScreen ? mSize.x() : std::min(mSize.y(), mSize.x() * 0.90f),
91: 		fullScreen ? mSize.y() : mSize.y() * 0.75f);
92: 	mMenu.setPosition((mSize.x() - mMenu.getSize().x()) / 2,
93: 		fullScreen ? 0 : mSize.y() * 0.15f);
94: 
95: 	auto theme = ThemeData::getMenuTheme();
96: 	auto list = mMenu.getList();
97: 	const auto position = list->getPosition();
98: 	const auto size = list->getSize();
99: 	// Prime this shared font before measuring its maximum glyph height.
100: 	theme->Text.font->sizeText(_("SEARCHING WI-FI NETWORKS") + std::string(_("COULDN'T SEARCH WI-FI NETWORKS. TRY AGAIN.")) +
101: 		_("SHOWING PREVIOUS RESULTS.") + _("COULDN'T CHECK WHICH NETWORKS ARE SAVED.") + _("NO WI-FI NETWORKS FOUND"));
102: 	const float statusHeight = theme->Text.font->sizeText("A\nA", 1.0f).y() + mSize.y() * 0.01f;
103: 	const float inset = ComponentList::rowInset();
104: 	const float spinnerWidth = theme->Text.font->getLetterHeight() * 2.0f;
105: 	mStatus = std::make_shared<TextComponent>(mWindow, "", theme->Text.font, theme->Text.color);
106: 	mStatus->setMultiLine(TextComponent::MultiLineType::MULTILINE);
107: 	mStatus->setLineSpacing(1.0f);
108: 	mStatus->setVerticalAlignment(ALIGN_CENTER);
109: 	if (EsLocale::isRTL())
110: 		mStatus->setHorizontalAlignment(ALIGN_RIGHT);
111: 	mStatus->setSize(size.x() - 2 * inset - spinnerWidth, statusHeight);
112: 	mStatus->setPosition(position.x() + inset + spinnerWidth, position.y());
113: 	mStatus->setZIndex(20);
114: 	mMenu.addChild(mStatus.get());
115: 	list->setPosition(position.x(), position.y() + statusHeight);
116: 	list->setSize(size.x(), std::max(0.0f, size.y() - statusHeight));
117: 
118: 	// No posted callback capturing this. The worker starts after the native
119: 	// components are ready and publishes values for update() to consume.
120: 	onRefresh(false);
121: }
122: 
123: void GuiWifi::load(const std::vector<WifiText::PickerRow>& rows)
124: {
125: 	auto list = mMenu.getList();
126: 	const bool buttonsFocused = mMenu.isCursorToButtons();
127: 	const int previousIndex = list->getCursorIndex();
128: 	const std::string selected = list->getSelectedUserData();
129: 	const bool unchanged = rows.size() == mRows.size() && std::equal(rows.begin(), rows.end(), mRows.begin(),
130: 		[](const WifiText::PickerRow& a, const WifiText::PickerRow& b)
131: 		{
132: 			return a.name == b.name && a.saved == b.saved && a.connected == b.connected &&
133: 				a.savedKnown == b.savedKnown && a.profile == b.profile;
134: 		});
135: 
136: 	// Keep the actual list and scroll offset on identical refreshes. When
137: 	// rows change, restore the SSID, or the nearest surviving index. The
138: 	// native list keeps that row visible instead of jumping to the top.
139: 	if (!unchanged)
140: 	{
141: 		mMenu.clear();
142: 		mRows = rows;
143: 		for (const auto& row : mRows)
144: 			addRow(row);
145: 		if (!mRows.empty())
146: 		{
147: 			auto match = std::find_if(mRows.begin(), mRows.end(), [&selected](const WifiText::PickerRow& row) { return row.name == selected; });
148: 			const int index = match == mRows.end() ? std::min(previousIndex, (int)mRows.size() - 1) : (int)(match - mRows.begin());
149: 			list->setCursorIndex(std::max(0, index), true);
150: 		}
151: 	}
152: 	if (mRows.empty())
153: 		mMenu.setCursorToButton(0);
154: 	else if (!buttonsFocused)
155: 		mMenu.setCursorToList();
156: }
157: 
158: // The name as NetworkManager has it -- case and spaces kept, since a
159: // network's name is what the player recognises it by (addEntry would
160: // upper-case it) -- and, on the right at the row's own weight, CONNECTED for
161: // the network the device is on or SAVED for one it holds a profile for: the
162: // shape and the word of MANAGE SAVED NETWORKS' mark, a fact beside the
163: // action.
164: void GuiWifi::addRow(const WifiText::PickerRow& network)
165: {
166: 	auto theme = ThemeData::getMenuTheme();
167: 	ComponentListRow row;
168: 
169: 	auto name = std::make_shared<TextComponent>(mWindow, network.name, theme->Text.font, theme->Text.color);
170: 	if (EsLocale::isRTL())
171: 		name->setHorizontalAlignment(Alignment::ALIGN_RIGHT);
172: 	row.addElement(name, true);
173: 
174: 	if (network.connected || network.saved)
175: 	{
176: 		const std::string mark = network.connected ? _("CONNECTED") : _("SAVED");
177: 		auto label = std::make_shared<TextComponent>(mWindow, mark, theme->Text.font, theme->Text.color, Alignment::ALIGN_RIGHT);
178: 		label->setSize(theme->Text.font->sizeText(mark + "  ").x(), 0);
179: 		row.addElement(label, false);
180: 	}
181: 
182: 	const WifiText::PickerRow chosen = network;
183: 	row.makeAcceptInputHandler([this, chosen] { onSelect(chosen); });
184: 	mMenu.addRow(row, false, false, network.name);
185: }
186: 
187: // A press: the network the device is on joins again with the key
188: // NetworkManager holds -- it answers at once, or comes back if it had
189: // dropped (#308 F-WF-05: this used to close on the list's snapshot); a
190: // saved one is offered CONNECT / FORGET / CANCEL (#318, D-UI-118); any
191: // unsaved open network connects without a key; protected or unknown ones ask
192: // for their key. One whose profile could not be asked about
193: // is asked about again first (F-WF-03/06). WifiText::pressAction decides.
194: void GuiWifi::onSelect(const WifiText::PickerRow& row)
195: {
196: 	if (mWaitingLoad || mScan)
197: 		return;
198: 
199: 	if (row.savedKnown && WifiText::profilesForSsid(row.name, mSaved).size() > 1 && row.profile.empty())
200: 	{
201: 		chooseProfile(row.name);
202: 		return;
203: 	}
204: 	act(WifiText::pressAction(row), row.name, WifiText::joinName(row));
205: }
206: 
207: // Several profiles can name one SSID. The player chooses a profile before
208: // CONNECT or FORGET; neither operation guesses which stored key they meant.
209: void GuiWifi::chooseProfile(const std::string& name)
210: {
211: 	auto page = new GuiSettings(mWindow, _("SAVED NETWORKS"));
212: 	for (const auto& profile : WifiText::profilesForSsid(name, mSaved))
213: 		page->addEntry(profile.name, true, [this, name, profile, page]
214: 		{
215: 			// Closing page destroys this row's callback and its captures.
216: 			// Keep the values on the stack before opening the next dialog.
217: 			GuiWifi* picker = this;
218: 			const std::string networkName = name;
219: 			const std::string profileName = profile.name;
220: 			page->close();
221: 			picker->act(WifiText::PressAction::ChooseSaved, networkName, profileName);
222: 		});
223: 	mWindow->pushGui(page);
224: }
225: 
226: // A join goes by the profile's name, which need not be the SSID the row
227: // shows (#308 claude F-WF-12, gpt F-WF-03): the connected row joined by its
228: // SSID, which is no profile's name for a renamed one, and the player read
229: // COULDN'T CONNECT, with forget-and-rejoin advice, about the network they
230: // were on.
231: void GuiWifi::act(WifiText::PressAction action, const std::string& name, const std::string& joinAs)
232: {
233: 	switch (action)
234: 	{
235: 	case WifiText::PressAction::Join:
236: 		join(name, joinAs);
237: 		break;
238: 	case WifiText::PressAction::ChooseSaved:
239: 	{
240: 		// A saved network the device is not on: CONNECT with the key
241: 		// NetworkManager holds, or FORGET it -- the second choice is the one
242: 		// ROCKNIX's own saved-Wi-Fi work offers, behind the manage page's
243: 		// confirmation and reader (GuiMenu::forgetWifiNetworkWithConfirmation),
244: 		// which rebuilds this list once the profile is gone. CONNECT first;
245: 		// CANCEL last, so B lands on it (es-ui-style-guide: back never lands
246: 		// on the destructive choice). The profile is forgotten by the name
247: 		// NetworkManager has for it (joinAs), which is the name the manage
248: 		// page shows.
249: 		const std::string text = Utils::String::format(_("%s IS SAVED.").c_str(), name.c_str())
250: 			+ "\n\n" + _("CONNECT WITH ITS SAVED KEY, OR FORGET IT?");
251: 		mWindow->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(mWindow, text,
252: 			_("CONNECT"), [this, name, joinAs] { join(name, joinAs); },
253: 			_("FORGET"), [this, joinAs] { GuiMenu::forgetWifiNetworkWithConfirmation(mWindow, joinAs, false, [this] { onRefresh(false); }); },
254: 			_("CANCEL"), nullptr));
255: 		break;
256: 	}
257: 	case WifiText::PressAction::AskKey:
258: 		checkSecurityAndConnect(name);
259: 		break;
260: 	case WifiText::PressAction::CheckAgain:
261: 		// Never taken for a network with no profile: the key path rebuilds a
262: 		// profile of that name from what is typed.
263: 		mWindow->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(mWindow,
264: 			_("COULDN'T CHECK WHICH NETWORKS ARE SAVED.") + std::string("\n\n") + _("CHECK AGAIN NOW?"),
265: 			_("YES"), [this] { onRefresh(false); },
266: 			_("NO"), nullptr));
267: 		break;
268: 	}
269: }
270: 
271: // INPUT MANUALLY: a hidden network's name, taken as a row would be -- a
272: // name NetworkManager holds a profile for joins, the one the device is on
273: // needs nothing, any other is asked for its key. Not trimmed: a name may
274: // begin or end with a space, and it is matched as NetworkManager has it.
275: void GuiWifi::onManualInput()
276: {
277: 	if (mWaitingLoad || mScan)
278: 		return;
279: 
280: 	auto onName = [this](const std::string& name)
281: 	{
282: 		if (name.empty())
283: 			return;
284: 		const auto profile = WifiText::preferredProfile(name, mSaved);
285: 		if (mSavedKnown && WifiText::profilesForSsid(name, mSaved).size() > 1 && profile.empty())
286: 		{
287: 			chooseProfile(name);
288: 			return;
289: 		}
290: 		act(WifiText::manualAction(name, mCurrent, mSaved, mSavedKnown), name,
291: 			profile.empty() ? WifiText::manualJoinName(name, mCurrent, mCurrentProfile) : profile);
292: 	};
293: 
294: 	if (Settings::getInstance()->getBool("UseOSK"))
295: 		mWindow->pushGui(new GuiTextEditPopupKeyboard(mWindow, mTitle, "", onName, false));
296: 	else
297: 		mWindow->pushGui(new GuiTextEditPopup(mWindow, mTitle, "", onName, false));
298: }
299: 
300: // A saved network: its profile, with the key NetworkManager holds, behind
301: // the spinner -- wifictl join waits up to 90 s for the association, as
302: // connect does, and the screen would otherwise freeze for it. The script
303: // also moves the settings wifi.ssid and wifi.key onto the joined network,
304: // so the paths that connect from the settings (the restore wizard, the
305: // ENABLE WI-FI switch) still name the network the player is on; SystemConf
306: // is re-read so the interface sees the same file.
307: void GuiWifi::join(const std::string& name, const std::string& profile)
308: {
309: 	Window* window = mWindow;
310: 	mWaitingLoad = true;
311: 	LOG(LogInfo) << "wifi picker: joining the saved network " << name << " (profile " << profile << ")";
312: 	window->pushGui(new GuiLoading<WifiText::JoinAnswer>(window, _("CONNECTING TO WI-FI"),
313: 		[profile](IGuiLoadingHandler*) { return ApiSystem::getInstance()->joinWifiNetwork(profile); },
314: 		[this, window, name, profile](WifiText::JoinAnswer answer)
315: 		{
316: 			mWaitingLoad = false;
317: 			const int code = answer.code;
318: 			if (!answer.joined())
319: 			{
320: 				LOG(LogWarning) << "wifi picker: could not join the saved network " << name << " (profile " << profile << ", wifictl join exited " << code << ")";
321: 				// The advice by what happened (WifiText::joinFailure, #308
322: 				// F-WF-03/06): NetworkManager not answering is not a key that
323: 				// changed, and forgetting the network on that advice would
324: 				// lose a profile that was fine.
325: 				const std::string advice = WifiText::joinFailure(code) == WifiText::JoinFailure::ServiceNotAnswering
326: 					? _("THE WI-FI SERVICE DIDN'T ANSWER. TRY AGAIN IN A MOMENT.")
327: 					: _("IF ITS KEY HAS CHANGED, FORGET IT UNDER MANAGE SAVED NETWORKS AND JOIN IT AGAIN WITH THE NEW KEY.");
328: 				window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window,
329: 					Utils::String::format(_("COULDN'T CONNECT TO %s.").c_str(), name.c_str()) + "\n\n" + advice, _("OK")));
330: 				return;
331: 			}
332: 			// Re-read for what the join wrote (wifi.ssid, wifi.key), keeping
333: 			// any change this interface has not saved yet -- one the settings
334: 			// lock refused -- unless the join wrote that key (G-E1-03).
335: 			SystemConf::getInstance()->loadSystemConf(true);
336: 			joined(name);
337: 		}));
338: }
339: 
340: // Any other network: its key first -- empty for an open network -- then
341: // the connection wifictl connect makes, which leaves a profile behind for
342: // next time. On success the settings take the network and its key, as they
343: // did when the row edited them: the network configured last, for the paths
344: // that connect from the settings.
345: void GuiWifi::checkSecurityAndConnect(const std::string& name)
346: {
347: 	mWaitingLoad = true;
348: 	mWindow->pushGui(new GuiLoading<std::string>(mWindow, _("CHECKING WI-FI NETWORK"),
349: 		[name](IGuiLoadingHandler*) { return ApiSystem::getInstance()->getWifiSecurity(name); },
350: 		[this, name](const std::string& security)
351: 		{
352: 			mWaitingLoad = false;
353: 			if (security == "open")
354: 				connect(name, "", true);
355: 			else
356: 				askKeyAndConnect(name);
357: 		}));
358: }
359: 
360: void GuiWifi::askKeyAndConnect(const std::string& name)
361: {
362: 	auto onKey = [this, name](const std::string& key) { connect(name, key); };
363: 
364: 	if (Settings::getInstance()->getBool("UseOSK"))
365: 		mWindow->pushGui(new GuiTextEditPopupKeyboard(mWindow, _("WI-FI KEY"), "", onKey, false));
366: 	else
367: 		mWindow->pushGui(new GuiTextEditPopup(mWindow, _("WI-FI KEY"), "", onKey, false));
368: }
369: 
370: void GuiWifi::connect(const std::string& name, const std::string& key, bool openNetwork)
371: {
372: 	Window* window = mWindow;
373: 	mWaitingLoad = true;
374: 	LOG(LogInfo) << "wifi picker: connecting to " << name;
375: #if !WIN32
376: 	const std::string country = SystemConf::getInstance()->get("wifi.country");
377: 	auto apply = [name, key, country, openNetwork](IGuiLoadingHandler*) { return openNetwork ? ApiSystem::getInstance()->connectOpenWifi(name) : ApiSystem::getInstance()->enableWifi(name, key, country); };
378: #else
379: 	auto apply = [name, key, openNetwork](IGuiLoadingHandler*) { return openNetwork ? ApiSystem::getInstance()->connectOpenWifi(name) : ApiSystem::getInstance()->enableWifi(name, key); };
380: #endif
381: 	window->pushGui(new GuiLoading<bool>(window, _("CONNECTING TO WI-FI"), apply,
382: 		[this, window, name, key, openNetwork](bool ok)
383: 		{
384: 			mWaitingLoad = false;
385: 			if (!ok)
386: 			{
387: 				LOG(LogWarning) << "wifi picker: could not connect to " << name;
388: 				window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window,
389: 					Utils::String::format(_("COULDN'T CONNECT TO %s.").c_str(), name.c_str()) + "\n\n" + (openNetwork ? _("REFRESH THE NETWORK LIST AND TRY AGAIN.") : _("CHECK THE KEY AND TRY AGAIN.")), _("OK")));
390: 				return;
391: 			}
392: 			SystemConf::getInstance()->set("wifi.ssid", name);
393: 			SystemConf::getInstance()->set("wifi.key", key);
394: 			SystemConf::getInstance()->saveSystemConf();
395: 			joined(name);
396: 		}));
397: }
398: 
399: // The picker's job is done: the toast says so over whatever the page
400: // returns to, and the page that opened the picker rebuilds to read the
401: // connection back. The callback is copied out first -- it belongs to that
402: // page, and this component is gone before it runs.
403: void GuiWifi::joined(const std::string& name)
404: {
405: 	Window* window = mWindow;
406: 	auto onJoined = mOnJoined;
407: 	// <glyph> <subject> : <outcome>, one space after the glyph, as every
408: 	// toast (#308 F-WF-08); CONNECTED is the row's word for the same fact.
409: 	window->displayNotificationMessage(_U("\uF058 ") + WifiText::joinedNotice(name, _("CONNECTED")));
410: 	delete this;
411: 	if (onJoined != nullptr)
412: 		onJoined();
413: }
414: 
415: bool GuiWifi::input(InputConfig* config, Input input)
416: {
417: 	if (input.value != 0 && config->isMappedTo(BUTTON_BACK, input))
418: 	{
419: 		if (!mWaitingLoad)
420: 			delete this;
421: 		return true;
422: 	}
423: 
424: 	// A press during a scan cannot join an old row, open a key editor or
425: 	// queue another scan. BACK remains available without joining the worker.
426: 	if (mScan || mWaitingLoad)
427: 		return true;
428: 	return GuiComponent::input(config, input);
429: }
430: 
431: bool GuiWifi::hitTest(int x, int y, Transform4x4f& parentTransform, std::vector<GuiComponent*>* result)
432: {
433: 	if (!mScan)
434: 		return GuiComponent::hitTest(x, y, parentTransform, result);
435: 
436: 	// Mouse/touch bypass input(). During scanning only the native BACK
437: 	// button may capture a press, so a release after completion cannot act
438: 	// on a refreshed row or start the action of a previously pressed button.
439: 	std::vector<GuiComponent*> hits;
440: 	const bool hit = GuiComponent::hitTest(x, y, parentTransform, &hits);
441: 	if (result)
442: 		for (auto component : hits)
443: 			if (auto button = dynamic_cast<ButtonComponent*>(component))
444: 				if (button->getText() == _("BACK"))
445: 					result->push_back(button);
446: 	return hit;
447: }
448: 
449: std::vector<HelpPrompt> GuiWifi::getHelpPrompts()
450: {
451: 	std::vector<HelpPrompt> prompts;
452: 	if (!mScan && !mWaitingLoad)
453: 		prompts = mMenu.getHelpPrompts();
454: 	prompts.push_back(HelpPrompt(BUTTON_BACK, _("BACK")));
455: 	return prompts;
456: }
457: 
458: void GuiWifi::update(int deltaTime)
459: {
460: 	GuiComponent::update(deltaTime);
461: 	if (!mScan || !mScan->ready.load(std::memory_order_acquire))
462: 		return;
463: 
464: 	const Answer answer = mScan->answer;
465: 	mScan.reset();
466: 	mBusy.reset();
467: 	mWindow->releaseMouseCapture();
468: 
469: 	// A failed range query keeps prior network names. Saved/current metadata
470: 	// is freshly read even then; unknown metadata keeps the existing guarded
471: 	// CheckAgain route rather than interpreting an unknown profile as absent.
472: 	std::vector<std::string> inRange = answer.inRange;
473: 	const bool retainedResults = !answer.inRangeKnown && !mRows.empty();
474: 	if (!answer.inRangeKnown)
475: 		for (const auto& row : mRows)
476: 			inRange.push_back(row.name);
477: 	const auto rows = WifiText::pickerRows(inRange, answer.saved, answer.current,
478: 		answer.savedKnown, answer.currentKnown);
479: 	mSaved = answer.saved;
480: 	mSavedKnown = answer.savedKnown;
481: 	mCurrent.clear();
482: 	mCurrentProfile.clear();
483: 	for (const auto& row : rows)
484: 		if (row.connected)
485: 		{
486: 			mCurrent = row.name;
487: 			mCurrentProfile = row.profile;
488: 		}
489: 	load(rows);
490: 
491: 	std::string status;
492: 	if (!answer.inRangeKnown)
493: 	{
494: 		status = _("COULDN'T SEARCH WI-FI NETWORKS. TRY AGAIN.");
495: 		if (retainedResults)
496: 			status += "\n" + std::string(_("SHOWING PREVIOUS RESULTS."));
497: 	}
498: 	else if (rows.empty())
499: 		status = _("NO WI-FI NETWORKS FOUND");
500: 	else if (!mSavedKnown)
501: 		status = _("COULDN'T CHECK WHICH NETWORKS ARE SAVED.");
502: 	mStatus->setText(status);
503: 	LOG(LogInfo) << "wifi picker: scan " << (answer.inRangeKnown ? "completed" : "failed")
504: 		<< "; rows=" << rows.size() << "; retained=" << retainedResults;
505: 	updateHelpPrompts();
506: }
507: 
508: void GuiWifi::onRefresh(bool rescan)
509: {
510: 	if (mScan || mWaitingLoad || sScansStopping)
511: 		return;
512: 
513: 	// The process owns the one worker; picker Back never waits for it.
514: 	// Reopening attaches to a pending scan. A later explicit refresh joins
515: 	// the completed worker and starts a new job instead of reusing its answer.
516: 	auto worker = scanWorker(true);
517: 	if (worker->stopping)
518: 		return;
519: 	mScan = worker->scan;
520: 	if (mScan && mScan->ready.load(std::memory_order_acquire))
521: 	{
522: 		if (worker->thread.joinable())
523: 			worker->thread.join();
524: 		worker->scan.reset();
525: 		mScan.reset();
526: 	}
527: 	const bool reused = mScan != nullptr;
528: 	if (!mScan)
529: 	{
530: 		mScan = std::make_shared<Scan>();
531: 		worker->scan = mScan;
532: 		auto scan = mScan;
533: 		try
534: 		{
535: 			worker->thread = std::thread([scan, rescan]
536: 			{
537: 				try
538: 				{
539: 					Answer answer;
540: 					const auto range = WifiScanProcess::read({"wifictl", rescan ? "scanlist" : "list"}, std::chrono::seconds(rescan ? 45 : 30), &scan->cancelled);
541: 					answer.inRangeKnown = range.code == 0;
542: 					answer.inRange = range.lines;
543: 
544: 					// Match ApiSystem's saved/current protocol, but reject signal
545: 					// and read failures too. No singleton or GUI is worker-owned.
546: 					if (!scan->cancelled.load(std::memory_order_acquire))
547: 					{
548: 						const auto saved = WifiScanProcess::read({"wifictl", "saved", "--ssid"}, std::chrono::seconds(15), &scan->cancelled);
549: 						answer.savedKnown = saved.code == 0;
550: 						if (answer.savedKnown)
551: 							for (const auto& line : saved.lines)
552: 							{
553: 								WifiText::SavedNetwork network;
554: 								if (!WifiText::parseSavedDetailsLine(line, network))
555: 								{
556: 									answer.savedKnown = false;
557: 									break;
558: 								}
559: 								answer.saved.push_back(network);
560: 							}
561: 						if (!answer.savedKnown)
562: 							answer.saved.clear();
563: 					}
564: 
565: 					if (!scan->cancelled.load(std::memory_order_acquire))
566: 					{
567: 						const auto current = WifiScanProcess::read({"wifictl", "current"}, std::chrono::seconds(10), &scan->cancelled);
568: 						answer.current = WifiText::parseCurrent(current.lines);
569: 						answer.currentKnown = (current.code == 0 && !answer.current.empty()) || current.code == 1;
570: 						if (current.code != 0)
571: 							answer.current.clear();
572: 					}
573: 					scan->answer = std::move(answer);
574: 				}
575: 				catch (...)
576: 				{
577: 					// The default answer is unknown, not a successful empty scan.
578: 					LOG(LogWarning) << "wifi picker: scan worker failed";
579: 				}
580: 				LOG(LogInfo) << "wifi picker: scan worker finished";
581: 				scan->ready.store(true, std::memory_order_release);
582: 			});
583: 		}
584: 		catch (const std::exception&)
585: 		{
586: 			LOG(LogWarning) << "wifi picker: could not start scan worker";
587: 			mScan->ready.store(true, std::memory_order_release);
588: 		}
589: 	}
590: 
591: 	if (mMenu.getList()->getScrollingVelocity() != 0)
592: 		mMenu.getList()->stopScrolling();
593: 	auto transform = Transform4x4f::Identity();
594: 	mMenu.hitTest(-1, -1, transform);
595: 	releasePickerButtons(&mMenu);
596: 	mWindow->releaseMouseCapture();
597: 	mStatus->setText(_("SEARCHING WI-FI NETWORKS"));
598: 	const float spinnerWidth = ThemeData::getMenuTheme()->Text.font->getLetterHeight() * 2.0f;
599: 	mBusy = std::make_shared<BusyComponent>(mWindow, "");
600: 	mBusy->setBackgroundVisible(false);
601: 	mBusy->setSize(spinnerWidth, mStatus->getSize().y());
602: 	mBusy->setPosition(mStatus->getPosition().x() - spinnerWidth, mStatus->getPosition().y());
603: 	mBusy->setZIndex(20);
604: 	mMenu.addChild(mBusy.get());
605: 	LOG(LogInfo) << "wifi picker: " << (reused ? "attached to pending scan" : "scan started");
606: 	updateHelpPrompts();
607: }
~~~~

15: // The Wi-Fi picker behind NETWORK SETTINGS' WI-FI NETWORK row (fork #191,
16: // renamed from WI-FI SSID by D-UI-071; the
17: // maintainer's paradigm of 2026-09-15: the row is the network the device is
18: // on, this list is what is in range). One row per network in range -- the
19: // one joined now first and marked CONNECTED, the ones NetworkManager holds a
20: // profile for marked SAVED. A press on a saved row offers CONNECT (with
21: // the key NetworkManager has), FORGET (the manage page's confirmation and
22: // reader) or CANCEL (#318, D-UI-118); a press on the connected row joins it
23: // again at once; a press on any other asks for the key and connects,
24: // which saves it for next time; INPUT MANUALLY takes a hidden network's
25: // name the same way. Once the device is on a network the page that opened
26: // the picker is told, so it rebuilds and reads the connection back.
27: class GuiWifi : public GuiComponent
28: {
29: public:
30: 	GuiWifi(Window* window, const std::string& title, const std::function<void()>& onJoined);
31: 	// Main-thread normal-exit hook; closing a picker does not stop its scan.
32: 	static void shutdownScans();
33: 	bool input(InputConfig* config, Input input) override;
34: 	void update(int deltaTime) override;
35: 	bool hitTest(int x, int y, Transform4x4f& parentTransform, std::vector<GuiComponent*>* result = nullptr) override;
36: 	std::vector<HelpPrompt> getHelpPrompts() override;
37: 
38: private:
39: 	// The three answers the list is built from, fetched together off the
40: 	// interface thread.
41: 	struct Answer
42: 	{
43: 		std::vector<std::string> inRange;
44: 		bool inRangeKnown = false;
45: 		std::vector<WifiText::SavedNetwork> saved;
46: 		std::string current;
47: 		// Whether `wifictl saved` and `wifictl current` answered at all: an
48: 		// empty list and no list are different answers (#308 F-WF-03/06).
49: 		bool savedKnown = false;
50: 		bool currentKnown = false;
51: 	};
52: 
53: 	// The worker owns only this value. No GUI or Window pointer crosses into
54: 	// it, and the UI consumes the answer only after the release/acquire pair.
55: 	struct Scan
56: 	{
57: 		Answer answer;
58: 		std::atomic<bool> ready{false};
59: 		std::atomic<bool> cancelled{false};
60: 	};
61: 
62: 	static bool sScansStopping; // main-thread-only; also blocks first use during teardown
63: 	struct ScanWorker;
64: 	static ScanWorker* scanWorker(bool create);
65:

67: 	void addRow(const WifiText::PickerRow& row);
68: 	void onSelect(const WifiText::PickerRow& row);
69: 	void chooseProfile(const std::string& name);
70: 	// name is the network as the player sees it; joinAs the name `wifictl
71: 	// join` takes (WifiText::joinName, manualJoinName).
72: 	void act(WifiText::PressAction action, const std::string& name, const std::string& joinAs);
73: 	void onManualInput();
74: 	void onRefresh(bool rescan = true);
75: 	void join(const std::string& name, const std::string& profile);
76: 	void checkSecurityAndConnect(const std::string& name);
77: 	void askKeyAndConnect(const std::string& name);
78: 	void connect(const std::string& name, const std::string& key, bool openNetwork = false);
79: 	void joined(const std::string& name);
80: 
81: 	MenuComponent mMenu;
82: 	std::shared_ptr<TextComponent> mStatus;
83: 	std::shared_ptr<BusyComponent> mBusy;
84: 	std::shared_ptr<Scan> mScan;
85: 	std::vector<WifiText::PickerRow> mRows;
86: 	std::string mTitle;

1: #pragma once
2: 
3: // A bounded, read-only subprocess for the Wi-Fi picker (#577). No shell,
4: // GUI, singleton or callback is owned here. Kept separate so a disposable
5: // process fixture can exercise the actual runner without linking ES.
6: #include <atomic>
7: #include <chrono>
8: #include <string>
9: #include <vector>
10: 
11: #if !WIN32
12: #include <cerrno>
13: #include <csignal>
14: #include <fcntl.h>
15: #include <poll.h>
16: #include <spawn.h>
17: #include <sys/wait.h>
18: #include <unistd.h>
19: extern char** environ;
20: #endif
21: 
22: namespace WifiScanProcess
23: {
24: struct Answer
25: {
26: 	int code = -1; // spawn/read/signal/timeout/cleanup failure is never empty success
27: 	std::vector<std::string> lines;
28: };
29: 
30: #if !WIN32
31: class Child
32: {
33: public:
34: 	Child(pid_t child, int output) : pid(child), fd(output) {}
35: 	Child(const Child&) = delete;
36: 	Child& operator=(const Child&) = delete;
37: 	~Child() { finish(); }
38: 
39: 	bool finish()
40: 	{
41: 		if (finished)
42: 			return cleaned;
43: 		finished = true;
44: 		close(fd);
45: 		// Even a reaped leader may have descendants holding the pipe. The
46: 		// spawn creates this group; never signal ES's own process group.
47: 		cleaned = kill(-pid, SIGKILL) == 0 || errno == ESRCH;
48: 		if (!cleaned)
49: 			kill(pid, SIGKILL);
50: 		const auto deadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + std::chrono::seconds(2);
51: 		while (!reaped)
52: 		{
53: 			const pid_t waited = waitpid(pid, &status, WNOHANG);
54: 			if (waited == pid)
55: 				reaped = true;
56: 			else if ((waited < 0 && errno != EINTR) || std::chrono::steady_clock::now() >= deadline)
57: 				return cleaned = false;
58: 			else
59: 				poll(nullptr, 0, 10);
60: 		}
61: 		return cleaned;
62: 	}
63: 
64: 	pid_t pid;
65: 	int fd;
66: 	int status = 0;
67: 	bool reaped = false;
68: private:
69: 	bool finished = false;
70: 	bool cleaned = false;
71: };
72: #endif
73: 
74: inline Answer read(const std::vector<std::string>& command, std::chrono::milliseconds timeout,
75: 	const std::atomic<bool>* cancelled = nullptr)
76: {
77: 	Answer answer;
78: #if !WIN32
79: 	const auto stopped = [cancelled]
80: 	{ return cancelled && cancelled->load(std::memory_order_acquire); };
81: 	if (command.empty() || timeout.count() <= 0 || stopped())
82: 		return answer;
83: 	std::vector<char*> argv;
84: 	for (const auto& part : command)
85: 		argv.push_back(const_cast<char*>(part.c_str()));
86: 	argv.push_back(nullptr);
87: 
88: 	int output[2];
89: 	if (pipe2(output, O_CLOEXEC) != 0)
90: 		return answer;
91: 	// File actions must not close stdout after dup2 when an inherited
92: 	// standard descriptor was absent and pipe2 reused its number.
93: 	for (int& descriptor : output)
94: 		if (descriptor <= STDERR_FILENO)
95: 		{
96: 			const int moved = fcntl(descriptor, F_DUPFD_CLOEXEC, STDERR_FILENO + 1);
97: 			if (moved == -1)
98: 			{
99: 				close(output[0]); close(output[1]);
100: 				return answer;
101: 			}
102: 			close(descriptor);
103: 			descriptor = moved;
104: 		}
105: 	posix_spawn_file_actions_t actions;
106: 	posix_spawnattr_t attributes;
107: 	if (posix_spawn_file_actions_init(&actions) != 0)
108: 	{
109: 		close(output[0]); close(output[1]);
110: 		return answer;
111: 	}
112: 	if (posix_spawnattr_init(&attributes) != 0)
113: 	{
114: 		posix_spawn_file_actions_destroy(&actions);
115: 		close(output[0]); close(output[1]);
116: 		return answer;
117: 	}
118: 	pid_t pid = -1;
119: 	const bool configured =
120: 		posix_spawn_file_actions_adddup2(&actions, output[1], STDOUT_FILENO) == 0 &&
121: 		posix_spawn_file_actions_addclose(&actions, output[0]) == 0 &&
122: 		posix_spawn_file_actions_addclose(&actions, output[1]) == 0 &&
123: 		posix_spawnattr_setflags(&attributes, POSIX_SPAWN_SETPGROUP) == 0 &&
124: 		posix_spawnattr_setpgroup(&attributes, 0) == 0;
125: 	const int spawned = configured && !stopped() ? posix_spawnp(&pid, argv[0], &actions, &attributes, argv.data(), environ) : -1;
126: 	posix_spawnattr_destroy(&attributes);
127: 	posix_spawn_file_actions_destroy(&actions);
128: 	close(output[1]);
129: 	if (spawned != 0)
130: 	{
131: 		close(output[0]);
132: 		return answer;
133: 	}
134: 
135: 	Child child(pid, output[0]);
136: 	const int flags = fcntl(child.fd, F_GETFL);
137: 	if (flags == -1 || fcntl(child.fd, F_SETFL, flags | O_NONBLOCK) == -1)
138: 		return answer;
139: 	const auto deadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + timeout;
140: 	std::string outputText;
141: 	bool eof = false;
142: 	while (true)
143: 	{
144: 		if (stopped())
145: 			return answer;
146: 		char buffer[4096];
147: 		while (!eof)
148: 		{
149: 			if (stopped())
150: 				return answer;
151: 			const ssize_t count = ::read(child.fd, buffer, sizeof(buffer));
152: 			if (count > 0)
153: 			{
154: 				// A corrupt/unbounded command must not grow the UI's heap.
155: 				if (outputText.size() + count > 256 * 1024 || std::chrono::steady_clock::now() >= deadline)
156: 					return answer;
157: 				outputText.append(buffer, count);
158: 			}
159: 			else if (count == 0)
160: 				eof = true;
161: 			else if (errno == EAGAIN || errno == EWOULDBLOCK || errno == EINTR)
162: 				break;
163: 			else
164: 				return answer;
165: 		}
166: 		if (!child.reaped)
167: 		{
168: 			const pid_t waited = waitpid(child.pid, &child.status, WNOHANG);
169: 			if (waited == child.pid)
170: 				child.reaped = true;
171: 			else if (waited < 0 && errno != EINTR)
172: 				return answer;
173: 		}
174: 		if (eof && child.reaped)
175: 			break;
176: 		if (std::chrono::steady_clock::now() >= deadline)
177: 			return answer;
178: 		pollfd descriptor{eof ? -1 : child.fd, POLLIN, 0};
179: 		if (poll(&descriptor, 1, 25) < 0 && errno != EINTR)
180: 			return answer;
181: 	}
182: 	if (stopped() || !child.finish() || !WIFEXITED(child.status))
183: 		return answer;
184: 	answer.code = WEXITSTATUS(child.status);
185: 	if (answer.code == 0)
186: 	{
187: 		size_t start = 0;
188: 		while (start < outputText.size())
189: 		{
190: 			const size_t end = outputText.find('\n', start);
191: 			const std::string line = outputText.substr(start, end == std::string::npos ? end : end - start);
192: 			if (!line.empty())
193: 				answer.lines.push_back(line);
194: 			if (end == std::string::npos)
195: 				break;
196: 			start = end + 1;
197: 		}
198: 	}
199: #endif
200: 	return answer;
201: }
202: }

230: int SaveStateRepository::getNextFreeSlot(FileData* game, std::shared_ptr<SaveStateConfig> config)
231: {
232: 	if (!isEnabled(game))
233: 		return -99;
234: 
235: 	auto repo = game->getSourceFileData()->getSystem()->getSaveStateRepository();	
236: 	auto states = repo->getSaveStates(game, config);
237: 
238: 	if (states.size() == 0)
239: 		return config != nullptr ? config->firstslot : 0;
240: 
241: 	for (int i = 99999; i >= 0; i--)
242: 	{
243: 		auto it = std::find_if(states.cbegin(), states.cend(), [i](const SaveState* x) { return x->slot == i; });
244: 		if (it != states.cend())
245: 			return i + 1;
246: 	}
247: 
248: 	return -99;
249: }

1: // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT AND BSD-3-Clause
2: // Copyright (c) 2026 pixelelated contributors
3: // Padding recurrence/serialization adapted from bodgit/rvz (BSD-3-Clause).
4: // See RVZ-NOTICES.md and LICENSE.rvz-padding. Container code follows Dolphin's
5: // published WiaAndRvz format; no RetroArch or Dolphin implementation is copied.
6: #include "rvz_hash.h"
7: #include "rcheevos/include/rc_hash.h"
8: #include "libretro-common/include/streams/interface_stream.h"
9: #include "libretro-common/include/libretro.h"
10: #include <boost/uuid/detail/sha1.hpp>
11: #include <zstd.h>
12: #include <algorithm>
13: #include <array>
14: #include <cstdint>
15: #include <cstdlib>
16: #include <cstring>
17: #include <memory>
18: #include <stdexcept>
19: #include <vector>
20: #include <utility>
21: 
22: namespace
23: {
24: constexpr uint64_t Sector = 0x8000;
25: constexpr uint32_t MaxChunk = 2 * 1024 * 1024;
26: constexpr uint32_t MaxWork = MaxChunk + 1024 * 1024;
27: constexpr uint64_t MaxDisc = uint64_t(2) * 1024 * 1024 * 1024;
28: constexpr uint64_t MaxFile = uint64_t(4) * 1024 * 1024 * 1024;
29: constexpr uint32_t MaxGroups = 65536;
30: constexpr uint32_t MaxRaw = 4096;
31: using Bytes = std::vector<uint8_t>;
32: 
33: void require(bool condition)
34: {
35:     if (!condition)
36:         throw std::runtime_error("Invalid or unsupported GameCube RVZ");
37: }
38: uint32_t be32(const uint8_t* p)
39: {
40:     return (uint32_t(p[0]) << 24) | (uint32_t(p[1]) << 16) |
41:         (uint32_t(p[2]) << 8) | p[3];
42: }
43: uint64_t be64(const uint8_t* p)
44: {
45:     return (uint64_t(be32(p)) << 32) | be32(p + 4);
46: }
47: bool sha1Matches(const uint8_t* data, size_t size, const uint8_t* expected)
48: {
49:     boost::uuids::detail::sha1 state;
50:     boost::uuids::detail::sha1::digest_type digest;
51:     state.process_bytes(data, size);
52:     state.get_digest(digest);
53:     // Boost changed digest_type from host words to bytes. Accept both APIs.
54:     for (size_t i = 0; i < 20; ++i)
55:     {
56:         const auto byte = sizeof(digest[0]) == 1 ? digest[i] :
57:             ((digest[i / 4] >> (24 - 8 * (i % 4))) & 0xff);
58:         if (byte != expected[i])
59:             return false;
60:     }
61:     return true;
62: }
63: 
64: // BSD-3-Clause adaptation of bodgit/rvz/internal/padding/reader.go.
65: // State is local, and output uses explicit bytes on both endian families.
66: void padding(const uint8_t* seed, uint64_t offset, uint8_t* out, size_t size)
67: {
68:     std::array<uint32_t, 521> words{};
69:     for (size_t i = 0; i < 17; ++i)
70:         words[i] = be32(seed + 4 * i);
71:     for (size_t i = 17; i < words.size(); ++i)
72:         words[i] = (words[i - 17] << 23) ^ (words[i - 16] >> 9) ^ words[i - 1];
73:     const auto advance = [&]() {
74:         for (size_t i = 0; i < 32; ++i)
75:             words[i] ^= words[i + words.size() - 32];
76:         for (size_t i = 32; i < words.size(); ++i)
77:             words[i] ^= words[i - 32];
78:     };
79:     for (unsigned i = 0; i < 4; ++i)
80:         advance();
81:     size_t byteIndex = static_cast<size_t>(offset % Sector);
82:     while (byteIndex >= words.size() * 4)
83:     {
84:         advance();
85:         byteIndex -= words.size() * 4;
86:     }
87:     const unsigned shifts[] = {24, 18, 8, 0};
88:     for (size_t i = 0; i < size; ++i)
89:     {
90:         if (byteIndex == words.size() * 4)
91:         {
92:             advance();
93:             byteIndex = 0;
94:         }
95:         out[i] = static_cast<uint8_t>(words[byteIndex / 4] >> shifts[byteIndex % 4]);
96:         ++byteIndex;
97:     }
98: }
99: 
100: Bytes unpack(const Bytes& packed, size_t expected, uint64_t discOffset)
101: {
102:     Bytes result(expected);
103:     size_t input = 0, output = 0, records = 0;
104:     while (input < packed.size())
105:     {
106:         require(++records <= 4096 && packed.size() - input >= 4);
107:         const uint32_t tag = be32(packed.data() + input);
108:         input += 4;
109:         const uint32_t length = tag & 0x7fffffff;
110:         require(length <= result.size() - output);
111:         if (tag & 0x80000000)
112:         {
113:             require(packed.size() - input >= 68);
114:             if (length)
115:                 padding(packed.data() + input, discOffset + output, result.data() + output, length);
116:             input += 68;
117:         }
118:         else
119:         {
120:             require(length <= packed.size() - input);
121:             if (length)
122:                 std::memcpy(result.data() + output, packed.data() + input, length);
123:             input += length;
124:         }
125:         output += length;
126:     }
127:     require(output == expected);
128:     return result;
129: }
130: 
131: struct StreamCloser
132: {
133:     void operator()(intfstream_t* stream) const
134:     {
135:         if (stream)
136:         {
137:             intfstream_close(stream);
138:             std::free(stream);
139:         }
140:     }
141: };
142: struct Raw
143: {
144:     uint64_t offset, end, aligned;
145:     uint32_t first, count;
146: };
147: struct Group
148: {
149:     uint64_t offset;
150:     uint32_t size, packed;
151:     bool compressed;
152: };
153: class Reader
154: {
155: public:
156:     explicit Reader(const char* path) : stream(intfstream_open_file(path,
157:         RETRO_VFS_FILE_ACCESS_READ, RETRO_VFS_FILE_ACCESS_HINT_NONE))
158:     {
159:         require(stream != nullptr);
160:         const int64_t size = intfstream_get_size(stream.get());
161:         require(size >= 0x48 + 0xd5 && uint64_t(size) <= MaxFile);
162:         fileSize = static_cast<uint64_t>(size);
163:         const Bytes header = physical(0, 0x48);
164:         require(be32(header.data()) == 0x52565a01);
165:         const uint32_t version = be32(header.data() + 4);
166:         const uint32_t compatible = be32(header.data() + 8);
167:         require(version >= 0x00030000 && version <= 0x01000000 &&
168:             compatible >= 0x00030000 && compatible <= version);
169:         require(sha1Matches(header.data(), 0x34, header.data() + 0x34));
170:         discSize = be64(header.data() + 0x24);
171:         require(discSize >= 0x2460 && discSize <= MaxDisc &&
172:             be64(header.data() + 0x2c) == fileSize);
173:         const uint32_t secondSize = be32(header.data() + 0xc);
174:         require(secondSize >= 0xd5 && secondSize <= 4096);
175:         const Bytes second = physical(0x48, secondSize);
176:         require(sha1Matches(second.data(), second.size(), header.data() + 0x10));
177:         require(be32(second.data()) == 1 && be32(second.data() + 0x90) == 0);
178:         compression = be32(second.data() + 4);
179:         require((compression == 0 || compression == 5) && second[0xd4] == 0);
180:         chunk = be32(second.data() + 0xc);
181:         require(chunk >= Sector && chunk <= MaxChunk && (chunk & (chunk - 1)) == 0);
182:         std::copy_n(second.data() + 0x10, head.size(), head.begin());
183:         require(be32(head.data() + 0x1c) == 0xc2339f3d);
184:         const uint32_t rawCount = be32(second.data() + 0xb4);
185:         const uint32_t groupCount = be32(second.data() + 0xc4);
186:         require(rawCount && rawCount <= MaxRaw && groupCount && groupCount <= MaxGroups);
187:         Bytes table = decode(be64(second.data() + 0xc8), be32(second.data() + 0xd0),
188:             groupCount * 12, compression == 5);
189:         groups.reserve(groupCount);
190:         for (uint32_t i = 0; i < groupCount; ++i)
191:         {
192:             const uint8_t* p = table.data() + size_t(i) * 12;
193:             const uint32_t flags = be32(p + 4);
194:             Group group{uint64_t(be32(p)) * 4, flags & 0x7fffffff, be32(p + 8),
195:                 (flags & 0x80000000) != 0};
196:             require(group.size <= MaxWork && group.packed <= MaxWork);
197:             require(group.offset <= fileSize && group.size <= fileSize - group.offset);
198:             groups.push_back(group);
199:         }
200:         table = decode(be64(second.data() + 0xb8), be32(second.data() + 0xc0),
201:             rawCount * 24, compression == 5);
202:         raw.reserve(rawCount);
203:         for (uint32_t i = 0; i < rawCount; ++i)
204:         {
205:             const uint8_t* p = table.data() + size_t(i) * 24;
206:             const uint64_t offset = be64(p), length = be64(p + 8);
207:             require(offset < discSize && length && length <= discSize - offset);
208:             Raw entry{offset, offset + length, offset - offset % Sector,
209:                 be32(p + 16), be32(p + 20)};
210:             require(entry.first < groups.size() && entry.count <= groups.size() - entry.first);
211:             require(entry.count == (entry.end - entry.aligned + chunk - 1) / chunk);
212:             raw.push_back(entry);
213:         }
214:         std::sort(raw.begin(), raw.end(), [](const Raw& a, const Raw& b) { return a.offset < b.offset; });
215:         uint64_t end = raw.front().offset;
216:         require(end == 0 || end == head.size());
217:         for (const Raw& entry : raw)
218:         {
219:             require(entry.offset == end);
220:             end = entry.end;
221:         }
222:         require(end == discSize);
223:     }
224: 
225:     void seek(int64_t offset, int origin)
226:     {
227:         uint64_t base;
228:         if (origin == SEEK_SET) base = 0;
229:         else if (origin == SEEK_CUR) base = position;
230:         else if (origin == SEEK_END) base = discSize;
231:         else { failed = true; return; }
232:         if (offset < 0)
233:         {
234:             if (offset < -static_cast<int64_t>(base)) { failed = true; return; }
235:             position = base - static_cast<uint64_t>(-offset);
236:         }
237:         else
238:         {
239:             if (static_cast<uint64_t>(offset) > discSize - base) { failed = true; return; }
240:             position = base + static_cast<uint64_t>(offset);
241:         }
242:     }
243:     int64_t tell() const { return static_cast<int64_t>(position); }
244:     size_t read(void* destination, size_t requested)
245:     {
246:         if (failed || requested > discSize - position || requested > 128 * 1024 * 1024 - totalRead)
247:         {
248:             failed = true;
249:             return 0;
250:         }
251:         totalRead += requested;
252:         auto* out = static_cast<uint8_t*>(destination);
253:         size_t done = 0;
254:         while (done < requested)
255:         {
256:             if (position < head.size())
257:             {
258:                 const size_t take = std::min(requested - done, head.size() - size_t(position));
259:                 std::memcpy(out + done, head.data() + position, take);
260:                 position += take;
261:                 done += take;
262:                 continue;
263:             }
264:             const auto found = std::upper_bound(raw.begin(), raw.end(), position,
265:                 [](uint64_t value, const Raw& entry) { return value < entry.offset; });
266:             require(found != raw.begin());
267:             const Raw& entry = *(found - 1);
268:             require(position < entry.end);
269:             const uint64_t groupBase = entry.aligned + (position - entry.aligned) / chunk * chunk;
270:             const uint32_t index = entry.first + uint32_t((position - entry.aligned) / chunk);
271:             const size_t length = static_cast<size_t>(std::min(uint64_t(chunk), entry.end - groupBase));
272:             if (cachedIndex != index || cachedBase != groupBase || cache.size() != length)
273:             {
274:                 const Group& group = groups[index];
275:                 Bytes decoded;
276:                 if (!group.size)
277:                 {
278:                     accountDecoded(length);
279:                     decoded.assign(length, 0);
280:                 }
281:                 else
282:                 {
283:                     decoded = decode(group.offset, group.size,
284:                         group.packed ? group.packed : length, group.compressed && compression == 5);
285:                     if (group.packed)
286:                     {
287:                         accountDecoded(length);
288:                         decoded = unpack(decoded, length, groupBase);
289:                     }
290:                 }
291:                 cache = std::move(decoded);
292:                 cachedIndex = index;
293:                 cachedBase = groupBase;
294:             }
295:             const size_t into = static_cast<size_t>(position - groupBase);
296:             const size_t take = std::min(requested - done, cache.size() - into);
297:             require(take != 0);
298:             std::memcpy(out + done, cache.data() + into, take);
299:             done += take;
300:             position += take;
301:         }
302:         return done;
303:     }
304:     bool failed = false;
305: 
306: private:
307:     Bytes physical(uint64_t offset, size_t bytes)
308:     {
309:         require(bytes <= MaxWork && offset <= fileSize && bytes <= fileSize - offset);
310:         require(intfstream_seek(stream.get(), static_cast<int64_t>(offset), SEEK_SET) >= 0);
311:         Bytes result(bytes);
312:         require(intfstream_read(stream.get(), result.data(), bytes) == static_cast<int64_t>(bytes));
313:         return result;
314:     }
315:     void accountDecoded(size_t bytes)
316:     {
317:         require(bytes <= MaxWork && bytes <= 512 * 1024 * 1024 - totalDecoded);
318:         totalDecoded += bytes;
319:     }
320:     Bytes decode(uint64_t offset, uint32_t bytes, size_t expected, bool compressed)
321:     {
322:         accountDecoded(expected);
323:         Bytes input = physical(offset, bytes);
324:         if (!compressed)
325:         {
326:             require(input.size() == expected);
327:             return input;
328:         }
329:         require(!input.empty());
330:         const size_t frameSize = ZSTD_findFrameCompressedSize(input.data(), input.size());
331:         require(!ZSTD_isError(frameSize) && frameSize == input.size());
332:         Bytes output(expected);
333:         const size_t actual = ZSTD_decompress(output.data(), output.size(), input.data(), input.size());
334:         require(!ZSTD_isError(actual) && actual == expected);
335:         return output;
336:     }
337:     std::unique_ptr<intfstream_t, StreamCloser> stream;
338:     uint64_t fileSize = 0, discSize = 0, position = 0;
339:     uint32_t chunk = 0, compression = 0;
340:     std::array<uint8_t, 128> head{};
341:     std::vector<Raw> raw;
342:     std::vector<Group> groups;
343:     Bytes cache;
344:     uint32_t cachedIndex = UINT32_MAX;
345:     uint64_t cachedBase = 0;
346:     size_t totalRead = 0, totalDecoded = 0;
347: };
348: 
349: void* openRvz(const char* path)
350: {
351:     try { return new Reader(path); }
352:     catch (...) { return nullptr; }
353: }
354: void closeRvz(void* opaque) { delete static_cast<Reader*>(opaque); }
355: void seekRvz(void* opaque, int64_t offset, int origin)
356: {
357:     static_cast<Reader*>(opaque)->seek(offset, origin);
358: }
359: int64_t tellRvz(void* opaque) { return static_cast<Reader*>(opaque)->tell(); }
360: size_t readRvz(void* opaque, void* data, size_t bytes)
361: {
362:     Reader& reader = *static_cast<Reader*>(opaque);
363:     try { return reader.read(data, bytes); }
364:     catch (...) { reader.failed = true; return 0; }
365: }
366: struct Iterator
367: {
368:     explicit Iterator(const char* path) { rc_hash_initialize_iterator(&value, path, nullptr, 0); }
369:     ~Iterator() { rc_hash_destroy_iterator(&value); }
370:     rc_hash_iterator_t value;
371: };
372: } // namespace
373: 
374: bool generateGameCubeRvzHash(char hash[33], const char* path)
375: {
376:     hash[0] = '\0';
377:     try
378:     {
379:         Iterator iterator(path);
380:         if (!iterator.value.path)
381:             return false;
382:         iterator.value.callbacks.filereader = {openRvz, seekRvz, tellRvz, readRvz, closeRvz};
383:         if (rc_hash_generate(hash, RC_CONSOLE_GAMECUBE, &iterator.value))
384:             return true;
385:     }
386:     catch (...) {}
387:     hash[0] = '\0';
388:     return false;
389: }
~~~~
