35: static bool retryGameCubeRvzHash(FileData* game)
36: {
37:     if (!gameCubeRvzHashEnabled() || game->getSystem() == nullptr)
38:         return false;
39:     const auto& platforms = game->getSystem()->getPlatformIds();
40:     // A mixed-platform system can select another console in getCheevosHash.
41:     // Keep the repair confined to the unambiguous GameCube mapping.
42:     const bool gameCubeOnly = platforms.size() == 1 &&
43:         platforms.count(PlatformIds::NINTENDO_GAMECUBE) != 0;
44:     return GameCubeRvzHashRetry::needed(true, gameCubeOnly,
45:         Utils::FileSystem::getExtension(game->getPath()),
46:         game->getMetadata(MetaDataId::CheevosHash), game->getMetadata(MetaDataId::CheevosId));
47: }

49: ThreadedHasher::ThreadedHasher(Window* window, HasherType type, std::queue<FileData*> searchQueue, const std::vector<FileData*>& lookupOnly, bool forceAllGames)
50: 	: mWindow(window), mWndNotification(nullptr)
51: {
52: 	mForce = forceAllGames;
53: 	mExit = false;
54: 	mType = type;
55: 	mCheevosIndexed = false;
56: 	mThreadCount = 0;
57: 
58: 	mSearchQueue = searchQueue;
59: 	mTotal = mSearchQueue.size();
60: 
61: 	if ((mType & HASH_CHEEVOS_MD5) == HASH_CHEEVOS_MD5)
62: 	{
63: 		try
64: 		{
65: 			mCheevosHashes = RetroAchievements::getCheevosHashes();
66: 			if (mCheevosHashes.size() == 0)
67: 			{
68: 				// Offline, or not yet: nothing can be identified without it. Said
69: 				// once, so a boot that indexed nothing can be told apart from one
70: 				// that never tried (fork #183); the link-up run tries again.
71: 				LOG(LogWarning) << "ThreadedHasher: RetroAchievements' hash library did not come; nothing is indexed this run";
72: 				// The games that waited for it are said to be waiting (fork
73: 				// #299, D-UI-104): the toast, and the control script's marker
74: 				// so the link's return looks them up.
75: 				if (!mSearchQueue.empty())
76: 					ProxyCards::indexRanOffline(mWindow, (int) mSearchQueue.size());
77: 				while (!mSearchQueue.empty())
78: 					mSearchQueue.pop();
79: 				// And nothing is counted: mTotal was taken from the queue
80: 				// above, so it stayed at the games that were waiting. A card
81: 				// and threads were made for an empty queue, the destructor
82: 				// toasted INDEXING COMPLETED beside the offline card, and the
83: 				// threads -- ending at once -- could delete this before
84: 				// start() read it back (#308 1-raoffline claude F-RA-05).
85: 				// With 0, the check below returns before any of that.
86: 				mTotal = 0;
87: 			}
88: 			else
89: 				sCheevosLibraryCame = true;
90: 
91: 			// The games with a hash and no id (audit #186 PL-08): the ones
92: 			// the library knows join the queue for a lookup and a save, and
93: 			// nothing else -- their ROM is never read again. RC-5 left every
94: 			// game indexed in a session that ended with a reboot in this
95: 			// state (fork #183): the hash in its recovery file, the id
96: 			// decided afterwards in memory and lost. The ones the library
97: 			// does not know stay as they are, so a library of games without
98: 			// a set costs no card and no toast at every startup.
99: 			for (FileData* game : lookupOnly)
100: 			{
101: 				const std::string hash = Utils::String::toUpper(game->getMetadata(MetaDataId::CheevosHash));
102: 				if (mCheevosHashes.find(hash) == mCheevosHashes.cend())
103: 					continue;
104: 				mSearchQueue.push(game);
105: 				mLookupOnly.insert(game);
106: 				mTotal++;
107: 			}
108: 			mCheevosIndexed = !mCheevosHashes.empty() && !mSearchQueue.empty();
109: 		}
110: 		catch (const std::exception& e)
111: 		{
112: 			// The fetch failed outright -- offline, nearly always -- with the
113: 			// same games waiting: the same word to the player (fork #299).
114: 			if (!mSearchQueue.empty())
115: 				ProxyCards::indexRanOffline(mWindow, (int) mSearchQueue.size());
116: 			mType = (HasherType)0;
117: 			throw e;
118: 		}
119: 	}
120: 
121: 	// A run with nothing to do -- lookups alone, and the library knew none
122: 	// of them -- shows no card and starts no thread; start() deletes it.
123: 	if (mTotal == 0)
124: 		return;
125: 
126: 	mWndNotification = mWindow->createAsyncNotificationComponent();
127: 
128: 	if (mType == HASH_CHEEVOS_MD5)
129: 		mWndNotification->updateTitle(ICONINDEX + _("SEARCHING RETROACHIEVEMENTS"));
130: 	else 
131: 		mWndNotification->updateTitle(ICONINDEX + _("SEARCHING NETPLAY GAMES"));
132: 
133: 	int num_threads = std::thread::hardware_concurrency() / 2;
134: 	if (num_threads == 0)
135: 		num_threads = 1;
136: 
137: 	mThreadCount = num_threads;
138: 	for (size_t i = 0; i < num_threads; i++)
139: 		mThreads.push_back(new std::thread(&ThreadedHasher::run, this));
140: }
141: 
142: ThreadedHasher::~ThreadedHasher()
143: {
144: 	if ((mType & HASH_CHEEVOS_MD5) == HASH_CHEEVOS_MD5 && mTotal > 0)
145: 	{
146: 		mWindow->displayNotificationMessage(ICONINDEX + _("INDEXING COMPLETED") + std::string(". ") + _("UPDATE GAMELISTS TO APPLY CHANGES."));
147: 
148: 		// The offline cache follows this index (fork #184, D-RA-013): once
149: 		// the games are identified, and while the device is still connected
150: 		// (the hash library just came from RetroAchievements), the games not
151: 		// yet cached for offline play are cached from the ids and hashes
152: 		// written above -- raofflineproxy-ctl topup --after-index, from a
153: 		// thread of its own, with a card once it has work (fork #293). Not
154: 		// after a run the player stopped, and not after one that identified
155: 		// nothing.
156: 		if (!mExit && mCheevosIndexed)
157: 			OfflineAchievements::topUpAfterIndex(mWindow);
158: 	}
159: 
160: 	if (mWndNotification != nullptr)
161: 		mWndNotification->close();
162: 	mWndNotification = nullptr;
163: 
164: 	ThreadedHasher::mInstance = nullptr;
165: }
166: 
167: std::string ThreadedHasher::formatGameName(FileData* game)
168: {
169: 	return "[" + game->getSystemName() + "] " + game->getName();
170: }
171: 
172: void ThreadedHasher::updateUI(const std::string label)
173: {
174: 	std::string idx = std::to_string(mTotal + 1 - mSearchQueue.size()) + "/" + std::to_string(mTotal);
175: 	int percent = 100 - (mSearchQueue.size() * 100 / mTotal);
176: 		
177: 	mWndNotification->updateText(label);
178: 	mWndNotification->updatePercent(percent);	
179: }
180: 
181: void ThreadedHasher::run()
182: {
183: 	std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(mLoaderLock);
184: 
185: 	bool cheevos = ((mType & HASH_CHEEVOS_MD5) == HASH_CHEEVOS_MD5);
186: 	bool netplay = ((mType & HASH_NETPLAY_CRC) == HASH_NETPLAY_CRC);
187: 
188: 	while (!mExit && !mSearchQueue.empty())
189: 	{
190: 		FileData* game = mSearchQueue.front();
191: 
192: 		auto label = formatGameName(game);
193: 
194: 		LOG(LogDebug) << "Hashing " << formatGameName(game);
195: 		updateUI(label);
196: 
197: 		mSearchQueue.pop();
198: 
199: 		lock.unlock();
200: 
201: 		if (mPaused)
202: 		{
203: 			while (!mExit && mPaused)
204: 			{
205: 				std::this_thread::yield();
206: 				std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(500));
207: 			}
208: 		}		
209: 
210: 		// A game here for a lookup alone is not read: not its CRC, not its
211: 		// hash (checkCheevosHash returns on the hash it has; checkCrc32 would
212: 		// not, in a system netplay never asked for).
213: 		const bool lookupOnly = mLookupOnly.count(game) > 0;
214: 
215: 		if (netplay && !lookupOnly)
216: 		{
217: 			LOG(LogDebug) << "CheckCrc32 : " << label;
218: 			game->checkCrc32(mForce);
219: 		}
220: 
221: 		if (cheevos)
222: 		{
223: 			LOG(LogDebug) << "CheckCheevosHash : " << label;
224: 			if (!lookupOnly)
225: 				// Queue eligibility alone is insufficient: FileData skips every
226: 				// stored hash unless forced. Force only this failed RVZ entry.
227: 				game->checkCheevosHash(mForce || retryGameCubeRvzHash(game));
228: 
229: 			auto hash = Utils::String::toUpper(game->getMetadata(MetaDataId::CheevosHash));
230: 			if (!hash.empty())
231: 			{
232: 				// The id reaches the disk with the hash. checkCheevosHash
233: 				// saved the game to the recovery folder with the hash and no
234: 				// id, because the id is decided here, afterwards, and until
235: 				// now it lived in memory until the gamelist was written at
236: 				// exit. The offline cache's scan reads that folder for the
237: 				// index (fork #184, D-RA-013), so a changed id is saved the
238: 				// same way.
239: 				const std::string before = game->getMetadata(MetaDataId::CheevosId);
240: 				auto cheevos = mCheevosHashes.find(hash);
241: 				const std::string id = cheevos != mCheevosHashes.cend() ? cheevos->second : std::string();
242: 				game->setMetadata(MetaDataId::CheevosId, id);
243: 				if (id != before)
244: 				{
245: 					saveToGamelistRecovery(game);
246: 					if (lookupOnly)
247: 						LOG(LogInfo) << "ThreadedHasher: id " << id << " for " << label << " from the hash library, no file read (audit #186 PL-08)";
248: 				}
249: 			}
250: 
251: 			LOG(LogDebug) << "CheckCheevosHash OK : " << label;;
252: 		}		
253: 
254: 		lock.lock();
255: 	}
256: 
257: 	mThreadCount--;
258: 
259: 	if (mThreadCount == 0)
260: 	{
261: 		lock.unlock();
262: 		delete this;
263: 		ThreadedHasher::mInstance = nullptr;
264: 
265: 	}
266: }
267: 
268: bool ThreadedHasher::checkCloseIfRunning(Window* window)
269: {
270: 	if (ThreadedHasher::mInstance != nullptr)
271: 	{
272: 		window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window, _("GAME HASHING IS RUNNING. DO YOU WANT TO STOP IT?"), _("YES"), []
273: 		{
274: 			ThreadedHasher::stop();
275: 		}, _("NO"), nullptr));
276: 
277: 		return false;
278: 	}
279: 
280: 	return true;
281: }
282: 
283: void ThreadedHasher::start(Window* window, HasherType type, bool forceAllGames, bool silent, std::set<std::string>* systems)
284: {
285: 	if (ThreadedHasher::mInstance != nullptr)
286: 	{
287: 		if (silent)
288: 			return;
289: 
290: 		if (!checkCloseIfRunning(window))
291: 			return;
292: 	}
293: 	
294: 	std::queue<FileData*> searchQueue;
295: 	// The games with a hash and no id: a lookup against the library once it
296: 	// has come, no read (CheevosIndex, audit #186 PL-08).
297: 	std::vector<FileData*> lookupOnly;
298: 	
299: 	for (auto sys : SystemData::sSystemVector)
300: 	{
301: 		if (sys->isGroupSystem() || sys->isCollection())
302: 			continue;
303: 
304: 		bool takeNetplay = ((type & HASH_NETPLAY_CRC) == HASH_NETPLAY_CRC) && sys->isNetplaySupported();
305: 		bool takeCheevos = ((type & HASH_CHEEVOS_MD5) == HASH_CHEEVOS_MD5) && sys->isCheevosSupported();
306: 
307: 		if (!takeNetplay && !takeCheevos)
308: 			continue;
309: 
310: 		if (systems != nullptr && systems->find(sys->getName()) == systems->cend())
311: 			continue;
312: 		
313: 		if (!sys->isGameSystem() || sys->getRootFolder() == nullptr)
314: 			continue;
315: 
316: 		if (sys->isGroupChildSystem() ? sys->isHidden() : !sys->isVisible())
317: 			continue;
318: 
319: 		for (auto file : sys->getRootFolder()->getFilesRecursive(GAME))
320: 		{
321: 			bool netPlay = takeNetplay && (forceAllGames || file->getMetadata(MetaDataId::Crc32).empty());
322: 			CheevosIndex::Take take = CheevosIndex::Take::None;
323: 			if (takeCheevos)
324: 			{
325: 				take = CheevosIndex::take(forceAllGames, file->getMetadata(MetaDataId::CheevosHash), file->getMetadata(MetaDataId::CheevosId));
326: 				if (retryGameCubeRvzHash(file))
327: 					take = CheevosIndex::Take::Hash;
328: 			}
329: 			bool cheevos = take == CheevosIndex::Take::Hash;
330: 
331: 			if (cheevos)
332: 			{
333: 				std::string ext = Utils::String::toLower(Utils::FileSystem::getExtension(file->getPath()));
334: 				
335: 				if (ext == ".pbp" || ext == ".cso") // Currently unsupported formats
336: 					cheevos = false;
337: 			}
338: 
339: 			if (netPlay || cheevos)
340: 				searchQueue.push(file);
341: 			else if (take == CheevosIndex::Take::Lookup)
342: 				lookupOnly.push_back(file);
343: 		}
344: 	}
345: 
346: 	if (searchQueue.size() == 0 && lookupOnly.empty())
347: 	{
348: 		if (!silent)
349: 			window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window, _("NO GAMES FIT THAT CRITERIA.")));
350: 
351: 		return;
352: 	}
353: 
354: 	// Lookups alone, from the silent startup run: once a day at most
355: 	// (CheevosIndex::lookupDue) -- the pass costs the hash library, and
356: 	// nearly every library has a game RetroAchievements does not know whose
357: 	// hash would ask for it at every boot. A run the player asked for, or
358: 	// one with games to hash (the library comes anyway), is not held back.
359: 	// The stamp is written once the library has come, so an offline boot
360: 	// does not spend the day's pass on a fetch that failed.
361: 	const bool lookupsAlone = searchQueue.empty() && silent && ((type & HASH_CHEEVOS_MD5) == HASH_CHEEVOS_MD5);
362: 	const long long now = (long long) time(nullptr);
363: 	if (lookupsAlone)
364: 	{
365: 		const long long last = strtoll(Settings::getInstance()->getString("CheevosLookupOnlyLast").c_str(), nullptr, 10);
366: 		if (!CheevosIndex::lookupDue(last, now))
367: 		{
368: 			LOG(LogInfo) << "ThreadedHasher: " << lookupOnly.size() << " game(s) with a hash and no id; the lookup pass ran within the day, next one later";
369: 			return;
370: 		}
371: 	}
372: 
373: 	try
374: 	{
375: 		ThreadedHasher* hasher = new ThreadedHasher(window, type, searchQueue, lookupOnly, forceAllGames);
376: 		// Stamped only when the library came: a boot without it has not spent
377: 		// the day's pass (the comment above promised as much; the code did not).
378: 		if (lookupsAlone && !hasher->mCheevosHashes.empty())
379: 		{
380: 			Settings::getInstance()->setString("CheevosLookupOnlyLast", std::to_string(now));
381: 			Settings::getInstance()->saveFile();
382: 		}
383: 		if (hasher->mTotal == 0)
384: 		{
385: 			// Lookups alone and the library knew none of them: nothing was
386: 			// started, nothing is shown -- as a run with nothing to hash.
387: 			delete hasher;
388: 			if (!silent)
389: 				window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window, _("NO GAMES FIT THAT CRITERIA.")));
390: 			return;
391: 		}
392: 		ThreadedHasher::mInstance = hasher;
393: 	}
394: 	catch (const std::exception& e)
395: 	{
396: 		LOG(LogError) << "Game Hash failed : " << e.what();
397: 
398: 		if (!silent)
399: 			window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window, e.what()));
400: 	}	
401: }
402: 
403: void ThreadedHasher::stop()
404: {
405: 	auto thread = ThreadedHasher::mInstance;
406: 	if (thread == nullptr)
407: 		return;
408: 
409: 	try
410: 	{
411: 		thread->mExit = true;
412: 	}
413: 	catch (...) {}
414: }
415: 
~~~~

9: loader=importlib.machinery.SourceFileLoader('device_state_reader',str(checker));spec=importlib.util.spec_from_loader(loader.name,loader);m=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec);loader.exec_module(m)

11: 
12: def link_chain(fs,path):
13:  pending=list(PurePosixPath(path).parts[1:]);done=[];links=[]
14:  while pending:
15:   part=pending.pop(0)
16:   if part in ('','.'):continue
17:   if part=='..':assert done;done.pop();continue
18:   candidate='/'+ '/'.join(done+[part]);target=fs.link(candidate)
19:   if target is not None:
20:    links.append({'path':candidate,'target':target});assert len(links)<=40
21:    target_path=PurePosixPath(target)
22:    if target_path.is_absolute():done=[];pending=list(target_path.parts[1:])+pending
23:    else:pending=list(target_path.parts)+pending
24:   else:done.append(part)
25:  return {'resolved':'/'+'/'.join(done),'links':links}

1: #!/usr/bin/env python3
2: """Owned qa19/20 continuation; existing tools/cases unchanged, SOURCE-ONLY UNRUN."""
3: import ctypes,hashlib,json,os,re,signal,socket,subprocess,sys,time
4: from pathlib import Path
5: R=Path(__file__).resolve().parent;C=json.loads((R/'config.json').read_text());TREE=Path(C['checkout'])
6: assert not (R/'result.json').exists(),'fresh owner required'
7: result={'rc':1,'started':time.time(),'commands':[],'reports':[],'qemu':[],'backend_owners':{},'cleanup_errors':[]};seq=0;next_sample=0;owned={};attempted=[];pair_attempted=False
8: STANDARD=['scripts','lifetime','wrapper','vocabulary','french','quoting','menumap','register','pair-identity','fresh','round-trip','exit','time-to-play','walks','frame-diff']
9: def sha(p):
10:  with p.open('rb') as f:return hashlib.file_digest(f,'sha256').hexdigest()
11: def write(p,v):
12:  with p.open('x') as f:json.dump(v,f,indent=2);f.write('\n')
13: assert sha(R/'census.py')==C['code']['census.py'];sys.path.insert(0,str(R))
14: from census import direct_children,signal_child,proc_identity
15: BASE={k:v for k,v in os.environ.items() if not k.startswith('CLOUD_QA_')}
16: BASE.update(LAST_GOOD_SOURCE_ROOT=str(TREE),TMPDIR=str(R/'tmp'),VM_PAIR_DIR=str(R/'pair'),VM_QEMU_RUNTIME_DIR=str(R/'runtime'),VM_GL='auto',ES_SRC=C['es_source'],RETROARCH_SRC=C['retroarch_source'],QA_SYSTEM_ROOT=C['system_root'],WALK_BASELINE=C['baseline'],PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE='1')
17: def env(backend):return dict(BASE,CLOUD_QA_BACKEND=backend,CLOUD_QA_STATE=str(R/'cloud'/backend),CLOUD_QA_NAME=C['minio_name'],CLOUD_QA_IMAGE=C['minio_image'],CLOUD_QA_USER='qauser',CLOUD_QA_PASS='qa-password',CLOUD_QA_BIND='127.0.0.1',CLOUD_QA_GUEST_HOST='10.0.2.2',CLOUD_QA_DEAD_PORT='9011',ROCKNIX_ARTIFACTS=str(R/'artifacts'/backend))
18: def proc(pid):
19:  row=proc_identity(pid)
20:  if row is None:return None
21:  try:
22:   stat=Path('/proc',str(pid),'stat').read_bytes().rpartition(b')')[2].split()
23:   if stat[0]==b'Z':return None
24:   row['argv']=[x.decode(errors='replace') for x in Path('/proc',str(pid),'cmdline').read_bytes().split(b'\0') if x]
25:   return row
26:  except (FileNotFoundError,ProcessLookupError):return None
27: def same(row):
28:  now=proc(row['pid']);return now if now and now['start_ticks']==row['start_ticks'] else None
29: def managed(row):
30:  # Existing vm-pair and backend helpers stop these exact long-lived owners.
31:  args=row.get('argv',[])
32:  if args and Path(args[0]).name.startswith('qemu-system-') and any('if=none,id=rocknix,format=qcow2,file='+str(R/'pair'/('vm-'+g+'.qcow2')) in args for g in ['a','b']):return 'qemu'
33:  if str(R/'cloud/webdav/data') in args and 'serve' in args and 'webdav' in args:return 'webdav'
34:  if any(str(R/'cloud/sftp/sftp/sshd_config') in a.split() for a in args) and any('sshd' in a for a in args):return 'sftp'
35:  return None
36: def observe(active):
37:  global next_sample
38:  # Adopted helper exits must become absent before the existing kill-0 checks.
39:  # Never reap Popen's stage: poll()/wait() own that original status. WNOHANG
40:  # collects only terminated children; no signal or blocking wait occurs here.
41:  for identity in direct_children(os.getpid()):
42:   if identity['pid']==active.pid:continue
43:   try:pid,status=os.waitpid(identity['pid'],os.WNOHANG)
44:   except ChildProcessError:continue
45:   if pid:
46:    receipt=dict(identity,wait_status=status,returncode=os.waitstatus_to_exitcode(status),phase='observation',epoch=time.time())
47:    result.setdefault('reaped_children',[]).append(receipt)
48:    with (R/'records/adopted-exits.jsonl').open('a') as f:f.write(json.dumps(receipt)+'\n')
49:  children=direct_children(os.getpid())
50:  for identity in children:
51:   row=proc(identity['pid'])
52:   if row:owned[(row['pid'],row['start_ticks'])]=row
53:  if time.monotonic()<next_sample:return
54:  files=[f for root in [R/'records',R/'artifacts'] for f in root.rglob('*') if f.is_file() and not f.is_symlink()]
55:  total=0;vanished=0
56:  for f in files:
57:   try:total+=f.stat().st_size
58:   except FileNotFoundError:vanished+=1
59:  assert total<=C['record_limit_bytes'] and len(files)<=4096,'original records bound'
60:  sample={'epoch':time.time(),'vanished_during_record_sample':vanished,'record_bytes':total,'record_files':len(files),'direct_children':children,'meminfo':Path('/proc/meminfo').read_text(),'memory_pressure':Path('/proc/pressure/memory').read_text(),'io_pressure':Path('/proc/pressure/io').read_text(),'filesystem_available_bytes':os.statvfs(R).f_bavail*os.statvfs(R).f_frsize,'qemu_status':{}}
61:  for row in owned.values():
62:   if managed(row)=='qemu' and same(row):sample['qemu_status'][str(row['pid'])]=Path('/proc',str(row['pid']),'status').read_text()
63:  with (R/'records/resources.jsonl').open('a') as f:f.write(json.dumps(sample)+'\n')
64:  next_sample=time.monotonic()+5
65: 
66: def reap(active=None):
67:  deadline=time.monotonic()+13;grace=time.monotonic()+2;interventions=[]
68:  while True:
69:   if active:active.poll()
70:   for identity in direct_children(os.getpid()):
71:    row=proc(identity['pid'])
72:    if row and managed(row):continue # never signal a live VM/backend through generic cleanup
73:    if active and identity['pid']==active.pid:continue
74:    try:pid,status=os.waitpid(identity['pid'],os.WNOHANG)
75:    except ChildProcessError:continue
76:    if pid:result.setdefault('reaped_children',[]).append({'pid':pid,'wait_status':status})
77:   children=[]
78:   for identity in direct_children(os.getpid()):
79:    row=proc(identity['pid'])
80:    if row and managed(row):continue
81:    children.append(identity)
82:   if not children:return interventions
83:   assert time.monotonic()<deadline,'adopted host cleanup deadline'
84:   if not active and time.monotonic()<grace:time.sleep(.05);continue
85:   for identity in children:
86:    row=proc(identity['pid'])
87:    if row:owned[(row['pid'],row['start_ticks'])]=row
88:    action=signal_child(identity,os.getpid())
89:    if action['outcome']=='pidfd-kill':interventions.append(action);result.setdefault('forced_child_cleanup',[]).append(action)
90:   time.sleep(.02)
91: def call(name,args,seconds,backend='webdav',check=True):
92:  global seq
93:  seq+=1;stem=R/'records'/('%03d-%s'%(seq,name));row={'name':name,'argv':list(map(str,args)),'backend':backend,'timeout_seconds':seconds,'timed_out':False,'original_returncode':None};result['commands'].append(row);child=None
94:  try:
95:   with Path(str(stem)+'.stdout').open('xb') as out,Path(str(stem)+'.stderr').open('xb') as err:
96:    child=subprocess.Popen(row['argv'],cwd=TREE,env=env(backend),stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,stdout=out,stderr=err,start_new_session=True);row['pid']=child.pid;deadline=time.monotonic()+seconds
97:    while child.poll() is None:
98:     observe(child);assert out.tell()<=16*1024**2 and err.tell()<=16*1024**2,'stage stream bound'
99:     if time.monotonic()>=deadline:row['timed_out']=True;raise TimeoutError(name)
100:     time.sleep(.25)
101:    row['original_returncode']=child.wait()
102:   if check:assert row['original_returncode']==0,name
103:   return Path(str(stem)+'.stdout').read_text()
104:  except BaseException as exc:
105:   row['error']=type(exc).__name__+': '+str(exc)
106:   if child:
107:    try:row['forced_cleanup']=reap(active=child)
108:    finally:row['original_returncode']=child.wait(timeout=3)
109:   raise
110:  finally:write(Path(str(stem)+'.exit.json'),row)
111: def free(port):
112:  with socket.socket() as sock:sock.bind(('127.0.0.1',port))
113: def backend_identity(backend):
114:  if backend=='s3':
115:   obj=json.loads(call('s3-identity',['docker','inspect',C['minio_name']],15,backend))[0]
116:   assert obj['Name']=='/'+C['minio_name'] and obj['Image']==C['minio_image'] and obj['State']['Running']
117:   assert obj['HostConfig']['PortBindings']=={'9000/tcp':[{'HostIp':'127.0.0.1','HostPort':'9012'}]}
118:   row={k:obj[k] for k in ['Id','Name','Image','State','Mounts']}
119:  else:
120:   pidfile=R/'cloud'/backend/('webdav.pid' if backend=='webdav' else 'sftp/sftp.pid');row=proc(int(pidfile.read_text()));assert row and managed(row)==backend
121:  old=result['backend_owners'].get(backend)
122:  if old:assert (row['Id']==old['Id'] if backend=='s3' else row['pid']==old['pid'] and row['start_ticks']==old['start_ticks'])
123:  result['backend_owners'][backend]=row;return row

127:  p=reports[0];text=p.read_text();assert text.startswith('# vm-qa on '+C['commit'][:10]+'\n')
128:  rows=[]

176:  for backend in ['webdav','sftp','s3']:
177:   attempted.append(backend)
178:   call(backend+'-up',[TREE/'tools/cloud-test-backend','up'],C['bounds_seconds']['backend-up'],backend);backend_identity(backend)
179:   qa=R/'harness/tools/vm-qa' if backend=='webdav' else TREE/'tools/vm-qa'
180:   args=[qa,'--skip-up','--only','scripts' if backend=='webdav' else 'round-trip']
181:   if backend!='webdav':args+=['--backend',backend]
182:   args.append(image)
183:   call('standard' if backend=='webdav' else backend,args,C['bounds_seconds']['standard' if backend=='webdav' else backend],backend)
184:   backend_identity(backend);report(backend,['scripts'] if backend=='webdav' else ['round-trip'])
185:  assert sha(image)==C['image']['sha256']
186:  for path,commit in [(TREE,C['commit']),(Path(C['es_source']),C['es_commit'])]:
187:   assert call('source-after',['git','-C',path,'rev-parse','HEAD'],15).strip()==commit
188:   assert not call('source-clean-after',['git','-C',path,'status','--porcelain=v1','--untracked-files=no'],15).strip()
189:  for p,h in C['dependencies'].items():assert sha(Path(p))==h,p
190:  for n,h in C['code'].items():assert sha(R/n)==h,n
191:  for n,target in C['harness_links'].items():assert (R/n).is_symlink() and os.readlink(R/n)==target,n
192:  result['rc']=0
193: except BaseException as exc:result['error']=type(exc).__name__+': '+str(exc)
194: finally:
195:  signal.alarm(C['cleanup_seconds'])
196:  try:
197:   # cloud-round-trip's accepted ControlPersist connection is closed normally.
198:   # These exact key/host/ports belong to this fresh pair; nonzero means already gone.
199:   if pair_attempted and (R/'pair/qa-key').exists():
200:    for port in [10022,10023]:
201:     try:call('ssh-master-exit-'+str(port),['ssh','-i',R/'pair/qa-key','-p',str(port),'-o','BatchMode=yes','-o','ControlPath=/tmp/crt-'+str(os.getuid())+'-%C','-O','exit','root@127.0.0.1'],10,check=False)
202:     except BaseException as exc:result['cleanup_errors'].append('SSH master: '+str(exc))
203:   for backend in reversed(attempted):
204:    try:
205:     # Helpers retain their original down behavior. Scope its numeric PID/port
206:     # sweep to the private owner before invoking it; never stop a shared endpoint.
207:     if backend=='s3':
208:      names=call('s3-pre-down-list',['docker','ps','-a','--format','{{.Names}}'],15,backend).splitlines()
209:      if C['minio_name'] in names:
210:       obj=json.loads(call('s3-pre-down-identity',['docker','inspect',C['minio_name']],15,backend))[0]
211:       assert obj['Image']==C['minio_image'] and (not result['backend_owners'].get('s3') or obj['Id']==result['backend_owners']['s3']['Id'])
212:       result['s3_final_mounts']=obj['Mounts']
213:     else:
214:      pidfile=R/'cloud'/backend/('webdav.pid' if backend=='webdav' else 'sftp/sftp.pid')
215:      if pidfile.exists():
216:       current=proc(int(pidfile.read_text()))
217:       if current:assert managed(current)==backend
218:      port=9010 if backend=='webdav' else 9013
219:      listeners=call(backend+'-listeners',['ss','-ltnp','sport','=',':'+str(port)],10,backend)
220:      for pid in re.findall(r'pid=(\d+)',listeners):assert managed(proc(int(pid)) or {})==backend,'foreign backend listener'
221:      if len(listeners.strip().splitlines())>1:assert re.search(r'pid=\d+',listeners),'listener ownership unavailable'
222:     call(backend+'-down',[TREE/'tools/cloud-test-backend','down'],C['bounds_seconds']['backend-down'],backend)
223:     if backend=='s3':
224:      for mount in result.get('s3_final_mounts',[]):
225:       assert mount['Type']=='volume' and mount['Name'] not in result['prior_docker_volumes'],'refuse shared/non-volume backend data retirement'
226:       call('retire-owned-s3-volume',['docker','volume','rm',mount['Name']],15,backend)
227:    except BaseException as exc:result['cleanup_errors'].append(backend+' stop: '+str(exc))
228:   if pair_attempted:
229:    try:call('pair-down',[TREE/'tools/vm-pair','down'],C['bounds_seconds']['pair-down'])
230:    except BaseException as exc:result['cleanup_errors'].append('pair stop: '+str(exc))
231:   try:
232:    reap();result['remaining_children']=direct_children(os.getpid())
233:    if result['remaining_children']:result['cleanup_errors'].append('remaining owned children')
234:    for port in [10022,10023,5909,5910,9010,9011,9012,9013]:free(port)
235:    names=call('final-container-list',['docker','ps','-a','--format','{{.Names}}'],15).splitlines();assert C['minio_name'] not in names
236:    result['backend_name_absent']=True
237:   except BaseException as exc:result['cleanup_errors'].append('final ownership check: '+str(exc))
238:  except BaseException as exc:result['cleanup_errors'].append('cleanup deadline: '+str(exc))
239:  finally:
240:   signal.alarm(0);result['surviving_owned_processes']=[r for r in owned.values() if same(r)]
241:   result['qemu_still_live']=[r for r in result['qemu'] if same(r)]
242:   if result['cleanup_errors'] or result.get('forced_child_cleanup') or result['surviving_owned_processes'] or result['qemu_still_live']:result['rc']=1
243:   result['ended']=time.time();write(R/'records/process-identities.json',list(owned.values()));write(R/'result.json',result)
244: raise SystemExit(result['rc'])
~~~~

1: Cloud setup - pixelelated __md_scope=new URL("../..",location),__md_hash=e=>[...e].reduce(((e,_)=>(e<<5)-e+_.charCodeAt(0)),0),__md_get=(e,_=localStorage,t=__md_scope)=>JSON.parse(_.getItem(t.pathname+"."+e)),__md_set=(e,_,t=localStorage,a=__md_scope)=>{try{t.setItem(a.pathname+"."+e,JSON.stringify(_))}catch(e){}} Skip to content 0.0.1 is in development. Read the release status . pixelelated Cloud setup Initializing search Home Guides Releases Credits pixelelated Home Guides Guides Cloud setup Cloud setup Table of contents Add a connection Choose your folders Check or create folders Restore settings Manage a saved connection If sign-in does not finish ROM setup BIOS setup Releases Credits Table of contents Add a connection Choose your folders Check or create folders Restore settings Manage a saved connection If sign-in does not finish 0.0.1 preview guide These instructions describe the upcoming release. Earlier builds may have different menus. Cloud setup ¶ Connect your cloud storage to back up and restore files from your handheld. You'll need an internet connection and an account with your chosen provider. Add a connection ¶ Open cloud settings: Press START , then select GAME SETTINGS → CLOUD SETTINGS → MANAGE CLOUD STORAGE . Open your connections: Under CLOUD STORAGE SETUP , select CLOUD CONNECTIONS . This lists the connections saved on this device. Add your provider: Select ADD CONNECTION and choose your provider. Follow the sign-in instructions on the screen. Choose the connection to use: If the new connection is saved without becoming active, select it under CLOUD CONNECTIONS , then choose USE THIS CONNECTION and confirm. The active connection is marked IN USE . Adding the same provider again creates a separate saved connection. It does not replace your existing sign-in. A saved connection may still need you to sign in again; select it and choose CHECK CONNECTION to see whether it answers. Connecting does not create cloud folders or turn on automatic save sync. Choose your folders ¶ From MANAGE CLOUD STORAGE , select CHANGE CLOUD FOLDER . You can change each location separately: Setting Default for a new setup What goes there SAVES FOLDER /pixelelated/Saves Game saves, save states, and screenshots SETTINGS FOLDER /pixelelated/Backups Settings backups ROMS, BIOS, AND GAME CONTENT FOLDER /pixelelated/Content ROMs, BIOS files, and scraped game content An upgrade keeps your existing folder choices. Use the paths shown on your device if they differ from these examples. Changing one path leaves the others in place and does not move your files. Check or create folders ¶ Choose what to check: Return to MANAGE CLOUD STORAGE and select CHECK CLOUD FOLDERS . Select the categories you want to check. Run the check: Select CHECK FOLDERS . The results show what was found at the selected locations. Read the finding: Select SEE INSTRUCTIONS for the expected path and the next step for that category. Create missing folders if needed: Return to the category choices, select CREATE FOLDERS , and review the confirmation. This creates the selected folders and setup notes. It does not add your ROMs or BIOS files. Checking reads the selected folders. It does not reorganize your files or start a transfer. Category choices apply to checking and creating folders; they do not enable automatic sync. Use a computer to add ROMs or add BIOS files , then run CHECK FOLDERS again. A successful folder check does not verify every file's contents or its compatibility with an emulator. Restore settings ¶ From MANAGE CLOUD STORAGE , select BACKUP AND RESTORE → RESTORE FROM THE CLOUD . After the cloud scan, SETTINGS offers the newest compatible settings backup for your device model. The row shows the model name and backup date. Backups from other device models are not offered, even if they are newer. If there is no compatible backup for your model, SETTINGS stays visible but dimmed, with NO SETTINGS BACKUP FROM THIS DEVICE YET . Manage a saved connection ¶ Select a connection under CLOUD CONNECTIONS to see its available actions. Action What happens CHECK CONNECTION Checks whether that saved connection answers USE THIS CONNECTION Changes the active connection after confirmation and keeps your folder paths SIGN IN AGAIN Replaces that connection's sign-in after the new sign-in is verified EDIT CONNECTION Changes the settings supported by that provider REMOVE CONNECTION Removes local access to that connection, leaving cloud files in place Before changing a connection used by automatic save sync, turn automatic sync off. After switching accounts, check your selected folders before the next backup or restore. Removing the active connection leaves no connection in use. If sign-in does not finish ¶ Choose TRY AGAIN to start a fresh sign-in, or CANCEL SIGN-IN to stop. A failed sign-in is not always an expired code. Follow any explanation shown on the screen. A failed or cancelled SIGN IN AGAIN keeps the existing sign-in. Your connection should now be marked IN USE , with folder findings for the categories you checked. var target=document.getElementById(location.hash.slice(1));target&&target.name&&(target.checked=target.name.startsWith("__tabbed_")) Back to top pixelelated · Built on the work of the open-source handheld community. ## Primary retrospective — whole file

# Retrospective — completed M7 handoff (#603)

**Written:** 2026-10-15T11:26:49.366092+00:00. **Frozen record:** cdd92f10605517e789e09d13b6f4ba68573c2617. Scope is the67 criteria in15 closures, not all inherited distro code.

## Architectural assessment

Preservation work is coherent across installer transactions, native-save preparation, scanner lifetime and qualification readers. Steam components share a locked identity-bound journal and bounded staging; each component preserves unrelated prior state and retires only owned work. Dolphin prepares shared save paths before engine launch and publishes achievement sessions atomically. Wi-Fi owns its asynchronous read outside the screen and explicitly joins it on normal frontend shutdown. Tool-only PPSSPP/header/matcher corrections retain product-source identity.

Complexity remains concentrated in the embedded Steam Python transactions and RVZ parser. Their accepted tests cover defined failure and interruption cases; they do not establish arbitrary concurrent Steam-client safety, universal RVZ support or native ARM client execution. No new architectural defect survived source/original inspection.

## Project invariants

Progress preservation is tested by actual old-negative/new-positive manifests for Steam and Dolphin, plus retained storage originals. Account-bearing configuration is excluded by the scoped selected-state checker with planted-positive and malformed controls; no account values are needed in this audit. Restore strips destructive delete flags and selects a compatible archive by device-model label; no timestamp-based multi-device save conflict resolution is introduced. Clean and retained final-image observations remain separate.

| Dolphin launcher × save helper × engine | Real state trees, GC/Wii links/cards and ordinary game saves | TESTED |123 helper/caller/error cases,8 mounted cases, native ordinary/manual save originals. Unknown retained real trees refuse rather than merge/delete. |

## Build versus adopt

Raw captured official upstream metadata/PR records were read before source-disposition prose. ABL change adopts the official archive digest at the same version/URL and verifies member bytes, rather than weakening checksum enforcement. Dolphin HostUrl and proxy startup are distinct upstream capabilities; bounded source work and contribution queue disposition remain recorded under #585. RVZ padding attribution and BSD notice are present. No new implementation, upstream PR or full corresponding-source clearance is produced by this audit; open #528 work belongs to another lane.

## Spec fidelity and already-written state

All literal requirements were preserved, including #575's explicit supported-limitation alternative and #577's documentation-follow-up requirement. Existing Steam trees and aliases, Dolphin retained paths, failed RVZ sentinels, saved Wi-Fi profiles and cloud archive layouts have explicit treatment. This audit does not add owner-game/achievement, physical-device, sudden-power, arbitrary-state or full-native-ARM criteria after the fact.

## Missing artifacts and prescription review

The historical #600 lint original was not available in its evidence packet; source diff alone was insufficient. A root-allocated fresh lint now provides current exact-source stdout/stderr/exit/hash custody (AC08 amendment). The original #196 manual process/stat transcript was not located by bounded raw-artifact searches; accepted exact-source before/after frames, QA ledger, and current launcher controls remain the inherited scope. No contradiction or regression was found, and no raw transcript was invented. These are explicit coverage limits, not claims of newly reproduced historical runs.

No new Critical/High/Medium product finding is established. No fix was made, so no defect-class sibling patch can be silently declared complete. Related transaction paths (runtime,FEX,Proton,client), both Dolphin launchers and both final clean/retained runs were examined as separate occurrences.

## Platform architecture conformance

Tenant-zero, database schema-before-code and network API-first rubric items are not applicable to this immutable distro handoff. Applicable equivalent is source→recipe→installed payload→actual VM behavior, verified above. Dogfooding means disposable GENERIC_X64 originals; no personal device is substituted.

## Retrospective summary

Architectural conformance is HIGH within the named boundaries. Interaction evidence supports the67 literal criteria. Broad release/source custody and external independence remain separate gates. There is no silently added product scope or fresh confirmed defect from this retrospective.

# Audit Analysis — completed M7 handoff (#603)

**Date:** 2026-10-15. **Scope:** Milestone / Independent. **Primary:** OpenAI Codex, exact served model ID not exposed; identity source is developer instruction. **Frozen record:** cdd92f10605517e789e09d13b6f4ba68573c2617. **Products:** VM/H700 f8636de1d105275dbf9420bbabbbc26f79abb10f; SM9aa7affee9d4ad5e69725cab20b6d7ad84d93dea; ES16dbac5211eea7e70db7394372033be2be7df829; website6aac689c6feaa8d4afefbd5a8b2121e5206f14b9.

**Status:** Primary Phases1–4.5 complete. Phase4.6 pending exact packet transfer authorization and both verified Facilitator calls. This audit is not complete; no Phase5/punch-list or completion marker is issued.

## Executive Summary

The primary review independently evaluated67 literal criteria across15 completed issues, reading source, original command results, artifact identities, retained failures and representative exact-source frames before opening prior per-criterion verdicts. All67 are supported within their stated scopes. AC08 initially lacked a retained original lint result; a new root-allocated host lint on exact source returned0 and is recorded as a current check, not a recovered historical result.

No new confirmed product defect survived primary review. The previous audit's frozen scores and six resolved findings are unchanged. Later evidence supports the present handoff: exact ABL/header acceptance, PPSSPP parser discrimination, final installed clean/retained behavior, native state continuation and published compatible-restore guidance. The historical #196 raw manual launch/stat transcript was not located; surviving before/after frames, accepted ledger and current launcher controls are explicitly separated. External independent review remains mandatory and pending; this is not RC/publication approval.

## Code Quality Assessment

Strengths: bounded staged replacement, identity-bound recovery, explicit refusal of unknown retained state, process-owned Wi-Fi work, and exact installed/artifact provenance. Negative controls distinguish actual child failures from wrapper success. Complexity hotspots are Install Steam.sh's embedded transaction code, the RVZ container reader and lifetime interactions in GuiWifi/WifiScanProcess. Coverage limits do not disappear because a suite passes.

## Cornerstone Conformance

Overall HIGH within scope;03-retrospective.md records all30 canonical rule faces,74 blindspot entries (including duplicate-number29) and8 cross-system interactions. Single new host lint was explicitly allocated. No device/cloud/provider action, product edit, build or source-custody sweep occurred in this lane.

## Spec Fidelity

Aligned: all67 literal criteria, same-model restore, known-state preservation, explicit standalone capability limits, and synthetic/installed/native separation. No new standalone integration, physical boot/radio, real account award or universal format claim is introduced. The changed historical verdicts are later evidence/source resolutions; no original failure is rewritten.

## Missing Artifacts

Historical #600 pkgcheck receipt was not retained in the inspected packet; fresh current check remedies present verification without fabricating history. Historical #196 raw manual launch/stat output was not located by the bounded search; exact-source frames and accepted ledger remain. Independent Fable output/provenance does not yet exist and is a completion prerequisite, not an omitted test to call PASS.

## Coverage Boundary

Examined: all67 criteria, current product/tool code at named frozen sources, compact original VM/host/firmware/publication records, selected process/file manifests, raw negative controls, current official metadata snapshots, and representative actual pixels. AC08 alone ran anew: host pkgcheck0, no compile/network. Every other runtime result is an original readback with its own source and timestamp.

Deliberately not examined: entire inherited distribution, bulk firmware caches, open #528 corresponding-source collectors, owner ROMs/accounts/cloud, physical radio/boot/power, universal game/format corpus and concurrent native ARM Steam/FEX/Proton gameplay. Selected storage preservation does not cover every arbitrary file or adjacent backup. No new benchmarks/VM/replays were performed. Historical #196 ledger assertions are not raw launch/stat receipts. External text reviewer will receive no image pixels unless separately authorized; primary visual reads remain identified.

## Finding Verification (Phase4.5)

No new Critical or High finding exists. Refutation covered misleading baseline cleanup, wrapper0 versus child failure, generic ABL bypass, PPSSPP credential false negatives, copied-artifact agreement, synthetic engine versus actual NoGUI, state presence versus visible restoration, and scope changes after prior completion. The initial AC08 evidence shortfall was narrowed to missing historical lint custody, then independently resolved for current verification by original-zero lint. These are recorded in02, not erased.


## Verified blind response
